tolkāppiya urittoki
tolkāppiyam nam vāḻviyal āvaṇam; nam muntiyar kaṇṭa moḻiyiyal vaḷaṅkaḷ aṉittiyum tiraṭṭit tanta tēṉ tēṭṭu!
attēṭṭic cuvitta kōttumpikaḷ paḻiya uriyāciriyarkaḷ. avarkaḷ urikaḷiyellām oṉṟu tiraṭṭi, variciyuṟat tamiḻulakam koḷḷa vitta urittokutikaḷ iviyām!
muṉṉip patippāciriyarkaḷ teḷivuṟuttum maṇikkuvikaḷ ellāmum, avarkaḷ vitta vippuppaṭi vitta ceppēṭukaḷ it tolkāppiya urit tokiyām. mēlum, ivi kiṭittaṟku ariya karuvūlamumām!
***** ***** *****
iḷampūraṇar, cēṉāvariyar, pērāciriyar, nacciṉārkkiṉiyar,  teyvac ciliyār, kallāṭar ākiya paḻiya uriyāciriyarkaḷ, uri viḷakkam pala kālaṅkaḷil eḻutiyuḷar. eḻuttu, col, poruḷ ākiyavaṟṟiṉ patippukaḷ orē kālattil oruvarāl veḷiyiṭappaṭa villi. avaṟṟiyellām ōriṭattil orē vēḷiyil oṭṭu mottamāka muṉṉip patippāciriyarkaḷ uri - viḷakkam - kuṟippu - iṇippu ākiyavaṟṟuṭaṉ peṟa veḷiyiṭum ariya periya muyaṟciyil veḷiyiṭappaṭuvatu tamiḻmaṇ patippakattiṉ ippatippākum. tolkāppiyattiṟkuk kiṭitta urikaḷ aṉittiyum orumottamākat taruvatāl itu, tolkāppiya urittoki eṉappaṭṭatu. tokiyāvatu tokukkappaṭṭatu. 
irā. iḷaṅkumaraṉ



te. po. mīṉāṭci cuntaraṉār
paṉmoḻip pulavar te.po.mī. eṉappaṭum ivar. ceṉṉi, cintātirippēṭṭiyil 08.01.1901il poṉṉucāmi eṉpār makaṉārākat tōṉṟiyavar. ip poṉṉucāmi, perum pērāciriyarāka viḷaṅkiyavar ākiya mīṉāṭci cuntaraṉār māṇavarvaḻi māṇavar ātalāl ippeyar cūṭṭiṉār tammakaṉārkku!
`te’ eṉpatu ivartam muṉṉōr vāḻnta `teṉpaṭṭiṉam’ eṉṉum peyar kuṟippatākum. atu ceṅkaṟpaṭṭu  māvaṭṭattatu.
1922il caṭṭappaṭippu muṭittu, 1924 il varalāṟṟut tuṟiyil mutukalippaṭṭam peṟṟa ivar, ceṉṉip palkalikkaḻakattil em.ō.el. paṭṭamum peṟṟār. nāṭṭut toṇṭilum kalvit toṇṭilum īṭupaṭṭa ivar attoṇṭukaḷil nakarāṭci,  paḷḷikaḷ niṟuvutal ākiyavaṟṟil perum poṟuppukaḷi ēṟṟuc ciṟappuṟac ceytār. palappala mānāṭukaḷil paṅku koṇṭār. tamiḻpa pulamiyoṭu, āṅkilam, teluṅku, kaṉṉaṭam, maliyāḷam, vaṭamoḻi, inti, pireñcu, cerumaṉ ākiya paṉmoḻi payiṉṟavarākavum vaṇṇaṉi moḻinūl vallārākavum viḷaṅkiṉār.
paṭippārvam mikkavarāy nūlakam nūlakam eṉac ceṉṟu, tāmē nūlakamākat tikaḻum tiṟaṉuṟṟār. ivar uṭaṉ piṟantārākiya kiruṭṭiṇacāmip pāvalar nāṭakat tuṟi vallārāy viḷaṅki, iṅkilāntu ceṉṟum nāṭakam naṭattiyavarāvar.
1958 il aṇṇāmalip palkalik kaḻakat tamiḻtuṟit talivarāy viḷaṅki moḻiyiyal  tuṟiyi vaḷampaṭac ceytār. 1961 il amerikkac cikākōp palkalik kaḻakattilum pērāciriyarākap paṇi ceytār. maturiyil 1966il palkalik kaḻakam tōṉṟiyatum mutal tuṇi vēntarāka amarntu ataṉ palvēṟu tuṟi vaḷarccikku aṭittaḷa miṭṭavarāvar.
`palkalic celvar’, `paṉmoḻip pulavar’ eṉṉum virutukaḷum ṭi.liṭ. paṭṭaṅkaḷum peṟṟa ivar cilappatikāratti `makkaḷ kāviyam’ eṉa muḻaṅki, kāṉal vari mutalāyavaṟṟil ariya āyvukaḷi vaḻaṅkiṉār.
tolkāppiyak kallāṭap patippu - collatikāram ivar patipput toṇṭiṉ ciṟappuc cāṉṟākum.

irā. iḷaṅkumaraṉ

tolkāppiyam ariya patippukaḷum tēvikaḷum
pattoṉpatām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ iṭip pakutiyil  tamiḻiṉ paṇṭi ilakkaṇa/ ilakkiya nūlkaḷ acciṭap peṟṟaṉa. avvakiyil tamiḻil accaṭikkappaṭṭa mutal ilakkaṇa nūlāka iṉṟu aṟiyappaṭuvatu naṉṉūlē.    kāraṇam tamiḻaka maṭaṅkaḷ naṉṉūlip payiṟṟilakkaṇa mākavum payaṉ pāṭṭu ilakkaṇamākavum koṇṭiruntaṉa.  1835 -il tiruttaṇiki vicākap perumāḷiyar tāmē uriyeḻuti naṉṉūl mūlamum kāṇṭikiyuriyum eṉṉum talippil veḷiyiṭṭār. toṭarntu tāṇṭavarāya mutaliyārum,a. muttuccāmi piḷḷiyum iṇintu ilakkaṇap pañcakam eṉṉum talippil naṉṉūl,  nampiyakap poruḷ, puṟapporuḷ veṇpāmāli ākiyavaṟṟiṉ mūlaṅkaḷi veḷik koṇarn taṉar. itaṉ toṭarcciyāka 1838 -il vīramā muṉivariṉ toṉṉūl viḷakkam accākkam peṟṟatu. 1847 -il tāṉ maḻavi makāliṅkiyarāl tamiḻiṉ tol ilakkaṇam tolkāppiya eḻuttatikāram uriyōṭu acciṭappaṭṭatu. 
toṭarntu yāḻppāṇattu nallūr āṟumukanāvalar, yāḻppāṇattu ci.vi. tāmōtaram piḷḷi, kōmaḷapuram irācakōpālapiḷḷi, cōṭacāvatāṉam cupparāya ceṭṭiyār, pōṉṟōr 19 -ām nūṟṟāṇṭil, tolkāppiyattik kiṭitta urikaḷōṭu patippittaṉar. 1935 - il tolkāppiyat tiṟku eḻutappaṭṭa urikaḷ  (iṉṟu kiṭittuḷḷa aṉittu urikaḷum) patippikkappaṭṭu viṭṭaṉa. 1847 - 2003 vari tolkāppiyam eḻuttatikāram nacciṉārkkiṉiyar uri eṭṭup patippukaḷum, 1885-2003 vari tolkāppiyam poruḷatikāram nacciṉārkkiṉiyar muṉṉintiyal eṭṭup patippukaḷum, ceyyuḷiyal nacciṉārkkiṉiyar taṉittu mūṉṟu patippukaḷum veḷivantuḷḷaṉa. 1892 - 2003 vari tolkāppiyam collatikāram nacciṉārkkiṉiyar uri aintu patippukaḷum, 1868 - 2006 vari eḻuttu iḷampūraṇam ēḻu patippukaḷum, 1920 - 2005 vari tolkāppiyam poruḷatikāram iḷampūraṇam pattup patippukaḷum, 1927 - 2005 vari tolkāppiyam collatikāram iḷampūraṇam āṟu patippukaḷum, 1885 - 2003 vari tolkāppiyam poruḷatikāram pērāciriyam ēḻu patippukaḷum, 1929 - 2003 vari tolkāppiyam collatikāram teyvacciliyār mūṉṟu patippukaḷum, 1964 - 2003 vari tolkāppiyam collatikāram kallāṭam mūṉṟu patippukaḷum veḷivantuḷḷaṉa eṉa muṉivar pā. matukēvaraṉ tamatu tolkāppiyam patippu āvaṇam nūlil  kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār.
2003 -il tamiḻmaṇ patippakattiṉ vāyilākat tolkāppiya urikaḷ (iḷampūraṇam, nacciṉārkkiṉiyam, pērāciriyam, cēṉāvariyam, kallāṭam, teyvacciliyam) paṇṭita vitvāṉ ti.vē. kōpāliyar, muṉivar na. araṇamuṟuval ākiyōrip patippāciriyarākak koṇṭu, mutumuṉivar irā. iḷaṅkumaraṉāriṉ vāḻviyal viḷakkattuṭaṉ veḷivantuḷḷaṉa. 
iruppiṉum accil iṉṟu kiṭikkāta  ariya patippukaḷi mīḷ patippākap patippikka vēṇṭum eṉṉum nōkkattil, ci.vi. tāmōtaram piḷḷi patippu, kā. namaccivāya mutaliyār patippu, va.u. citamparam piḷḷi patippu, irā. veṅkaṭācalam piḷḷi patippu, ci. kaṇēciyar patippu,  te.po. mīṉāṭci cuntaraṉār patippu  ākiyōriṉ patippukaḷuṭaṉ tolkāppiyam toṭarpāka aṟiñar perumakkaḷ eḻutiyuḷḷa āyvuk kaṭṭurikaḷum iṇittu tamiḻmaṇ patippakattiṉ vaḻi mīḷ patippukaḷākap patippikkap paṭukiṉṟaṉa.
mīḷ patippil piṉpaṟṟiyuḷḷa potu neṟikaḷ
 mutumuṉivar irā. iḷaṅkumaraṉāriṉ tolkāppiyam kuṟitta viḷakkam, ovvōr atikāram paṟṟiya vāḻviyal viḷakkam, uriyāciriyar vivaram ākiyavi nūliṉ toṭakkat til tokuttu vaḻaṅkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. muṉṉip patippukaḷiṉ mukappup pakkaṅkaḷ koṭukkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. muṉṉip patippāciriyar paṟṟiya vivaraṅkaḷ kāla aṭippaṭiyil tokuttaḷikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
ovvōr iyaliṉ  toṭakkattil,   pērāciriyar ka. veḷḷivāraṇaṉār tolkāppiyam tokuppil eḻuti yuḷḷa tolkāppiya iyal paṟṟiya viḷakkam tarappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. muṉṉip patippil  aṭikkuṟippukaḷ anta antap pakkattil tarappaṭṭiruntaṉa. ippatippil ovvōr iyalukkum toṭar  ilakkamiṭṭu, antanta iyaliṉ iṟutiyil tokuttu  vaḻaṅkap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
	muṉṉip patippukaḷil nūṟpākkaḷik kiyāṇṭuḷḷa muṟiyiyē ip patippilum piṉpaṟṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. nūṟpāk kaḷukkut tamiḻ eṇ koṭukkappaṭṭiruppiṉ avi  naṭi muṟiyil ippōtu uḷḷa eṇkaḷāka māṟṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. nūṟpā akaramutali, mēṟkōḷ akaramutali pōṉṟavaṟṟiṟkup pakka eṇkaḷ koṭukkappaṭṭiruppiṉ avi nūṟpā eṇkaḷāka māṟṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
piṉṉiṇippākat tolkāppiyam toṭarpāka aṟiñar perumakkaḷ eḻutiyuḷḷa  kaṭṭurikaḷ iṇikkap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. nūṟpā mutaṟ kuṟippu akarāti maṭṭum koṭukkap paṭṭuḷḷa patippukaḷil avaṟṟōṭu, 2003 tamiḻmaṇ patippakattiṉ vaḻiyē paṇṭita vittuvāṉ ti.vē. kōpāliyar muṉivar na. araṇamuṟuval ākiyōrip patippāciriyarkaḷākak koṇṭu veḷivanta ilakkaṇap patippil iṇikkap paṭṭirunta, 
-	nūṟpā niral
-	mēṟkōḷ col niral
-	mēṟkōḷ coṟṟoṭar niral
-	ceyyuḷ mēṟkōḷ niral
-	kalic col niral( nūṟpāvaḻi)
-	kaliccol  niral (urivaḻi)
ākiyavi eṭuttup payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
-	pāṭavēṟupāṭukaḷ cuṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
-	muṉṉip patippil vaṭamoḻic coṟkaḷ payaṉ paṭuttappaṭṭiruppiṉ avi appaṭiyē kiyāḷap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
-	tolkāppiyap patippukaḷ kuṟitta vivaram tokukkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
ci.vi. tāmōtaram piḷḷi patippu  - 1892
collatikāram -  nacciṉārkkiṉiyar 	
1868 - il tolkāppiyam collatikāram cēṉāvari yaruriyip patippitta ci.vi. tāmōtaram piḷḷi, 1892 ām āṇṭu collatikāram nacciṉārkiṉiyar uriyip patippittār. collatikāram nacciṉārkkiṉiyar uri toṭarcciyākap patippikkappaṭṭālum, mutalil patippitta ci.vi. tāmōtaram piḷḷiyiṉ patippu iṉṟu accuvaṭivil puḻakkattil illi. appatippiṉi veḷiyiṭum nōkkattil ippatippu mīḷ patippāka veḷivarukiṟatu. ippatippil nūṟpā mutaṟkuṟippu akarāti maṭṭum koṭukkappaṭṭiruntatu. attuṭaṉ maṟṟa iṇippukaḷum iṇikkappaṭṭu, mutumuṉivar irā. iḷaṅkumaraṉ avarkaḷiṉ vāḻviyal viḷakkamum, ovvōr iyaliṉ toṭakkattil ka. veḷḷivāraṇaṉār eḻutiyuḷḷa iyal viḷakkamum koṭukkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. attuṭaṉ collatikāram toṭarpāka aṟiñar perumakkaḷ eḻutiyuḷḷa āyvuk kaṭṭurikaḷ piṉṉiṇippāka iṇikkap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. 
kā. namaccivāya mutaliyār patippu - 1927
collatikāram - iḷampūraṇam
	 iḷampūraṇam eḻuttatikāram poruḷatikāram urikaḷ muṉpē veḷivantaṉa. collatikāram   iḷampūraṇar uri 1927 - il kāvērippākkam namaccivāya mutaliyāriṉ ārāyccik kuṟippukaḷuṭaṉ ceṉṉi si. kumāracāmi nāyuṭu saṉsāl  veḷiyiṭappaṭṭatu. ituvē collatikāram iḷampūraṇar uriyiṉ mutaṟ patippākum. ip patippil aṭikkuṟippukaḷ antantap pakkattil koṭukkappaṭṭiruntaṉa. ippatippil toṭar  ilakkamiṭṭu, ovvōr iyaliṉ muṭivil  aṭik kuṟippukaḷ tarappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ip patippil   nūṟpākkaḷukkut tamiḻ eṇvariciyum naṭimuṟi eṇ variciyum koṭukkappaṭṭiruntaṉa. ippatippil naṭimuṟi eṇ muṟi maṭṭum piṉpaṟṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. 
va.u. citamparampiḷḷi patippu - 1928 , 1931, 1933, 1935
eḻuttatikāram, poruḷatikāram - iḷampūraṇam
1868 il tiricirapuram cōṭacāvatāṉam cupparāya ceṭṭiyārip patippāciriyarākak koṇṭu kaṉṉiyappa mutaliyārāl patippikkappaṭṭatu.  itaṉi aṭuttu 1928 -il tolkāppiyam iḷampūraṇam eḻuttatikāram patavuri eṉṉum peyaril va.u. citamparam piḷḷi patippu vēlāyutam piriṇṭiṅ pira, tūttukkuṭiyiliruntu veḷivantatu. appatippiṉ toṭakkattil koṭukkappaṭṭirunta  nūṟpā akarāti ippatippiṉ nūliṉ iṟutiyil koṭukkap paṭṭuḷḷatu. attuṭaṉ col niral (mēṟkōḷ), coṟṟoṭar niral (mēṟkōḷ), ceyyuḷ niral (mēṟkōḷ) kalic col niral (nūṟpā vaḻi) kalic col niral (urivaḻi) tokuttuk koṭukkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
1920 -il kā. namaccivāya mutaliyār poruḷatikāram iḷampūraṇam uri mutal pakutiyākak kaḷaviyal, kaṟpiyal, poruḷiyal mūṉṟu iyalkaḷiyum si. kumāracāmi nāyuṭu saṉ niṟuvaṉattār veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷār. maṟṟa pakutikaḷ veḷivantatāka aṟiya iyalavilli. ippatippiṟkup piṉ va.u. citamparam piḷḷi tolkāppiyam iḷampūraṇam poruḷatikāram akattiṇiyiyal, puṟattiṇiyiyal ākiya iru iyalkaḷ iṇinta patippu, ceṉṉip pirampūril iruntu veḷivantatu. ippatippil āṇṭu kuṟippiṭappaṭavilli. āṉāl 1921- il veḷivanta kuṟippiṉi, tolkāppiyam iḷampūraṇam eḻuttikāram patavuri patippil kuṟippiṭukiṟār. kaḷaviyal, kaṟpiyal, poruḷiyal 1933 - il vāviḷḷa irāmavāmi cāṭrulu aṇṭ saṉ mūlam veḷi vantuḷḷatu. 1935 - il mēṟkaṇṭa patippāḷarkaḷik koṇṭu meyppāṭṭiyal, uvamiyiyal, ceyyuḷiyal, marapiyal patippittuḷḷār. mūṉṟu pakutikaḷāka veḷivantālum  avaṟṟiṟkum toṭar eṇ koṭukkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.  1935 - il oruṅkiṇinta patippāka va.u. citamparam piḷḷi e. viyāpurippiḷḷi ākiyōr patippāciriyarkaḷāka amintu veḷivantuḷḷatu.  taṉittaṉip patippukaḷāka mūṉṟu pakutikaḷāka  veḷivantuḷḷa patippiṉi aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭu im mīḷ patippu patippikkappaṭukiṉṟatu.
irā. veṅkaṭācalam piḷḷi patippu  - 1929
collatikāram  - teyvac ciliyār
 1929 -il karantit tamiḻc caṅkattāl mutaṉ mutalil patippikkappaṭṭatu. patippāciriyar rā. vēṅkaṭācalam piḷḷi. itaṉ niḻaṟpaṭivam 1984 -il tamiḻp palkalikkaḻakam veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷatu.  ip patippil cūttira mutaṟ kuṟippu akarāti maṭṭum koṭukkappaṭṭiruntatu. attuṭaṉ col niral (mēṟkōḷ), coṟṟoṭar niral (mēṟkōḷ), ceyyuḷ niral (mēṟkōḷ) kalic col niral (nūṟpā vaḻi) kalic col niral ( urivaḻi) tokuttuk koṭukkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
ci. kaṇēciyar patippu - 1937, 1938, 1943,1948
eḻuttu nacciṉārkkiṉārkiṉiyar, collatikāram  cēṉāvariyar poruḷ -  nacciṉārkkiṉiyar, pērāciriyar
1847 - il maḻavi makāliṅkiyarāl eḻuttatikāram nacciṉārk kiṉiyam patippikkappaṭṭatu. 1891 - il ci. vi. tāmōtaram piḷḷi patippum 1923 - il ta. kaṉakacuntarattiṉ patippum kaḻakattiṉ mūlamum veḷivantaṉa. 1937 - il nā. poṉṉiyā avarkaḷāl kaṇēciyar eḻutiya uriviḷakkak kuṟippukaḷuṭaṉ eḻuttatikāram mūlamum nacciṉārkkiṉiyaruriyum veḷiyiṭappaṭṭaṉa.
1868  -il yāḻppāṇam nallūr āṟumuka nāvalar patippu, ceṉṉapaṭṭaṇam  ū. puṣparatac ceṭṭiyāratu kalāratnākara accukkūṭattil ci.vi. tāmōratam piḷḷiyāl patippikkappaṭṭatu. itē āṇṭu kōmaḷapuram irācakōpāla piḷḷiyiṉ patippum tolkāppiyam cēṉāvariyam eṉṟa peyaril veḷivantuḷḷatu. 1923 - il kantacāmiyār tiruttiya tiruttaṅkaḷōṭum kuṟippuriyōṭum kaḻakap patippāka veḷivantatu. 1938 -il ci. kaṇēciyar patippu avar eḻutiya uriviḷakkak kuṟippukaḷuṭaṉ veḷiyiṭappaṭṭatu.
1885 -il ci.vi. tāmōtaram piḷḷiyāl tolkāppiyam poruḷatikāram nacciṉārkkiṉiyar iyaṟṟiya uriyōṭum palatēcap piratirūpaṅkaḷik koṇṭu paricōtittu yāḻppāṇam ci.vi. tāmōtararāl patippikkappaṭṭatu eṉṉum kuṟippuṭaṉ patippu veḷivantatu. appatippil uḷḷa piṉṉāṉkiyal nacciṉārkkiṉiyar uri aṉṟu eṉṟu maṟuttu irā. irākaviyaṅkār 1902 - 1905 variyāṉa centamiḻ itaḻil āyvukkaṭṭuri eḻutit teḷivupaṭuttiṉār.  ataṉit toṭarntu poruḷatikāram muḻutum nāṉku pakutiyāka nacciṉārkkiṉiyam, pērāciriyam eṉap pirittu 1916, 1917 - ām āṇṭukaḷil ca. pavaṉāntam piḷḷi patippittār. 1917 - il rā. irākaviyaṅkār ceyyuḷiyalukku nacciṉārkkiyar uri iruppati aṟintu, nacciṉārkiṉiyar uri uriyāciriyar uriyuṭaṉ eṉa iru urikaḷiyum iṇittu veḷiyiṭṭār. 1934, 1935 -il e. viyāpurippiḷḷi avarkaḷāl ōlip pirati paricōtikkappaṭṭu mā.nā. cōmacuntaram piḷḷi avarkaḷatu ariya ārāyccik kuṟippukaḷuṭaṉum tiruttaṅ kaḷuṭaṉum tolkāppiyam poruḷatikāram mutaṟpākam veḷivantatu. 1948 - il tolkāppiya muṉivarāl iyaṟṟap paṭṭa tolkāppiyam poruḷatikāram (mutaṟpākam) muṉṉintiyalum nacciṉārkkiṉiyamum eṉa, ci. kaṇēciyar eḻutiya ārāyccik kuṟippuriyuṭaṉ nā. poṉṉiyā avarkaḷāl patippikkappaṭṭatu. 1943 -il tolkāppiyam poruḷatikāram (iraṇṭām pākam) eṉpatōṭu piṉṉāṉkiyalum pērāciriyamum ivi puṉṉālik kaṭṭuvaṉ tamiḻ vittuvāṉ, piramarī ci. kaṇēciyar avarkaḷ ēṭṭup piratikaḷōṭu oppunōkkit tiruttiya tiruttaṅkaḷōṭum eḻutiya uri viḷakkak kuṟippukaḷōṭum īḻakēcari atipar nā. poṉṉiyā avarkaḷāl tamatu cuṉṉākam, tirumakaḷ aḻuttakattil patippikkapaṭṭaṉa eṉa variyaṟukkap paṭṭiruntatu.
2007-il ulakat tamiḻārāycci niṟuvaṉam maṟupatippāka ci. kaṇēciyariṉ patippip patittuḷḷatu. appatippiṉip piṉpaṟṟiyē ippatippu uruvākkappaṭṭuḷḷatu.  cūttira akarāti, cūttira arumpata viḷakkam ākiyavaṟṟiṟku ippatippil pakka eṇ koṭukkappaṭṭiruntaṉa. avi im mīḷpatippil nūṟpā eṇkaḷāka māṟṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. aṉupantamāka ci. kaṇēciyyar cila kaṭṭurikaḷi appatippil iṇit tiruntār avaṟṟōṭu mēlum avaruṭiya cila kaṭṭurikaḷ ippatippil iṇikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. 
te.po. mīṉāṭci cuntaraṉār patippu  1971
collatikāram kallāṭam 
ci.vi. tāmōtaram piḷḷi tolkāppiyam poruḷati kāram nacciṉārkkiṉiyar uri patippuriyil (1885) kallāṭar uri paṟṟiya kuṟippiṉit tarukiṟār.  ṭi.eṉ appaṉiyaṅkār centamiḻ itaḻil (1920, tokuti-19, pakuti-1, pakkam-20) kallāṭaruri eṉṉum kaṭṭuriyil rīmāṉ em cēkṣakiri cātiriyār (1893) kallāṭar uri kuṟittu ārāyntuḷḷatākak kuṟippiṭukiṟār. cōḻavantāṉ aracañcaṇmukaṉār (1905) rā. irākaviyaṅkār (1917) peyar viḻiyāṉ, kā. namaccivāya mutaliyār(1920) navanītakiruṣṇa pārati(1920) pi. citampara puṉṉivaṉanāta mutaliyār(1922) kantacāmiyār (1923) va.u. cimparam piḷḷi (1928) maṉṉārkuṭi tamiḻp paṇṭitar ma.nā. cōmacuntaram piḷḷi (1929) araṅka. vēṅkaṭācalam piḷḷi (1929) ākiyōr kallāṭar uri kuṟitta kuṟippukaḷit tamatu kaṭṭurikaḷil kuṟippiṭukiṉṟaṉar.  piṉṉaṅkuṭi cā. cuppiramaṇiya cātiriyār kallāṭar uri ōriyaṇṭal kiyeḻuttup puttaka cāliyil uḷḷatu eṉṟum, avvuri evviyal vari uḷḷatu eṉṉum kuṟippuriyum tarukiṟār.
1950 - 1952  vari tarumapura ātīṉam veḷiyiṭṭa ñāṉa campantam itaḻil araciṉar kīḻtticic cuvaṭi nūlakattiṉ iraṇṭām piratiyi oṭṭip patippikkappaṭṭatu. kiḷaviyākkam toṭaṅki iṭiyiyal 10 - āvatu nūṟpā vari veḷivantatu. kallāṭar uriyit tammuṭiya tiruttaṅkaḷuṭaṉ veḷiyiṭṭavar makāvittuvāṉ taṇṭapāṇi tēcikar. 1963 -il tolkāppiyam collatikāram urikkōvi eṉṉum peyaril āpirakām aruḷappaṉum va.ai. cuppiramaṇiyamum iṇintu veḷiyiṭṭaṉar. itu nāṉku iyalukkāṉa patippāka amintiruntatu. kaḻakap patippāka 1964 -il ku. cuntaramūrtti viḷakkavuriyuṭaṉ kallāṭam veḷivantatu.
1971 -il te.po.mī avarkaḷāl oṉpatu  piratikaḷik koṇṭu oppiṭṭu araciṉar kīḻtticic cuvaṭikaḷ nūlakap patippāka veḷivantatu. 2003 -il ti.vē. kōpāliyar, na. araṇamuṟuval ākiyōrip patippāciriyarākak koṇṭu tamiḻ maṇ patippakam kallāṭaṉār uriyip patippit tuḷḷatu. 2007 -il ti.vē. kōpāliyarip  patippāciriyarākak koṇṭu, caracuvati makāl nūlakam tolkāppiyam collatikāram urikkottil kallāṭar uriyi veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷatu. kallāṭar uri muḻumiyāka ituvari kiṭittilatu.
immīḷpatippil centamiḻ antaṇar avarkaḷiṉ ilakkaṇa vāḻviyal viḷakkamum kā.  veḷḷivāraṇaṉāriṉ iyal viḷakkaṅkaḷum koṭukkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ivi tolkāppiyattiṟkuḷ pukuvārukku eḷitāṉa maṉaniliyi uruvākkuvatuṭaṉ, āyvāḷarkaḷukku mikunta payaṉ uṭiyatāka irukkum. piṉṉiṇippāka iṇikkappaṭṭuḷḷa kaṭṭurikaḷ, tolkāppiya uriyāciriyar, urikaḷ kuṟitta nuṭpattip pulappaṭuttuvatuṭaṉ, tolkāppiyam paṟṟiya nuṇmiyi aṟiyavum tuṇi ceyyum. nūṟpā, uri vaḻik kaliccoṟkaḷ, uriyil uḷḷa mēṟkōḷ akarāti pōṉṟavi tokuttut tarappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. tolkāppiyatti aṟiya virumpuvārukkum, āyvāḷarkaḷukkum  it tokuppu  payaṉ koḷḷum vakiyil uruvākkappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
tolkāppiyam mūlamum, uriyōṭum pala patippukaḷik kaṇṭuḷḷatu.  iruppiṉum toṭakkattil tamiḻ aṟiñar perumakkaḷāl uriyōṭu tolkāppiyattip patippitta patippukaḷ iṉṟu kiṭikkavilli. anniliyip pōkkum poruṭṭu, tamiḻariṉ paṇpāṭṭi, mēṉmiyi, uyarvip potittu vittuḷḷa nūlkaḷit tēṭit tēṭip patippikkum  tamiḻmaṇ patippaka urimiyāḷar aiyā kō. iḷavaḻakaṉār avarkaḷ paḻam patippukaḷip patippikka vēṇṭum eṉṟu virumpiyataṉ kāraṇamāka ippatippup paṇi mēṟkoḷḷappaṭṭatu. ippatippiṟku, tamiḻiṉ mūtta aṟiñar, centamiḻ antaṇar mutumuṉivar aiyā irā. iḷaṅkumaraṉār avarkaḷ patippāciriyarāka amintu eṉṉiyum ippaṇiyil iṇittukkoṇṭārkaḷ. 
 paḻantamiḻ nūlkaḷip pātukāttu vittuḷḷa pulavar pallaṭam māṇikkam avarkaḷiṭam iruntu te.po. mīṉāṭci cuntaraṉār tolkāppiyam collatikāram kallāṭar uri peṟappaṭṭup patippiṟkup payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭatu. maṟṟa urikaḷ aṉittum  tamiḻ iṇiyap palkalikkaḻaka nūlakam, ulakat tamiḻārāycci iṇiyap pakkattil iruntu pativiṟakkam ceyyappaṭṭu im mīḷpatippiṟkup payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.  ci. kaṇēciyar patippiṉi ulakat tamiḻārāycci niṟuvaṉattiṉ vaḻi veḷivanta ci. kaṇēciyyariṉ tolkāppiyap patippiṟku ulakat tamiḻarāycci niṟuvaṉattiṉ mēṉāḷ iyakkunar (muḻukūṭutal poṟuppu) muṉivar tiru ma. irācēntiraṉ avarkaḷ kaṇēciyar patippiṟku eḻutiyuḷḷa mukavuri avaruṭiya opputaluṭaṉ ippatippil cērkkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. 
muṉivar pā. matukēvaraṉ avarkaḷiṉ tolkāppiyam patippu āvaṇam 7 eṉṉum nūṟpakutiyil iruntu tol kāppiyap patippu aṭivukaḷ ippatippil iṇikkap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. tolkāppiyam toṭarpāṉa palvēṟu aṟiñar  perumakkaḷiṉ āyvuk kaṭṭurikaḷ tokuttu vaḻaṅkap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa.  tolkāppiyam urikaḷ muḻutum āyvu ceytuḷḷa pērāciriyar muṉivar ca. kurucāmi avarkaḷiṉ kaṭṭurikaḷ ippatippil iṇikkap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa.  patippiṟkut tuṇi ceyta mēṟkaṇṭa aṉivarukkum naṉṟiyiṉip pulappaṭuttik koḷḷukiṟēṉ. muṉivar srī. piracāntaṉ avarkaḷ tokutta   ilakkaṇa varampu nūlilirunta kaṭṭurikaḷ kaṇēciyar patippil iṇikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. centamiḻ, tamiḻppoḻil, tamiḻiyal mutaliya āyvitaḻkaḷiliruntu tolkāppiyam toṭarpāṉa kaṭṭurikaḷ cērkkap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. 
patippup paṇiyil īṭupaṭuttiya aiyā tiru kō. iḷavaḻakaṉār avarkaḷukkum patippil iṇintu ceyalpaṭap paṇitta aiyā mutumuṉivar irā. iḷaṅkumaraṉār avarkaḷukkum, patippiṟkut tuṇipurinta pulavar centali kutamaṉ aiyā avarkaḷukkum eṉ neñcam niṟinta naṉṟi. taṭṭaccuc ceytu koṭutta tirumati pa. kayalviḻi  tirumati kō. cittirā. aṭṭi vaṭivamitta celvaṉ pā.  ari (hariṣ),  maṟṟum patippakap paṇiyāḷarkaḷ tiru irā. paramēcuvaraṉ, tiru taṉacēkaraṉ, tiru ku. marutu,  tiru vi. matimāṟaṉ, ippatippiṟku utaviya eṉṉuṭiya muṉivar paṭṭa māṇavarkaḷ pā. mālati, kā. pāpu, cu. kōvintarācu, kā. kayalviḻi ākiya aṉivarukkum naṉṟiyiṉit terivittuk koḷkiṟēṉ. ip  patippup paṇiyil eṉṉi muḻuvatāka īṭupaṭat tuṇiyāka niṟkum eṉ kaṇavar maruttuvar mu. cēkkiḻār avarkaḷukku eṉ naṉṟiyiṉi urittākkukiṟēṉ.
kalpaṉā cēkkiḻār

nuḻivuri
tamiḻmaṇ patippakam itukāṟum veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷa aṟiñar perumakkaḷiṉ urikaḷ yāvum paḻiya ilakkiya ilakkaṇak karuvūlattiṉ vāyilkaḷit tiṟakkiṉṟa tiṟavukōlkaḷ; tamiḻari ēṟṟiviṭum ēṇippaṭikaḷ; varalāṟṟup pātiyik kaṭakka utavum ūrtikaḷ.
tamiḻaṟiñarkaḷiṉ aṟivuccelvaṅkaḷi muḻu muḻu nūl tokuppukaḷāka veḷiyiṭṭut tamiḻnūl patippil perum paṅkaḷippi em patippakam ceytu varuvati ulakat tamiḻarkaḷ aṉivarum aṟivar.
tamiḻakam vēṟṟiṉattavar paṭiyeṭuppāl tākkuṇṭu atirntu nilikulintu tamiḻmakkaḷ tam marapukaḷi maṟantapōtellām paḻamperum ilakkiyac celvaṅkaḷi eṭuttukkāṭṭi viḷakkit tamiḻ marapi vāḻac ceyta perumi uriyāciriyarkaḷukku uṇṭu. 
 tamiḻmoḻiyiṉ nilitta vāḻviṟkum veṟṟikkum kāraṇamāka iruppavarkaḷ uriyāciriyarkaḷē āvar. ilakkaṇak koḷkikaḷi viḷakki moḻikku varampu kaṭṭi ilakkiyak karuttukaḷi viḷakki kālantōṟum paṇpāṭṭi vaḷarttu, tamiḻ iṉattiṟkut toṇṭu ceyta perumi uriyāciriyarkaḷiyē cārum.
ovvoru uriyāciriyarum tamiḻiṉam uṟaṅkik koṇṭiruntapōtu viḻitteḻuntu, eḻuccikkural koṭutta muṟpōkkuc cintaṉiyāḷarkaḷ. avarkaḷ eḻutiyuḷḷa urikaḷ yāvum kālattiṉ kuralkaḷ; camutāyattiṉ etirpārppukaḷ; tamiḻ iṉa vaḷarccikku iṉṟiyamiyāta tēvikaḷ; nerukkaṭiyiṉ veḷippāṭukaḷ ākum.
	 - uriyāciriyarkaḷ, (mu.vi.  aravintaṉ).
tolkāppiyam ilakkaṇanūl maṭṭumaṉṟu; tamiḻariṉ aṟivumarapiṉ aṭiyāḷam.  tamiḻariṉ vāḻviyali, meyyiyalip pātukātta kālappēḻi. itil potintuḷḷa tarukkaviyal kūṟukaḷ intiyat tarukkaviyal varalāṟṟiṉ mūla vaṭivaṅkaḷ -  aṟiñarkaḷiṉ pārviyil pēraṟiñar aṇṇā, (muṉivar ka. neṭuñceḻiyaṉ)
mēṟkaṇṭa aṟiñarkaḷiṉ kūṟṟu, tolkāppiyattiṉ iṉṟiyami yāmiyiyum, uriyāciriyarkaḷiṉ karuttuc ceṟiviyum uḻippiyum uṇartta vallavi. ci.vi. tāmōtaram piḷḷi, kā. namaccivāya mutaliyār, va.u. citamparaṉār,  ci. kaṇēciyar, irā. vēṅkaṭācalam piḷḷi, te.po. mīṉāṭcicuntaraṉār pōṉṟa peruntamiḻ aṟiñarkaḷiṉ uḻippāl uruvāṉa iv vāḻviyal karuvūlam tolkāppiya  urittoki eṉum peyaril  tamiḻmaṇ patippakam mīḷ patippu ceytuḷḷatu. 
2003 -ām āṇṭil em patippakam tolkāppiyatti (eḻuttu - col - poruḷ)  muḻumiyāka veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷatu. itukāṟum veḷivantuḷḷa tolkāppiya nūl patippukaḷil  iṭam peṟāta ariya patippuc ceytikaḷ ivvurit tokiyil cērkkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
ivvurit toki ellā niliyilum ciṟappāka veḷivaruvataṟku  tōṉṟāt tuṇiyāka iruntavarkaḷip paṟṟi taṉippakkattil pativu ceytuḷḷōm.  
ivvāḻviyalnūl piḻiyaṟṟa cempatippāka veḷivaruvataṟku mutaṉmip patippāciriyar mutumuṉivar aiyā  irā. iḷaṅkumaraṉār, iṇi patippāciriyar arumi makaḷ muṉivar kalpaṉā cēkkiḻār ākiyōriṉ uḻippum paṅkaḷippum eṉṟum maṟakka muṭiyātavi. em tamiḻ nūl patippiṟku ellā niliyilum toṭarntu ākkamum ūkkamum aḷittu varum iṉiya naṇpar pulavar centali na. kavutamaṉ avarkaḷiṉ taṉṉalam karutā tamiḻt toṇṭiṟku eṉṟum naṉṟi uṭiyēṉ.   
tamiḻarkaḷ tam illamtōṟum pātukāttu vikka vēṇṭiya ivvaruntamiḻp putiyalit tamiḻ kūṟum ulakiṟku vaḻaṅkuvatip perumiyākak karuti makiḻkiṟōm. ippatippit tamiḻkkūṟum nallulakam ēṟṟippōṟṟum eṉṟu nampukiṟōm.
kō.  iḷavaḻakaṉ

collatikāram - kallāṭaṉār
te.po. mīṉāṭci cuntaraṉār - (1971)

tamiḻnāṭṭu aracu kīḻtticic cuvaṭikaḷ nūlakattiṉ vaḻiyāka innūl 1971 il veḷivantuḷḷatu. itaṉi mūlamākak koṇṭu ippatippi veḷiyiṭukiṉṟōm.



peyariyal
itukāṟum alvaḻi vēṟṟumiyākiya toṭarmoḻi yilakkaṇaṅ kūṟiya āciriyar, iṉi attoṭarmoḻikku uṟup pākiya taṉimoḻi yilakkaṇaṅ kūṟat toṭaṅki mutaṟkaṇ peyarilakkaṇa muṇarttukiṉṟār. ataṉāl itu peyariya leṉṉum peyarttāyiṟṟu. itaṉkaṇ 43-cūttiraṅkaḷ uḷḷaṉa. ivaṟṟi 41-cūttiraṅkaḷāka aṭakkuvar teyvacciliyār.
ellāc coṟkaḷum poruḷ kuṟittu varuvaṉavē. collāṟ kuṟikkappaṭṭa poruḷit terintukoḷḷutaṟkum collit terintukoḷḷutaṟkum accollē karuviyākum. col poruḷuṇarttum muṟi veḷippaṭunili, kuṟippunili yeṉa iruvakippaṭum. colleṉac ciṟappitturikkattakkaṉa peyarum viṉiyum eṉa iraṇṭēyām. iṭiccollum uriccollum peyar viṉikaḷic cārntu tōṉṟuvaṉa eṉpar āciriyar.
peyar eṉpatu poruḷ. poruḷi yuṇarttuñ col peyarccoleṉappaṭṭatu. poruḷatu puṭipeyarcciyākiya toḻiṟ paṇpiṉ kāriyam viṉiyākum. avviṉiyi yuṇarttuñcol viṉiccoleṉappaṭṭatu. peyarumākātu viṉiyumākātu avviraṇṭaṟkum naṭu nikaraṉavāy niṟpaṉa iṭiccoṟkaḷām. iṭi-naṭu. kuṇappaṇpum toḻiṟpaṇpu mākiya poruṭpaṇpi yuṇarttuñ coṟkaḷ uriccoṟkaḷām. poruṭkup paṇpu urimipūṇṭu niṟṟaliṉ appaṇpiṉi yuṇarttuñcol uriccoleṉappaṭṭateṉpar civañāṉa muṉivar.
uyartiṇip peyarum, aḵṟiṇip peyarum, avviru tiṇikkum otta urimiyuṭiya viravuppeyarum eṉap peyarc col mūṉṟu vakippaṭum. avaṉ, peṇmakaṉ, cāttaṉ eṉa ṉakaravīṟum, avaḷ, makkaḷ, makaḷ eṉa ḷakaravīṟum nampi, peṇṭāṭṭi, muḷḷi eṉa ikaravīṟum, āṭūu, makaṭūu, aḻitūu eṉa ukaravīṟum uyartiṇi āṇpāl peṇpāl kaḷukkum aḵṟiṇikkum uriyavāy vantaṉa. ippeyarc coṟkaḷi viṉic coṟpōla iṉṉīṟu iṉṉa pālukkurittu eṉa īṟu paṟṟip pakuttuṇarttutalākāmiyiṉ irutiṇip pirinta aimpāluṇarttuñ collātaṟkup peyaruḷ uriyaṉa uriyavām eṉṟār tolkāppiyaṉār.
ivviyalil 8-mutal 12-variyuḷḷa cūttiraṅkaḷāl uyar tiṇip peyarkaḷiyum, 13-mutal 14-variyuḷḷa cūttiraṅkaḷāl aḵṟiṇip peyarkaḷiyum, 17-mutal 19-variyuḷḷa cūttiraṅkaḷāl viravuppeyar pāl viḷaṅka niṟṟaliyum, 20-mutal 36-variyuḷḷa cūttiraṅkaḷāl iru tiṇip potuppeyarkaḷiyum avaṟṟiṉ vakiyiṉiyum virittuk kūṟuvar āciriyar.
aruvāḷaṉ, cōḻiyaṉ eṉṟāṅku nilampaṟṟi vaḻaṅkum peyar nilappeyar. cēraṉ, cōḻaṉ, pāṇṭiyaṉ eṉṟāṟpōla oruvaṉ piṟanta kuṭi paṟṟi vaḻaṅkuvaṉa kuṭippeyar. aviyattār, attikōcattār eṉṟāṅku oru tuṟikkaṇ urimi pūṇṭa palariyuṅ kuṟittu vaḻaṅkuvaṉa kuḻaviṉpeyar. taccaṉ, kollaṉ eṉṟāṟpōlat toḻilpaṟṟi vaḻaṅkum peyar viṉippeyar. amparkiḻāṉ, pērūrkiḻāṉ eṉṟāṟpōla uṭimip poruḷpaṟṟi ataṉiyuṭiyārkku vaḻaṅkum peyar uṭippeyareṉappaṭum. kariyaṉ ceyyaṉ, nallaṉ tīyaṉ eṉṟāṟpōla niṟa mutaliya kuṇam paṟṟi appaṇpuṭiyārkku vaḻaṅkum peyar paṇpukoḷ peyarākum. tantiyar, tāyar, taṉṉiyar eṉa ammuṟiyuṭiyār palarkkum vaḻaṅkum peyar pallōrk kuṟitta muṟinilip peyarām. peruṅkālar, peruntōḷar eṉṟāṟpōlac ciṉiyuṭimipaṟṟi acciṉi yuṭiyār palarkkum vaḻaṅkum peyar pallōrkkuṟitta ciṉi nilip peyarām. āyar, vēṭṭuvar mutalākat tiṇipaṟṟip palarkkum vaḻaṅkum peyar pallōrk kuṟitta tiṇi nilip peyarām. paṭṭi puttirar kaṅki māttirar eṉṟāṟpōla viḷiyāṭṭuk kuṟittu iḷiñarkaḷ pakutipaṭak kūṭit tamakkup paṭittiṭṭukkoḷḷum peyar kūṭivaru vaḻakkiṉ āṭiyaṟ peyareṉappaṭum. oruvar, iruvar, mūvar eṉa eṇṇākiya iyalpupaṟṟi ivvaḷaviṉar eṉṉum poruḷil vaḻaṅkum peyar iṉṟivareṉṉum eṇṇiyaṟ peyarām. iṅkeṭuttuk kāṭṭiya peyar vikaṟpamellām tam kālattē tamiḻakattil vaḻaṅkappeṟṟaṉavātaliṉ ivaṟṟip pāl viḷaṅka vanta uyartiṇip peyarkaḷil aṭakkik kūṟiṉār āciriyar.
ā, yāṉi, teṅku, palā eṉṟāṟpōṉṟu orumikkum paṉmikkum potuvāy vaḻaṅkum aḵṟiṇip peyarkaḷi ‘aḵṟiṇiyiyaṟpeyar’ eṉak kuṟiyiṭṭu vaḻaṅkuvar tol kāppiyar. ivaṟṟi naṉṉūlārum piṟarum pālpakā aḵṟiṇip peyar eṉa vaḻaṅkuvar. oruvarkkuk kāraṇaṅ karutātu cāttaṉ, koṟṟaṉ eṉṟāṅku iyalpāka iṭṭu vaḻaṅkum peyar iyaṟpeyareṉappaṭum. peruṅkālaṉ, muṭavaṉ eṉṟāṟpōṉṟu ciṉiyuṭimipaṟṟi mutaṟporuḷukku vaḻaṅkum peyar ciṉip peyarām. cīttalic cāttaṉ, koṭum puṟamaruti eṉṟāṟpōṉṟu ciṉippeyaroṭu toṭarntuvarum mutaṟpeyar ciṉimutaṟ peyarām. piṟappāl oruvarōṭoruvarkkuḷatākiya muṟi paṟṟit tanti, tāy mutalāka vaḻaṅkum peyarkaḷ muṟip peyarkaḷām. iyaṟpeyar, ciṉippeyar, ciṉimutaṟpeyar, muṟippeyar ākiya iviyum tām, tāṉ, ellām, nīyir, nī eṉpaṉavum irutiṇikkum uriyaṉavāy vaḻaṅkum potup peyarkaḷākum. ivaṟṟi viravuppeyar eṉa vaḻaṅkutalum uṇṭu. mēṟkūṟiya iyaṟpeyar, ciṉippeyar, ciṉimutaṟ peyar ākiya mūṉṟum āṇmi peṇmi orumi paṉmi eṉa innāṉkuṅ kāraṇamāka irutiṇikkum potuvāki vaḻaṅkuṅ kāl munnāṉku paṉṉiruṇṭuvakippaṭumeṉṟum, muṟippeyar āṇmi muṟippeyar peṇmi muṟippeyar eṉa iru vakippaṭumeṉṟum, ivi peṇmi cuṭṭiya peyar, āṇmi cuṭṭiya peyar, orumi cuṭṭiya peyar, paṉmi cuṭṭiya peyar eṉa nāṉkāyaṭaṅkumeṉṟum, ivaṟṟuḷ peṇmi cuṭṭiya peyar uyartiṇiyil peṇṇoruttiyiyum aḵṟiṇiyil peṇṇoṉṟiyum uṇarttutalum, āṇmi cuṭṭiya peyar uyartiṇiyuḷ oruvaṉiyum aḵṟiṇiyil āṇ oṉṟiyum uṇarttutalum orumi cuṭṭiya peyar uyartiṇiyuḷ oruvaṉ orutti eṉṉum irupālum aḵṟiṇiyil oṉṟaṉpālu mākiya mūṉṟu pālkaḷi yuṇarttutalum, paṉmi cuṭṭiya peyar irutiṇip paṉmiyum uṇartti varutalōṭu avaṟṟuḷ oru cāraṉa aḵṟiṇiyorumi aḵṟiṇip paṉmi uyar tiṇiyil āṇmi peṇmi ākiya innāṉku pālkaḷi yuṇarttutalum uṭiyaṉa eṉṟum viritturippar tolkāppiyar.
paṉmi cuṭṭiya peyar eṉpataṟku irutiṇiyilum paṉmip pālic cuṭṭivarum potup peyareṉpatē poruḷ. ituvē āciriyar karutteṉpatu tāmeṉ kiḷavi paṉmikkurittē’ (tol-col. 181) ‘ēṉikkiḷavi paṉmikkurittē’ (tol-col. 187) eṉavaruñ cūttiraṅkaḷāl uyartiṇip palarpāṟkum aḵṟiṇip palaviṉ pāṟkum potuvākiya niliyiṉip paṉmiyeṉṟa collāl avarkūṟutalāl naṉku pulaṉām. paṉmi cuṭṭiya peyarkaḷ tamakkuriya irutiṇip paṉmiyiyuñ cuṭṭi vaḻaṅkutalē muṟiyākavum avaṟṟuḷ orucāraṉa aḵṟiṇiyil oṉṟaṉpāl palaviṉpāl uyartiṇiyil āṇorumi peṇṇorumi ākiya innāṉku pālkaḷiyum kuṟittu varutaluṇṭeṉpār,
‘paṉmi cuṭṭiya ellāp peyarum
  oṉṟē palavē oruva reṉṉum
  eṉṟip pāṟkum ōraṉ ṉavvē’
eṉac cūttirañ ceytār tolkāppiyaṉār. itaṉkaṇ eṉṟippāṟkum eṉṟa ummiyāl paṉmi cuṭṭiya peyar taṉakkuriya irutiṇip paṉmiyiyum ēṟṟuvarutali āciriyar taḻīik kūṟiṉārātalvēṇṭum. ivvuṇmi ‘taṉpālēṟṟali ummiyāl taḻīiyiṉār’ eṉavarum civañāṉamuṉivar urik kuṟippiṉāl iṉitu pulaṉātal kāṇka. iṅkē “paṉmi cuṭṭiya peyareṉpatu veṇkuṭip peruviṟal eṉpatupōla orumiyiyipu nīkkātu iyipiṉmi māttiri nīkkip paṉmi cuṭṭum eṉpatupaṭa niṉṟatu” eṉpar cēṉāvariyar. iccūttirattiṟ kūṟiyavāṟu paṉmi cuṭṭiya peyar uyartiṇip paṉmiyiyuṇarttā toḻitalum ēṉiyorumikaḷi yuṇarttutalum poruntā veṉpatu karuti ittolkāppiyac cūttirak karutti maṟuttal eṉṉum matampaṭa ‘avaṟṟuḷ’ oṉṟēyiru, tiṇit taṉpālēṟkum’ eṉa naṉṉūlār cūttirañ ceytāreṉpar caṅkara namaccivāyar, paṉmi cuṭṭiya peyareṉṟatu uyartiṇi āṇorumi peṇṇorumi aḵṟiṇiyorumi paṉmi ākiya pala pālkaḷiyum cuṭṭi niṟṟaliṉ iṉamuḷḷa aṭimoḻiyē yeṉpatum evvakiyāl nōkkiṉum āciriyar tol kāppiyaṉār karuttu maṟukkattakkataṉṟeṉpatum civañāṉa muṉivar karuttākum.
oruvareṉṉum peyarccol oruvaṉ orutti yeṉṉum irupāṟkum potuvāy vaḻaṅkum. accol poruḷāl orumiyik kuṟippatāyiṉum palarpāṟ collōṭu toṭarum iyalpuṭiyatākum. peṇ makaṉ eṉṉum peyar peṇpāl viṉikoṇṭu muṭiyum. peyarkaḷiṉ īṟṟayaliluḷḷa vikuti ākāram ceyyuḷuḷ ōkāramākat tiriyum. ceyyuḷilē karup poruḷkaḷiṉmēl vaḻaṅkum irutiṇip potup peyarkaḷuḷ avvannilattu makkaḷāl aḵṟiṇip poruḷukkē yurimi yuṭiyaṉavāy vaḻaṅkum peyarkaḷ uyartiṇiyi yuṇarttā veṉpar āciriyar.
- ka. veḷḷivāraṇaṉār 
nūlvarici 10, pak. 204-208


1aintāvatu
peyariyal
152.	ellāc collum poruḷkuṟit taṉavē.
2eṉpatu cūttiram.
ivōttu eṉṉa peyarttō eṉiṉ, peyar ilakkaṇam uṇarttiṉamiyiṉ peyariyal eṉṉum peyarttu.
mēl ōttiṉōṭu itaṟku iyi3pu eṉṉō 4eṉiṉ, mēṟpeyar ilakkaṇam uṇartti, avilakkaṇam uṭiya aviyaṟpeyar ivi eṉṟu avaṟṟatu pakuti uṇarttiya eṭuttukkoṇṭāṉ eṉpatu.
5immutaṟ cūttiram eṉnutaliṟṟō eṉiṉ, nāṉku vakippaṭṭa colliṟkum potuvāyatōr ilakkaṇam 6uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
7itaṉ poruḷ:- tamiḻccol ellām oru poruḷ uṇarttutalik karutiyē naṭakkum ; poruḷ uṇarttutalik karutātu naṭappaṉa illi eṉṟavāṟu.
(u-m.) 
cāttaṉ, koṟṟaṉ eṉa av ap8peyar 9av ammakkaḷi uṇarttiṉa. 
uṇṭāṉ, tiṉṟāṉ 10eṉpaṉa avav viṉic coṟkaḷ avav viṉiyi uṇarttiṉa. 
atumaṉ uṟukāl eṉavum, ceṉmati tavac11cēy nāṭṭār 12eṉavum ivi aviṭiyum uriyum avap peyarviṉikaṉi 13aṭuttu oruporuḷi uṇarttiṉa.
maṟṟu, iḵtu, āyirutiṇiyiṉicikku maṉcol eṉpuḻi aṭaṅkiṟṟup piṟa eṉiṉ ; āṇṭu ellāc collum orutali yākap poruḷ uṇarttum eṉṉum tuṇipu viti iṉmiyiṉ īṇṭuk kūṟiṉāṉ eṉpatu.
maṟṟu acinili iṭiccoṟkaḷ poruḷ 14uṇarttā vāleṉiṉ, aviyum oruvāṟṟāṉ ciṟupāṉmi poruḷ uṇarttum eṉa uṇarka. allatūum itu perumpāṉmi 15eṉiṉum amiyum.
muyaṟkōṭu 16eṉṉum toṭakkattaṉa poruḷ uṇart tāvāleṉiṉ, ivi 17naṉmakkaḷ vaḻa..........18ṉṟu poruṇmi tirintu muyaṟkōṭu illi eṉap piṉvaruñ collōṭu paṭuttu 19nōkka vanta iṉmi viḷakka vantatām. 
iṉi iṟiñi, miṟiñi eṉṉum toṭakkattaṉaporuḷ 20viḷakkāvē eṉiṉ avi 21naṉmakka ........ eṉpatu.	(1)

153.	poruṇmi teritalum coṉmi teritalum
	colli ṉākum eṉmaṉār pulavar.
eṉpatu eṉnutaliṟṟō eṉiṉ, ituvum accoṟkaḷ poruḷ uṇarttumvaḻik kiṭanta tōr ilakkaṇam uṇarttal nutaliṟṟu.
itaṉ 1poruḷ:- poruḷkaḷ 2teriyaniṟṟalum, ap3poruḷi aṟiyappaṭātu coṟkaḷ teriya niṟṟalum eṉṉum ivi iraṇṭum, coṟkaḷ ētuvāka uḷavākum eṉṟu colluvar pulavar eṉṟavāṟu.
poruṇmi teritaṟku utāraṇam mēṟkāṭṭiṉavē. 
coṉmi teritaṟku 4utāraṇam:- peyarccol, viṉic col, iṭiccol, uriccol, peyareccam, viṉiyeccam, muṟṟuccol eṉa ivi av accoṟkaḷiyē poruḷāka uṇarttiṉa eṉak koḷka.
maṟṟituvum poruṇmi teritalē ām piṟa, eṉiṉ tām 5poruṇmi ........ .................  ḻic col eṉa vēṟupaṭṭu niṟkum ; ātaliṉ itaṉi vēṟu 6kūṟiṉāṉ eṉpatu.
maṟṟoru col taṉṉiṉ vēṟāyatōr collip poruṇmiyāka uṇarttātu, vēṟeṉ kiḷavi eṉṟāṟpōla accol taṉṉiyē uṇaraniṟṟalum 7uṇṭām eṉṉa, atuvum coṉmi terital eṉa aṭaṅkiṟṟup pōlum.
maṟṟum, mēl eytiya poruṇmiterital īṇṭuk kūṟal vēṇṭā eṉiṉ, muṟkūṟiyatiṟ piṟkūṟiyatu valiuṭittākaliṉ aḵtu vilakkuṇṇum eṉa ummi koṭuttāyiṉum 6ōtaṟpāla ataṉi naṉku uṇartaṟ poruṭṭu uṭaṉ ōtiṉāṉ eṉa uṇarka.		(2)

154.	teripuvēṟu niliyalum kuṟippiṟ ṟōṉṟalum
	irupāṟ ṟeṉpa poruṇmi niliyē.

164.	atuitu utueṉa varūum peyarum
	avi mutalākiya āytap peyarum
	aviivi uvieṉa varūum peyarum
	avi mutalākiya akarap peyarum
	yātu yā yāvi eṉṉum peyarum
	āvayiṉ mūṉṟō ṭappati ṉintum
	pālaṟi vanta aḵṟiṇip peyarē.
eṉpatu eṉnutaliṟṟō eṉiṉ, niṟutta muṟiyāṉē aḵṟiṇip peyar āmāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
itaṉ poruḷ:- atu, itu, utu, eṉṟu 1colla varu kiṉṟa cuṭṭu mutaṟpeyarkaḷum, cuṭṭeḻuttiṉi mutalāka uṭiya aḵtu, iḵtu, uḵtu 2eṉṉum āytat toṭar moḻik kuṟṟiyalukarap peyarkaḷum, avi, ivi, uvi eṉṟu colla 3varukiṉṟa cuṭṭumutaṟpeyarkaḷum, ac cuṭṭeḻuttiṉi mutalākuṭiya av, uv, iv eṉṉum 4vakarīṟṟuppeyarkaḷum, yātu, yā, yāvi eṉṟu colla varukiṉṟa avviṉāp 5poruḷiṭattu varum mūṉṟu peyarum ākavarūum patiṉintu 6peyarkaḷum orumip paṉmip pāl aṟiya 7vanta aḵṟiṇip peyarām eṉṟavāṟu. 165.	palla palacila eṉṉum peyarum
	uḷḷa illa eṉṉum peyarum
	viṉippeyark kiḷaviyum paṇpukoḷ peyarum
	iṉitteṉak kiḷakkum eṇṇuk kuṟip peyarum

	appāl oṉpatum avaṟṟō raṉṉa.

177.	1peṇmi cuṭṭiya ellāp peyarum
	oṉṟaṟkum oruttikkum oṉṟiya niliyē.
eṉpatu eṉnutaliṟṟō eṉiṉ, 2mēl virittavaṟṟuḷ peṇmip peyar ellāvaṟṟiyum tokuttut tiṇikku uriyavāṟu 3uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
itaṉ poruḷ:- mēl virittu ōtiṉavaṟṟuḷ peṇmiyik karutiṉa ellāppeyarum aḵṟiṇiyuḷ peṇ oṉṟaṟkum, uyartiṇiyuḷ oruttikkum niṟṟal poruntiṉa eṉṟavāṟu.
peṇmi cuṭṭiyapeyar, peṇmi iyaṟpeyar eṉavum, peṇmic ciṉip4peyar eṉavum, peṇmic ciṉi mutaṟpeyar eṉavum, peṇmi muṟippeyar eṉavum nāṉku vakippaṭum.
(u-m.) 
peṇmi iyaṟpeyar:- cātti vantatu, cātti vantāḷ 5eṉavum.
peṇmic ciṉippeyar - muṭatti vantatu, muṭatti vantāḷ 6eṉavum. kuṟaḷi eṉpatum appāṟpaṭum.
peṇmic ciṉimutaṟpeyar - muṭakkoṟṟi vantatu, muṭakkoṟṟi vantāḷ eṉavum.
peṇmi muṟippeyar - tāy vantatu, tāy vantāḷ eṉavum varum.
āy eṉpatum atu. yāy eṉpatō eṉiṉ, taṉmiyōṭu aṭuttamiyiṉ muṟip peyarēṉum uyartiṇi eṉappaṭum.(26)
aṭikkuṟippukaḷ
177-1 	i-ii-ie-eii “peṇmikaḷ”
2	i-ii-ie-eii “virittavaṟṟuṭ”
3 	i “uṇarttiṟṟu”
4 	i “pēr”
5 	ei-eiii “eṉavarum”
6 	ii-eii “eṉavum varum” (coṟṟoṭari muṭikka muyalum tiruttam)
178.	āṇmi cuṭṭiya ellāp peyarum
	oṉṟaṟkum oruvaṟkum oṉṟiya niliyē.
eṉpatu eṉnutaliṟṟō eṉiṉ, āṇmip peyar ellāvaṟṟiyum tokuttut tiṇikku uriya āmāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
itaṉ poruḷ:- āṇmiyik karutiṉa peyarkaḷ ellām aḵṟiṇiyuḷ oṉṟaṟkum uyartiṇiyuḷ oruvaṟkum niṟṟalporuntiṉa eṉṟavāṟu.
1avi āṇmi iyaṟpeyar eṉavum, āṇmic ciṉippeyar eṉavum, āṇmic ciṉimutaṟpeyar eṉavum, āṇmi muṟippeyar eṉavum nāṉku vakippaṭum.
āṇmi iyaṟpeyar :- cāttaṉ vantatu, cāttaṉ vantāṉ eṉavarum.
āṇmic ciṉippeyar - muṭavaṉ vantatu, muṭavaṉ vantāṉ 2eṉa varum.
āṇmic ciṉimutaṟpeyar - muṭakkoṟṟaṉ vantatu, muṭakkoṟṟaṉ vantāṉ eṉavarum.
āṇmi muṟippeyar - tanti vantatu, tanti vantāṉ eṉa varum. 
nunti eṉpatum atu. enti eṉpatu taṉmi aṭuttamiyiṉ uyartiṇip peyar ām.  	(27)
aṭikkuṟippukaḷ
178-1 	i “āvi” (ā-a māṟāṭṭam)
2 	ii-eii “e-m” (ivvāṟu curukkiyataṉic cilar ‘eṉavarum’ eṉṟum, ‘eṉṟum’ eṉavum eṉṟum virippar pōlum)
179.	paṉmi cuṭṭiya ellāp peyarum
	oṉṟē palavē oruvar eṉṉum
	eṉṟip pāṟkum ōraṉ ṉavvē.
eṉpatu eṉnutaliṟṟō eṉiṉ, paṉmip peyar ellāvaṟṟiyum tokuttut tiṇikku uriya āmāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
itaṉ poruḷ:- paṉmiyik karutiya ellāp peyarkaḷum aḵṟiṇikkaṇ oṉṟum palavum, uyartiṇik kaṇ āṇpālum peṇpālum eṉṟu collappaṭum nāṉku pāṟkum oru taṉmiya eṉṟavāṟu.
paṉmi cuṭṭiya peyar paṉmi iyaṟpeyarum, paṉmic ciṉip peyarum, paṉmic ciṉimutaṟpeyarum eṉa mūṉṟu 1vakippaṭum.
īṇṭup paṉmi eṉṟatu irutiṇip paṉmiyum aṉṟu; palapāṉ mēlum varutaliṟ paṉmi eṉṟā2ṉākak koḷka.
(u-m.) 
paṉmi iyaṟpeyar - yāṉi vantatu, yāṉi vantaṉa, yāṉi vantāṉ, yāṉi vantāḷ eṉa varum.
3īṇṭu 4oṉṟē palavē eṉṟataṉi 5aḵṟiṇi āṇ oṉṟaṉiyum peṇ oṉṟaṉiyum ākak koḷka. paṉmikkum aḵtu okkum. 6uyiril oṉṟu palavum koḷḷaṟka. oruvar eṉpataṉi uyartiṇi irupāṉ mēlum koḷka.
7paṉmi iyaṟ peyar.
paṉmic ciṉip peyar - neṭuṅkaḻuttal vantatu, vantaṉa, vantāṉ, vantāḷ eṉavarum.
paṉmic ciṉi mutaṟ peyar - peruṅkāl yāṉi vantatu, vantaṉa, vantāṉ, vantāḷ eṉavarum.
	(28)
aṭikkuṟippukaḷ
179-1 	ei “eṉṟu uṇarappaṭum”
2 	i “rā”

180.	orumi cuṭṭiya ellāp peyarum
	oṉṟaṟkum oruvaṟkum oṉṟiya niliyē.
eṉpatu eṉnutaliṟṟō eṉiṉ, orumippeyar ellā vaṟṟiyum tokuttut tiṇikku uriyāmāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
itaṉ poruḷ:- orumiyik karutiṉa ellāp peyarum, aḵṟiṇiyuḷ oṉṟaṟkum uyartiṇiyuḷ oruvaṟkum niṟṟal poruntiṉa eṉṟavāṟu.
orumippeyar orumi iyaṟpeyarum, orumic ciṉip peyarum, orumic ciṉimutaṟ peyarum eṉa mūṉṟu vakippaṭum.
(u-m.) 
1orumi iyaṟpeyar :- kōti vantatu, vantāṉ, vantāḷ eṉavarum.
2oṉṟu eṉpataṉi aḵṟiṇi āṇpāṉ mēlum peṇpāṉ mēlum koḷka. oruvar eṉpataṉi uyartiṇi irupāṉ mēlum koḷka.
orumic ciṉip peyar - ceviyili vantatu, vantāṉ, vantāḷ eṉa varum.
orumic ciṉi mutaṟ peyar :- koṭumpuṟa maruti vantatu, vantāṉ, vantāḷ eṉavarum.	(29)
aṭikkuṟippukaḷ
180-1 	ii “kōti vantāḷ”
2 	ie (ippatti muḻuvatum illi)
181.	tām eṉ kiḷavi paṉmik kurittē.
eṉpatu eṉnutaliṟṟō eṉiṉ, tām eṉpatu tiṇikku urittu āmāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
itaṉ poruḷ:- tām eṉpatu irutiṇip paṉmikkum urittu eṉṟavāṟu.
(u-m.) 
tām vantār, tām vantaṉa eṉa varum.
1vāḷātu paṉmi eṉṟamiyiṉ āṇ paṉmiyum peṇ paṉmiyum eṉak koḷka.	(30)

183.	ellām eṉṉum peyarnilik kiḷavi
	palvaḻi nutaliya nilittā kummē.
eṉpatu eṉnutaliṟṟō eṉiṉ, ellām eṉṉum col  tiṇikku urittu āmāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

(u-m.) ellām vantār, ellām vantaṉa, 2ellām vantir,  eṉa varum.

	ei “ellām vantaṉa ellām vantīr ellām vantēm”

184.	taṉṉuḷ uṟutta paṉmik kalla
	tuyartiṇi maruṅkiṉ ākkam illi.
eṉpatu eṉnutaliṟṟō eṉiṉ, eytiyatu oru maruṅku maṟuttal nutaliṟṟu.
itaṉ poruḷ:- mūṉṟu iṭattum irutiṇi mēlum ceṉṟamiyiṉ, taṉmi iṭattu aḵṟiṇik kaṇ ākātu 1eṉṟamiyāṉ ellām eṉṉum col colluvāṉ taṉṉi 2uḷḷuṟuttaḵtu aḵṟiṇi moḻi kūṟāmiyiṉ aḵṟiṇit taṉmip paṉmikku uyartiṇi iṭattu allatu aḵṟiṇi iṭattu ākutal illi eṉṟavāṟu.
uyartiṇi maruṅkiṉ allatu eṉa moḻi māṟṟik koḷka.
(u-m.) 
ellām uṇṭum eṉpatu.
maṟṟu iḵtu aḵṟiṇi moḻi kūṟāmiyiṉ aḵṟiṇit taṉmi illi eṉpatu peṟutum ; ākaliṉ itu kūṟal vēṇṭā eṉiṉ, mēl pal vaḻi nutaliya nilittākum eṉa ataṟku viti ceṉṟamiyiṉ atu 3vilakkal 4vēṇṭum eṉpatu.
maṟṟu, ellām uṇṭum eṉṟa taṉmikkaṇ varukiṉṟa varaviṉi uyartiṇip peyarāka vēṟu ōtaṟpāṟṟu eṉiṉ, ellām eṉpatu pōla uyartiṇikkē urittu ākātu muṉṉilik kaṇṇum paṭarkkik kaṇṇum viravāniṟṟaliṉ, īṇṭē 5ōta iṉṉuḻi varavaṉṟi uyartiṇip peyarām 
eṉṟavāṟu 6pōlum eṉpatu .	(33)

185.	nīyir nīeṉa varūum kiḷavi
	1pālteri pilavē uṭaṉmoḻip poruḷa.
eṉpatu eṉnutaliṟṟō eṉiṉ, nīyir nī eṉpaṉavaṟṟit  tiṇikku uriya āmāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
itaṉ poruḷ:- nīyir eṉavum nī eṉavum varūum coṟkaḷ tiṇi 2terivila ; uyartiṇiyāyum aḵṟiṇi yāyum uṭaṉ uṇarttalip poruṇmiyāka uṭiya eṉṟavāṟu.
maṟṟuttiṇi teriyāmiyiṉ aṉṟē viravup peyar āyatu; itu collavēṇṭumō eṉiṉ, mēl viravup 3peyar kaḷit 4tattammarapiṉ viṉiyōṭu allatu 5pāṟeripila  eṉṟamiyiṉ iviyum tattamarapiṉ tiṇiyāṉ uṇaraṟpāṭu ceṉṟami kaṇṭu, ivi muṉṉilip peyar ākaliṉ ivaṟṟukkuvarum muṉṉili viṉiyum viravā ākalāṉ eytiyatu vilakkutaṟkuk kūṟiṉāṉ6 eṉpatu.
(u-m.) 
nīyir vantīr, nī vantāy 7eṉattiṇi kaṇṭu koḷka. 	(34)

187.	ēṉik kiḷavi paṉmik kurittē.

188.	oruvar eṉṉum peyarnilik kiḷavi
	irupāṟkum urittē teriyum kāli.
eṉpatu eṉnutaliṟṟō eṉiṉ, uyartiṇi atikārattiṉ oḻipuuṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
itaṉ poruḷ:- oruvar eṉṟu collappaṭukiṉṟa 1peyarniliyuṭiya col uyartiṇi āṇpālum peṇpālum ākiya irupāṟkum urimiyi uṭittu ārāyum kālattu 2eṉṟavāṟu.
(u-m.) 
oruvar vantār eṉṟāl irupāṟkum urittu eṉak koḷka. irupāṟkum urittu eṉpatu oru collutaṟ kaṇ ṇēyō eṉiṉ, iru collutaṟ kaṇṇē eṉak koḷka. 
maṟṟu iruvarik kūṟum paṉmik kiḷavi eṉavum, piṟavum irupāliṉiyum orukālē taḻuviyum varumāl eṉiṉ āṇṭu 3collāṟpaṭuvatu orupālē maṟṟippāl kuṟippāṟ peṟappaṭukiṉṟatu eṉakkoḷka. 
maṟṟu irupāl eṉṟāṟ paṉmi oḻinta irupālum eṉpatu yāṅṅaṉam peṟutum eṉiṉ, oruvar eṉa niṉṟa orumi vāypāṭṭāṟ paṉmi nīṅkiṟṟu eṉak koḷka.
4pālatikārattē collātatu eṉṉi eṉiṉ, orupāṟku urittu ākātu viravi varukiṉṟa nīrmiyāl īṇṭup pōntatu eṉak koḷka.	(37)
aṭikkuṟippukaḷ

3	ei “colliṟ paṭuvatu”
4 	i-ii-iii-ie-e-eii “. . . tikārattē”
189.	taṉmi cuṭṭiṟ paṉmik kēṟkum.
eṉpatu eṉnutaliṟṟō eṉiṉ, iḵtu oruvar eṉṉum coṟku muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
itaṉ poruḷ:- 1muṟkūṟiya........ oruvar eṉṉum colliṉatu col muṭiyum taṉmiyik 2karutiṉ tāṉ kāṭṭukiṉṟa poruṇmikku ēṟpa orumic collōṭu muṭiyātu oruvar eṉa 3rakara īṟṟatāy niṉṟa ac coṟṟaṉmikku ēṟpap 4paṉmic col koṇṭu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.
(u-m.) 
oruvar vantār eṉavarum. ituvum ōr pāl paṟṟiya marapu vaḻuvamiti eṉak koḷka. 	(38)
aṭikkuṟippukaḷ

2 	“karutiṟṟāṉ”

	ii-eii “paṉmi . . .  muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu u-m”
	iii-e “paṉ . . . u-m.”
190.	iṉṉa peyarē ivieṉal vēṇṭiṉ
	muṉṉañ cērtti muṟiyiṉ uṇartal.
eṉpatu eṉnutaliṟṟō eṉiṉ, ivoruvar eṉṉum coṟkum 1mēṟkūṟiya nīyir nī eṉṉum coṟkum eytiyatōr ilakkaṇam uṇarttal nutaliṟṟu.
itaṉ poruḷ:- nīyir, nī 2eṉṟum oruvar eṉṟum niṉṟa ivi iraṇṭum ‘nīyir nī’ eṉpaṉa  uyartiṇikku urittu  aḵṟiṇikku urittu eṉṟum, ‘oruvar eṉpatu āṇpāṟku urittu ; peṇpāṟku urittu 3eṉṟu ...... pāl teriya nillāmiyiṉ avi īṇṭu iṉṉa peyar eṉpatu aṟiyal vēṇṭiṉ accoṟkaḷik kūṟuvāṉ karuttiṉi ac coṟ kaḷoṭu cērtti nīyir, nī eṉavum, oruvar eṉavum mēl ōtiya muṟiyāṉē tiṇiyum pālum uṇarntu 4koḷka eṉṟavāṟu.
vilaṅku varutaṟpālatallatōr vaḻiyiruntu nīyir vantīr, eṉṟāṉum, nī vantāy eṉṟāṉum kūṟiṉ īṇṭu uyartiṇi eṉavum iṉi, makkaḷ ilvaḻiyiruntu, avi kūṟiṉ aḵṟiṇi eṉavum, iṉik kāṭṭukkaṭ 5pōta pōkā niṉṟuḻi oruvar pukuntār eṉiṉ āṇpāl eṉavum āṇ makkaḷ pukutaṟ pālatalvaḻi oruvar iruntār eṉil peṇpāl eṉavum, iṭamum kālamum ākiya muṉṉattāṉ uṇarntavāṟu kaṇṭu koḷka.
maṟṟu, iḵtu atikārattāṉ oruvar eṉṉum coṟkē kūṟiyatu 6aṉṟō, nīyir nī eṉpataṉiyum uṭaṉ kūṭṭi uritta vāṟu eṉṉi eṉiṉ, paṉmi kūṟiya ataṉāṉum ēṟpuḻik kōṭal eṉpataṉāṉum immūṉṟaṟkum 7koḷḷappaṭṭatu eṉpatu. 	(39)

viṉiyiyal
viṉiyeṉpatu palaporuḷorucollāyt toḻiṟ paṇpiṉiyum ataṉ kāriyamākiya viṉinikaḻcciyiyum uṇarttum, toḻiṟpaṇpi yuṇarttuñ colli uriccolleṉavum ataṉ kāriyamākiya viṉinikaḻcciyi yuṇarttuñcolli viṉiccolleṉavum kūṟutal marapu. viṉiccollāvatu vēṟṟumiyurupēlātu veḷip paṭiyākavum kuṟippākavum kālattōṭu viḷaṅkuvatākum. iṟantakālam, nikaḻkālam etirkālam eṉak kālam mūṉṟām. toḻil muṟṟuppeṟṟanili iṟantakālam. toḻil toṭaṅki muṭivupeṟātu toṭarntu nikaḻumnili nikaḻkālam. toḻilē toṭaṅkappeṟātanili etirkālam. immukkālaṅkaḷuḷ oṉṟi veḷippaṭiyākak kāṭṭuvaṉavaṟṟi viṉiyeṉṟum ivaṟṟik kuṟippāka uṇarttuvaṉavaṟṟik kuṟippu eṉṟum kūṟuvar. tolkāppiyaṉār. piṟ kālattār ivaṟṟi muṟiyē terinili viṉiyeṉṟum kuṟippu viṉiyeṉṟum vaḻaṅkuvar. ivviṉiccoṟkaḷ muṟṟu, viṉiyeccam, peyareccam eṉa mūvakiya. pāluṇarttum īṟukaḷākiya vikutikaḷōṭukūṭi niṟintu niṟpaṉa viṉimuṟṟukkaḷām. aimpālavākiya viṉimutalit tarum vikutiyuṟuppuk kuṟinta kuṟiccoṟkaḷāy maṟṟoru viṉiccollōṭallatu muṟṟuppeṟātu niṟpaṉa viṉiyeccaṅkaḷām. pāluṇarttum vikutiyiṉṟik kuṟitta kuṟiccoṟkaḷāyp peyari eccamākavuṭiya viṉiccoṟkaḷ peyareccam eṉappaṭum. viṉiccoṟkaḷiṉ ilakkaṇa muṇarttiṉamiyāl itu viṉiyiyaleṉṉum peyarttāyiṟṟu. ivviyaliluḷḷa cūttiraṅkaḷi 49-āka iḷampūraṇarum 51-ākac cēṉāvariyarum nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum 54-ākat teyvacciliyārum pakuttu uri kūṟiyuḷḷārkaḷ. eccaviyaliluḷḷa ‘iṟappiṉ nikaḻviṉ’, ‘evvayiṉ viṉiyum’, ‘avitām tattaṅkiḷavi’ eṉavarum mūṉṟu cūttiraṅkaḷiyum viṉiyilakkaṇamātal paṟṟi ivviyaliṉ iṟutiyil, teyvacciliyār cērtturittamiyāl avar karuttuppaṭi ivviyaliṉ cūttiraṅkaḷ 54-āyiṉa. viṉiccoṟkaḷellā vaṟṟiyum uyartiṇikkuriyaṉa, aḵṟiṇikkuriyaṉa, iru tiṇikkumuriyaṉa eṉa mūṉṟu vakiyāka ivviyalil āciriyar pakuttuk kūṟiyuḷḷār.
uyartiṇikkuriyaṉa
uyartiṇiviṉi taṉmiviṉi paṭarkkiviṉi eṉa iṭattāl iruvakittu. avaṟṟuḷ taṉmiviṉi paṉmit taṉmiyum taṉittaṉmiyum eṉa iruvakippaṭum. am, ām, em, ēm, kum, ṭum, tum, ṟum eṉṉum ivveṭṭu vikutikaḷiyum iṟutiyākavuṭiya viṉiccoṟkaḷ paṉmiyuṇarttun taṉmic coṟkaḷām. taṉmik kuṟittup pēcutaṟkēṟṟa moḻivaḷam uyartiṇi māntarkkē yuriyatākaliṉ taṉmic coṟkaḷyāvum uyartiṇic collēyām. oruvaṉō oruttiyō taṉṉik kuṟittup pēcuṅkāl taṉakku orumiyallatu paṉmi collutaṟkiṭamilli. eṉiṉum taṉakku muṉṉum ayalilum uḷḷa piṟariyum taṉṉōṭu uḷappaṭuttuk kūṟum vaḻakkamuṇmiyāl taṉmip paṉmiyum koḷḷa vēṇṭiyatāyiṟṟu. ākavē paṉmit taṉmi yeṉṟatu uḷappāṭṭut taṉmiyēyām. taṉṉoṭu muṉṉiṉṟāri yuḷappaṭuttalum paṭarkkiyāri yuḷappaṭuttalum avviru tiṟattāriyum oruṅku uḷappaṭuttalum eṉa uḷappaṭuttal mūvakippaṭumeṉavum, am, ām eṉṟa vikutikaḷ muṉṉiliyāriyum tamarāyavaḻip paṭarkkiyāriyum, em, ēm eṉpaṉa paṭarkkiyāriyum, kum, ṭum, tum, ṟum eṉpaṉa avvirutiṟattāriyum uḷappaṭuttumeṉavum, am, ām, em, ēm eṉpaṉa mūṉṟu kālamum paṟṟi varutalum kum, ṭum, tum, ṟum eṉpaṉa etirkālampaṟṟi varutalum uṭiya veṉavum kūṟuvar cēṉāvariyar.
ku, ṭu, tu, ṟu eṉ, ēṉ, al eṉpavaṟṟi iṟatiyākavuṭiya ēḻum orumiyuṇarttum taṉittaṉmi (taṉmiyorumi) viṉiccoṟkaḷām. avaṟṟuḷ ceyku eṉṉum vāypāṭṭu viṉimuṟṟu viṉikoṇṭu muṭiyumāyiṉum muṟṟuc collākiya ilakkaṇattiṟ ciṟitum māṟupāṭatu.
aṉ, āṉ, aḷ, āḷ eṉṉum īṟṟiyuṭiya nālvakic coṟkaḷum uyartiṇi yorumi yuṇarttum paṭarkki viṉiccoṟkaḷām. ar, ār, pa eṉṉum īṟṟiyuṭiya mūvakic coṟkaḷum palarpāṟ paṭarkkiyām. mār eṉpatum uyartiṇip palarpāṟ paṭarkki viṉikkīṟātaliyuṭittu. atu muṭiyuṅkāl peyarkoḷḷātu viṉikoṇṭu muṭiyum. ivvāṟu am vikuti mutalāka mār vikuti yīṟākac collappaṭṭa irupattumūṉṟīṟṟu viṉiccoṟkaḷum uyartiṇikkē yuriyaṉavām.
mēṟcollappaṭṭavaṟṟuḷ paṉmiyuṇarttum taṉmic col tiṇiviravi yeṇṇuṅkāl aḵṟiṇiyi yuḷappaṭuttuttiritalum uṇṭu. yār eṉṉum viṉāviṉik kuṟippu uyartiṇi mūṉṟu pālukkum oppavuriyatākum. pāluṇarttum īṟukaḷākiya ṉa, ḷa, ra, eṉṉum iṟutiyiyuṭiya ākāramum muṉṉiliyil varum āy eṉpataṉ ākāramum ceyyuḷuḷ ōkāramāyt tiriyum.
āṟām vēṟṟumikkuriya uṭimipporuḷ, ēḻām vēṟṟumikkuriya nilapporuḷ, oppupporuḷ, paṇpu eṉṉumivaṟṟi nilikkaḷamākak koṇṭum aṉmi, iṉmi, uṇmi, vaṉmi eṉpavaṟṟi aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭum viṉikkuṟipput tōṉṟumeṉpar āciriyar kuṟippāṟ kāla muṇarttaliṉ itu kuṟippeṉappaṭṭatu. muṉṉark kūṟiya irupattumūṉṟīṟṟu viṉiccoṟkaḷukkīṟākiya eḻuttukkaḷiyē (vikutikaḷiyē) ivviṉickuṟippuc coṟkaḷum peṟṟup pāluṇarttuvaṉavām.
aḵṟiṇikkuriyaṉa
ā, ā, va eṉṉum iṟutiyiyuṭiya mūvaki viṉic coṟkaḷum aḵṟiṇippaṉmip paṭarkkiyām. tu, ṟu, ṭu eṉpavaṟṟi yiṟutiyāka uṭiyaṉa aḵṟiṇi orumippāṟkuriya viṉiccoṟkaḷām. iṅkeṭuttuk kāṭṭiya aṟuvakiyīṟṟuc coṟkaḷē aḵṟiṇikkuriya viṉiccoṟkaḷām. viṉāp poruḷiyuṭiya evaṉ eṉṉum viṉik kuṟippuccol aḵṟiṇiyirupālukkum oppavuriyatākum. iṉṟu, ila uṭiya, aṉṟu, uṭittu, alla, uḷa eṉpaṉavum paṇpukoḷ kiḷaviyum paṇpiṉākiya ciṉimutaṟ kiḷaviyum oppuppaṟṟi varuñcollum ākiya ippattum aḵṟiṇi viṉikkuṟippuc coṟkaḷām. mēl aḵṟiṇi viṉiccoṟkīṟāy niṉṟu pāluṇarttu meḻuttukkaḷē aḵṟiṇi viṉik kuṟippuc coṟkaṇṇum īṟāy niṉṟu pāl viḷakkuvaṉa.
irutiṇikkumuriyaṉa
muṉṉili viṉimuṟṟu, viyaṅkōḷ muṟṟu, viṉiyeccam, iṉmiyiyuṇarttum illi, il eṉpaṉa. vēṟu eṉṉum col, ceymmaṉ eṉṉum vāypāṭṭu muṟṟu, muṟṟum peyareccamumākiya ceyyumeṉṉum vāypāṭṭu viṉiccol, ceyta eṉṉum vāypāṭṭup peyareccam ākiya eṇvaki viṉiccoṟkaḷum irutiṇic collātaṟkum otta urimi yuṭiyaṉavām.
mēṟkūṟappaṭṭa viravu viṉikaḷuḷ i, ai, āy eṉṉum iṟutiyiyuṭiya muṉṉili viṉiccoṟkaḷ oruvaṉ, orutti, oṉṟu eṉṉum irutiṇi mukkūṟṟorumikkum oppavuriyaṉa. ir, īr, miṉ eṉṉum iṟutiyiyuṭiya muṉṉili viṉic coṟkaḷ uyartiṇip palarpālukkum aḵṟiṇip palaviṉ pālukkum oppavuriyaṉa. muṉṉili viṉiyallāta ēṉi eḻuvaki viṉiccoṟkaḷum irutiṇi aimpāl mūviṭattiṟkum potuvāy varuvaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ viyaṅkōḷ, viṉi muṉṉili taṉmiyeṉṉum iraṇṭiṭattilum nilipeṟātu. nikaḻkāla muṇarttum ceyyumeṉṉum mūṉṟu palarpāṟ paṭarkkiyilum taṉmi muṉṉilikaḷilum varutalilli.
maṟṟoru viṉiccollōṭu kūṭiyallatu muṟṟuppeṟāta, kuṟiccol viṉiyeccamākum. ceytu, ceyyū, ceypu, ceyteṉa, ceyyiyar, ceyyiya, ceyiṉ, cey, ceyaṟku eṉavarum ivvoṉpatu vāypāṭṭuc coṟkaḷum piṉ, muṉ, kāl, kaṭi, vaḻi, iṭattu eṉpavaṟṟi yiṟutiyāka vuṭiyaṉavum ivipōlak kālamuṇartti varuvaṉa piṟavum viṉiyecca viṉikaḷām. ivaṟṟuḷ mutaliluḷḷa ceytu, ceyyū, ceypu eṉa varum mūṉṟum taṉakkuriya viṉi mutal viṉiyiyē koṇṭu muṭivaṉa. avi mūṉṟum ciṉi viṉiyāṅkāl tamakkuriya mutal viṉiyikkoṇṭu muṭiyuṉum tamtoḻilik koṇṭu muṭintaṉavē. immūṉṟumallāta piṟa viṉiyeccaṅkaḷ tam viṉi mutal viṉiyiyēṉum aṉṟi aṅku vantu iyiyum piṟa viṉimutal viṉiyiyeṉum variyaṟiyiṉṟik koṇṭu muṭiyumiyalpiṉavām. viṉiyeccaṅkaḷ pala vāypāṭṭāṉ aṭukkivariṉum muṉṉiṉṟa eccam muṭiya ēṉiyavum poruḷāl muṭintaṉavēyām.
peyari oḻipākavuṭiya viṉiccol peyareccam atu ceyyum, ceyta eṉavarum iru vāypāṭukaḷil aṭaṅkum nilappeyar, poruṭpeyar, kālappeyar, karuvippeyar, viṉi mutaṟpeyar, viṉippeyar eṉavarum aṟuvakip peyarkaḷiyum koṇṭu muṭitaṟkēṟṟa poruḷ nilimiyiyuṭiya ippeyareccam irutiṇi yimpāṟku muriya potu viṉiyākum. ceyyum eṉṉum vāypāṭṭuc coṟkaḷ peyareccamāṅkāl muṉ ceyyumeṉṉum muṟṟiṟku vilakkappaṭṭa pallōr paṭarkki muṉṉili, taṉmi eṉṉum ivviṭaṅkaḷukkum uriyaṉavām.
peyareccamum viṉiyeccamum viṉi nikaḻcciyi yuṇarttātu ataṉi etirmaṟuttuc colliṉum peyareñca niṟṟalum viṉiyeñca niṟṟalumākiya tam poruḷ nilimiyil vēṟupaṭā. ivveccaṅkaḷukkum ivaṟṟi muṭikkuñ coṟkaḷāy varum peyar viṉikaḷukkumiṭiyē muṭikkuñcolloṭu toṭarpuṭiya vēṟu col vantu niṟṟalumuṇṭu.
ceyyumeṉṉum peyarecca vāypāṭukaḷiṉ īṟṟayaleḻut tākiya ukaram tāṉ ūrntuniṉṟa meyyoṭuṅ keṭṭu muṭitalum uṇṭu. ceytu eṉṉum vāyppāṭṭu iṟantakāla viṉiyeccam ēṉik kālac collōṭu iyitalum, viriviṉkaṇ etirkālamum nikaḻkālamum iṟantakālattōṭu mayaṅkutalum, mikkatu oṉṟiṉkaṇṇē iṟantakālamum etirkālamum nikaḻkālattōṭu mayaṅkutalum, itu ceyal vēṇṭum eṉṉum poruḷil varum viṉiccol tāṉum piṟarumākiya īriṭattum poruḷ tarutalum, vaṟpuṟuttalil varum viṉāp poruḷiyuṭiya viṉiccol etirmaṟip poruḷ tarutalum, iyaṟki paṟṟiyum teḷivupaṟṟiyum kālam mayaṅkutalum, ceyappaṭuporuḷ viṉimutalpōla varutalum, ivvāṟē vēṟiṭaṅkaḷil mukkālamum mayaṅkutalum ākiya vitikaḷi ivviyal 40-mutal 49-variyuḷḷa cūttiraṅkaḷāl āciriyar virittuk kūṟiyuḷḷār.
- ka. veḷḷivāraṇaṉār 
nūlvarici 10, pak. 208-213

viṉiyiyal
195.	1viṉieṉap paṭuvatu vēṟṟumi koḷḷātu
	niṉiyuṅ kālik kālamoṭu tōṉṟum.
eṉpatu eṉnutaliṟṟō eṉiṉ:- ivottu eṉṉa peyarttō eṉiṉ, viṉiyatu ilakkaṇam uṇarttiṉamiyiṉ viṉiyiyal eṉṉum peyarttu.
mēl ōttiṉōṭu 2ivottiṭi iyipu eṉṉiyō eṉiṉ, muṉṉip peyariyaluḷ niṟutta muṟiyāṉē peyar uṇartti ataṉ piṉṉar viṉi uṇarttiya toṭaṅkiṉār eṉpatu.
itaṉ talic cūttiram eṉnutaliṟṟō eṉiṉ, viṉic coṟkellām potuvāyatōr 3ilakkaṇam .............

(u-m.)

mēṟ kūṟiya muṟiyiṉ eṉṟa cūtti............ maṉṟatu viṉikku yātum ākātu eṉpatē yaṉṟi, viṉikkāyiṉ mutalum iṭiyum ellām ām eṉpatum koḷḷak kiṭantamiyiṉ atu vilakkiya eṉpatu.
niṉiyuṅkāli eṉṟataṉāṉ, kālamtaṉṉi mūṉṟu 6eṉpārum .............. iraṇṭē eṉpārum eṉap palamatam uṇṭu eṉpatu aṟivikkappaṭṭatu. 	(1)

200.	kaṭa taṟa eṉṉum,
	annāṉ kūrnta kuṟṟiya lukaramō
	1ṭeṉ ēṉ al eṉa varūum ēḻum
	taṉviṉi urikkun 2taṉmic collē.
eṉpatu eṉnutaliṟṟō 3eṉiṉ, taṉittaṉmi āmāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
itaṉ poruḷ:- kaṭa taṟa eṉṟu collappaṭṭa an nāṉku oṟṟiṉiyum ūrnta kuṟṟiyalukara īṟṟuc coṟkaḷ nāṉkum eṉ ēṉ al eṉṉum mūṉṟu īṟṟuc collum eṉac collavarukiṉṟa avēḻu collum taṉmippaṉmic col 4pōlac colluvāṉ taṉṉōṭu piṟaṉviṉiyiyum uṇarttātu, taṉ viṉiyiyē uṇarttum taṉmic collām eṉṟavāṟu.
oṭu, eṇṇoṭu. 
ivaṟṟuḷ muṉṉiya nāṉkum etirkālam oṉṟumē paṟṟi varum.
(u-m.) 
uṇku, uṇṭu, varutu, cēṟu, uriñuku, tirumuku eṉa varum.
ivaṟṟuḷ takara ukaram:- 4kaḻintu poḻinteṉa vāṉkaṇ māṟiṉun, tollatu 5viḷinteṉa nilam vaḷaṅkarappiṉum.
eṉṉum puṟappāṭṭiṉuḷ poḻintu eṉavum viḷintu eṉavum iṟanta kālam paṟṟi vantamiyiṉ. e......... 6ciṟu pāṉmi iṟanta kālamum uṇṭēṉum aṟintu koḷka.
iṉi, kakara ukarattiṟku:- aḻāaṟ kōviṉi nōy non tuṟivi. eṉṉum kuṟuntokip pāṭṭiṉuḷ 7aḻuku eṉṉum uṭaṉpāṭṭiṟku aḻāaṟku eṉa etirmaṟi vantamiyiṉ oḻi vantatu etirmaṟi vāypāṭum uḷavēl aṟika.
iṉi eṉ ēṉ eṉpaṉa iraṇṭu īṟum mukkālattum uṭaṉpāṭṭiṉum maṟiyiṉum varum eṉak kaṇṭu koḷka.
(u-m.) 
uṇṭaṉeṉ, uṇṭileṉ; uṇṇā niṉṟaṉeṉ, uṇkiṉṟaṉeṉ; 8uṇṇāniṉṟi ......; uṇpeṉ, uṇkuveṉ, uṇṇaleṉ 9eṉa varum. ivi eṉ.

10uṇṭēṉallēṉ eṉavarum maṟivikaṟpamum aṟika.
iviraṇṭīṟum mūṉṟu kālattum varutalāl muṉ vikkaṟpāṟṟu eṉiṉ, muṉ cūttirattuk kaṭataṟakkaḷ kaṭikkaṇniṉṟa atikāram paṟṟi avaṟṟi muṟkūṟiṉāṉ eṉavu.

218.	muṉṉili viyaṅkōḷ viṉiyeñcu kiḷavi
	iṉmi ceppal vēṟeṉ kiḷavi
	ceymmaṉa ceyyum ceyta eṉṉum
	ammuṟi niṉṟa āyeṇ kiḷaviyum
	pirivu vēṟupaṭūum ceytiya vāki
	irutiṇic coṟkum ōraṉṉa urimiya.
eṉpatu eṉnutaliṟṟō eṉiṉ, 1mēl niṟutta muṟiyāṉē viravuviṉi uṇarttuvāṉ 2toṭaṅki, avaṟṟatu peyarum muṟiyum tokiyum uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
itaṉ poruḷ:- muṉṉili iṭattiṉit taṉakkup poruṇmiyāka uṇarttum collum, ēvalit taṉakkup poruṇmiyākak koṇṭa collum. viṉic3colli oḻipāka uṭiya collum iṉmi eṉṉum paṇpiṉi uṇattutaluṭiya collum, vēṟu eṉṉum collum, ceymmaṉa eṉṉum collum, ceyyum eṉṉum collum, ceyta eṉṉum collum eṉṟu collappaṭukiṉṟa av 4aṭiviṉ kaṇ niṉṟa av eṭṭuc collum orukāl uyartiṇik 5kaṇṇum orukāl aḵṟiṇikkaṇṇum potumiyiṉ 6tirintu vēṟupaṭṭu niṟkum toḻiliṉi 7uṭiyavāki, irutiṇiyākiya poruṭkum oṉṟu pōṉṟa urimi uṭiya eṉṟavāṟu.
muṉṉili eṉpatu muṉ uyartiṇik kaṇṇum aḵṟiṇik kaṇṇum taṉmiyum paṭarkkiyum kūṟiya iṭaṅkaḷuḷ8 kūṟātu 9niṉṟatu atu ākalāṉum, tāṉ 10pala īṟṟāl payiṉṟu varuvatākiya 11vaḻakkiṟ payiṟci uṭimiyāṉum, muṟṟuc col ātalāṉum, muṉvikkappaṭṭatu. 
iṉi, ataṉ piṉṉar viyaṅkō12ḷ muṟṟuc collu māyp perumpāṉmi varavu, paṭarkki eṉa 13ōriṭattumāyp palavaḻakkiṟṟum ākalāṉ vikkappaṭṭatu. 
ivi iraṇṭum avaṟṟup poruṇmiyāl pala īṟṟi oṉṟāka aṭakkappaṭṭaṉa. 
avaṟṟiṉ piṉṉar avipōla vaḻakkup payiṟci uṭimi yāṉ viṉiyeccam vikkappaṭṭatu. iḵtu ac collum muṭipilakkaṇattāṉ oṉṟāka aṭakki ōtappaṭṭatu. 
iṉmi ceppalum vēṟeṉkiḷaviyum, muṉṉiliyum viyaṅkōḷum pō14l, muṟṟē eṉiṉum 15viṉiyeccam pōlātu ciṟuvaraviṉa ākalāṉum, viṉik16kuṟippiṉmi yāṉum viṉiyeccattiṉ piṉ vikkappaṭṭaṉa. 
avaṟṟiṉ piṉṉar 17ceymmaṉa eṉpatu terinili muṟṟē eṉiṉum palavāypāṭṭatu 18eṉṉum payilavaḻaṅkuvār iṉmiyiṉ piṉ vikkap paṭṭatu. 
ataṉ piṉṉar ataṉōṭu otta peyareccam ākaliṟ ceyta eṉpatu vikkappaṭṭatu.
iṉmi ceppal mutal aintum accoṟkaḷ tammiyē ōtiṉa eṉiṉum vēṟu eṉpatu oṉṟum oḻiya maṟṟiyaṉa ellām pala vāypāṭṭaṉa eṉṟu koḷka.
aḵtu eṉṉi āmāṟu eṉiṉ, illi eṉṉātu iṉmi ceppal eṉṟataṉāl illi eṉpatum il eṉpatum eṉa iraṇṭu āyiṟṟu.
ceymmaṉa, ceyyum, ceyta eṉpaṉa uṇmaṉa, tiṉmaṉa e-m; uṇṇum, tiṉṉum e-m, uṇṭa, tiṉṟa eṉavum, pala vāypāṭṭa āyiṉa.
ceymmaṉa eṉṟāl uṇmaṉa, tiṉmaṉa peṟumāṟu eṉṉi eṉiṉ, accoṟkaḷiṉ poruṇmiyum oruvaḻic ceymmaṉa eṉpataṉāṟ collappaṭutaliṉ, iḵtu avaṟṟiṟku ellām potuvāypāṭṭatu eṉpatu.
aḵtēl avvāṟākap poruḷ uṇara niṉṟa vaḻiaṉṟē iḵtu accol taṉṉi uṇara niṉṟa iṭamāl eṉiṉ, aḵtu okkum; poruṇmiyāl potumi uṭiya colliṉik kūṟavē ataṉ uḷvaki ākiya coṟkaḷum taṉṉiṉa muṭittal eṉṉum tantira uttiyāṉ aṭaṅkum eṉpatu pōlum karuttu.		(24)

220.	ir īr miṉ eṉa varūu mūṉṟum
	pallōr maruṅkiṉum palavaṟṟu maruṅkiṉum
	collō raṉiya eṉmaṉār pulavar.
eṉpatu eṉnutaliṟṟō eṉiṉ, muṉṉilip paṉmi uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
itaṉ poruḷ:- ir, īr, miṉ eṉṟu colla varukiṉṟa mūṉṟu īṟṟuc collum uyartiṇikkaṇ1 pallōr iṭattum aḵṟiṇikkaṇ palavaṟṟiṉ iṭattum collutali oru taṉmiyāka uṭiya eṉṟu colluvar āciriyar eṉṟavāṟu.

224.	ceytu ceyyūc 1ceypu ceyteṉac
	ceyyiyar ceyyiya ceyiṉceyac ceyaṟkeṉa
	2avvaki oṉpatum viṉiyeñcu kiḷavi.
eṉpatu eṉnutaliṟṟō eṉiṉ, ituvum mēl eñciya kiḷavi eṉṟu ōtiya potuviti yuḷ3paṭṭa viṉiyeccam eṉpataṟku vāypāṭṭu vēṟṟumiyum muṭipu vēṟṟumiyum kūṟuvāṉ 4toṭaṅki avviṉiyeccaṅkaḷuḷ ciṟappuṭiya vāypāṭukaḷit tokuttu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
itaṉ poruḷ:- ceytu eṉpatu mutalākac ceyaṟku eṉpatu īṟāka ōtappaṭṭa akkūṟṟu oṉpatu vāypāṭṭatām. muṉ viṉiyeñcu kiḷavi eṉṟu ōtappaṭṭatu 5eṉṟavāṟu.
6avaṟṟuḷ ceytu eṉpatu mutalākac ceyteṉa eṉpatu  īṟāka 7annāṉkum iṟanta 8kālatta ākalāṉ muṉṉē uṭaṉ vikkap 9paṭṭatu. 
avaṟṟuḷ ceytu eṉpatu peruvaḻakkiṟṟu ākaliṉ 10avaṟṟuḷḷum 11muṉvikkappaṭṭatu. 
avaṟṟuḷ ceyteṉa 12eṉpatu avaṟṟiṟku ellām ciṟu vaḻakkiṟṟu ātalāṉum, piṟaviṉiyum kōṭalāṉum piṉ vikkappaṭṭatu. 
iṉic ceyyiyar eṉpatu mutalākac ceyaṟku eṉpatu īṟāka aintum etirkālatta ākalāṉum piṟaviṉiyum kōṭal uṇmiyāṉum, piṟaviṉi 13kōṭalum uṭiya ceyteṉa viṉiyiṉ piṉṉar uṭaṉ vikkappaṭṭaṉa. 
av aintum etirkālattavēṉum 14ceyyiyar ceyyiya eṉpaṉa iraṇṭum vāypāṭu vēṟṟumi allatu poruḷ vēṟ ṟumi iṉmiyiṉ uṭaṉ vikkap15paṭṭaṉa. 
avaṟṟiṉ piṉṉarc ceyiṉ eṉpatu vāypāṭṭu vēṟṟumiyōṭu poruḷ vēṟṟumiyum uṭimiyiṉ 16piṉ vikkappaṭṭatu. 
ceya eṉpatu 17etirkālattē aṉṟi 18piṟakālattum ciṟupāṉmi varutalāṉ ataṉ piṉvikkappaṭṭatu. 
ceyaṟku eṉpatu avipōla vaḻakkup payiṟci iṉmiyiṉ ellāvaṟṟiṉum piṉvikkap paṭṭatu.
19itaṉmuṉ ceytu eṉṟu ōtiyavāypāṭu kuṟṟiyalukarat tā20ṉ ārāyappaṭṭa kaṭataṟa eṉṉum nāṉku īṟum, ikara īṟum, 21yakara īṟum eṉa 22aṟuvakip paṭṭatām. av aṟuvakiyum ceytu eṉap poruṇmiyāl oṉṟāka vikkappaṭṭatu.
aḵtēl ceyyū, ceypu, ceyteṉa eṉpaṉavum itaṉuḷ 23aṭaṅkāvō 24eṉiṉ, aṭaṅkumēṉum ivaṟṟiṟku vēṟupāṭu uṇṭu eṉpatu 25aṟivittaṟku vēṟuōtiṉāṉ eṉpatu. yātō vēṟṟumi eṉiṉ, ceytu eṉṟataṉīṟu 26uḻutal eṉṉum toḻiṟkaṇṇē 27uḻutu eṉat takara ukara īṟāyum, uṇṭal eṉṉum toḻiṟkaṇṇē uṇṭu eṉa ṭakara ukara īṟāyum, tiṉṟal eṉṉum toḻiṟ 28kaṇṇē tiṉṟu eṉa ṟakara ukara īṟāyum, pukutal eṉṉum toḻiṟkaṇṇē pukku eṉak kakara ukara īṟāyum, ōṭal eṉṉum toḻiṟkaṇṇē ōṭi eṉa ikara īṟāyum, tūvutal eṉṉum 29 viṉikaṇṇē tūy eṉa yakarīṟāyum, oru 30toḻiṟkaṇ vēṟupaṭa vantavāṟu.
oru toḻiṟkaṇ vēṟupaṭavārāmi uṭiya attoḻil ellāvaṟṟilum uḻūu e-m, uḻupu e-m, 31uḻuteṉa e-m; 32uṇṇūu e-m, uṇkupu e-m, 33uṇṭeṉa e-m; tiṉṉūu e-m; tiṉkupu e-m; 34tiṉṟeṉa eṉavum; 35pukūu eṉavum, pukupu eṉavum, 36pukkeṉa eṉavum; ōṭūu eṉavum, ōṭupu eṉavum, 37ōṭṭeṉa eṉavum; tūu eṉavum, tūpu eṉavum, 38tūyeṉa eṉavum vēṟupaṭātu varutal uṭi miyiṉ vēṟu kūṟiṉāṉ eṉpatu.
ceyyū eṉpataṟkuc ceyyā eṉpatūum ōr vāypāṭu; atuvum 39oṉṟeṉa muṭittal eṉpataṉāṟ koḷḷappaṭum. itaṉi 40“iṟantakālam virivu poruṭṭu” eṉpārum uḷar.
ceytu eṉpataṟkuc ceyyāniṉṟu eṉpatūum ōr nikaḻkāla vāypāṭu. atuvum oṉṟeṉa muṭittal eṉpataṉāl koḷḷappaṭum.
iṉic ceyyiyar eṉpatu maḻi peyyiyar eḻuntatu eṉpatu.
ceyyiya eṉpatu maḻi peyyiya eḻuntatu eṉpatu.
ceyiṉ eṉpatu maḻipeyyiṟ kuḷam niṟiyum eṉpatu. itu nikaḻiṉ 41atu nikaḻum eṉṉum kāraṇap poruḷ paṟṟi varum. 
itaṟku maḻi peytāṟ kuḷam niṟiyum 42eṉa āṉ eṉpatum ōr vāypāṭu. atuvum oṉṟeṉa muṭittal eṉpataṉāṟ koḷka.
naṉaviṟ puṇarcci 43naṭakkalum eṉa um īṟātalum koḷka. ituvum oṉṟeṉa muṭittal eṉpataṉāṟ 44koḷka.
maḻi peytakkāl eṉpatō eṉiṉ, atu piṉ ōtu kiṉṟa kāl eṉṉum vāypāṭu eṉak koḷka.
maḻi peyyumēlum maḻi  45peyyumēṉum eṉavarum ēl ēṉ eṉpaṉavō eṉiṉ, avaṟṟiyum itaṉ kuṟippu eṉṟu kōṭalum oṉṟu. aṉṟi iveccap poruḷ paṭuvaṉa cila iṭiccol eṉṟu 46kōṭal oṉṟu.
oṉṟāṉum tīccol eṉpuḻi āṉō eṉiṉ, atuvum appāl ōr iṭiccol eṉṟalum oṉṟu. āyiṉum eṉṉum col āṉum eṉa iṭikkuṟintu niṉṟatu eṉṟalum oṉṟu.
nuṇaṅkiya kēḷviyar 47allāl eṉpuḻi allāl eṉpatō eṉiṉ aṉṟi eṉṉum ceytu eṉ eccakkuṟippiṟku atuvum ōr vāypāṭu eṉpatu. alla āyiṉ eṉpatu poruḷākki itaṉ kuṟippu eṉpārum uḷar.
iṉic ceya eṉpatu maḻi peya eḻuntatu eṉpatu. maḻi 48peyyak kuḷaniṟintatu eṉa iṟantakālattuk kaṇṇum varum. maḻi peyyak kuḷaniṟintatu eṉa nikaḻkālattum ciṟupāṉmi varum. iv eccantāṉ oruvaḻi maḻi peyyak kuḷaniṟintatu eṉak kāraṇapporuḷāyum, kuḷaniṟiya maḻi peytatu eṉa kāriyap poruḷāyum, maḻi peya eḻuntatu eṉa ataṟporuṭṭu eṉṉum poruṭṭāyum maḻi peyyac cāttaṉ 49vantāṉ eṉa uṭaṉikaḻcciyāy nikaḻtaṟkaṇ eṉa iṭapporuṭṭāyum piṟavāṟṟāyum varum eṉpatu.
50iṉi, tuḷḷip periya ōtiṉum ciṟiyuṇarā eṉpuḻip periya ciṟiya eṉpaṉa perumi ciṟumip paṇpaṭiyāka vantamiyiṉ iveccattiṉ kuṟippu eṉṟalum oṉṟu. iv eccap 51poruḷpaṭum uriccol eṉṟalum oṉṟu.
iṉic cevvaṉṟerikiṟpāṉ eṉavum, putuvataṉ iyaṉṟa 52vaṇiyaṉ eṉavum, putuvatu puṉinta veṇki yāppu eṉavum, poykippūpputitīṉa eṉavum, peruṅkiyaṟṟa 53veṉpulampu eṉavum, ciṟunaṉi nī tuñciyēṟpiṉum eṉavum, ollik 54koṇṭāṉ vallik koṇṭāṉ eṉavum, piṟavum akarīṟu iṉṟip piṟīṟāy varuvaṉavum avvāṟē urikkappaṭum.
iṉic ceyaṟku eṉpatu uṇaṟku vantāṉ eṉpatu. iḵtu ataṟ poruṭṭu eṉṉum poruḷpaṟṟi varum. itu uṇal eṉṉum toḻiṟ peyar nāṉkām urupu ēṟṟavāṟu aṉṟō eṉiṉ, atuvum ōr vaḻakku uṇṭu. peyarpporuṇmi nōkkiya55vaḻi atu ākavum kāla nōkkiyavaḻi viṉi eccamākavum koḷka. eṟṟukku eṉpatu itaṉ kuṟippu vāypāṭākak koḷka. 	(30)

itaṉ poruḷ:- am mūṉṟu collum ciṉipporuḷ iṭattu viṉiyeccamāyt tōṉṟiṉ 3muṉ kūṟiṉa vitikku ēṟpa viṉimutalākac ciṉiviṉiyāṉ 4muṭiyātu. ac ciṉi yuṭiya mutal viṉiyōṭu oṉṟāy 5muṭiyiṉum. ap piṟaviṉiyum  muṉ 6kūṟiya viṉiyoṭu oṉṟu ātaṟṟaṉmiyi uṭiya, eṉṟu colluvar āciriyar eṉṟavāṟu.
(u-m.) 
kiyiṟṟu vīḻntāṉ, kiyiṟūu vīḻntāṉ, kiyiṟupu vīḻntāṉ eṉavarum.
maṟṟitu 7kiyiṟa vīḻntāṉ eṉac ceyaveṉṉeccat tiripu ākaṟpāṟṟu eṉiṉ, avvāṟu āvatu iṟutaṟṟoḻil 8kiyatum vīḻtaṟṟoḻiṉ mutalatum āyiṉ aṉṟē ; iṟutalum vīḻtalum kiyatākak kūṟukiṉṟatā9kiṉ ataṉuḷ aṭaṅkātu eṉpatu. ataṉ poruḷ kiyiṟṟu vīḻntavāṟākak koḷka. 

kālaḻuki vīḻntāṉ eṉpatum atu.
kiyiṟṟāṉ, kālaḻukiṉāṉ eṉpaṉa 10āṇṭikku utāraṇamō eṉiṉ 11avi viṉiyeccam aṉmiyiṟ kaṇṇum tōḷum muliyum piṟavum eṉṉum āṇṭikkē utāraṇam12 ākak koḷka. 
maṟṟuk kiyiṟṟu vīḻntāṉ kālaḻuki vīḻntāṉ eṉpaṉavum āṇṭit tiṇivaḻuvamitikku utāraṇam 13ātaliṉ īṇṭik kūṟavēṇṭā eṉiṉ. tiṇivaḻuvukkaṉṟu īṇṭuk 14kūṟukiṉṟatu; ataṟku viti kaṇṇum tōḷum  eṉpatē ; 15īṇṭuk kūṟiyatu, 16mēltaṉ viṉiyāṉ muṭiyum 17eṉṟaṉa oruvaḻit taṉṉōṭu toṭarnta 18piṟaviṉiyāṉum muṭiyum eṉpatu āyiṟṟu.
ciṉiyoṭu muṭiyā mutaloṭu muṭiyiṉum viṉi yōraṉiya 19 eṉṟamiyāṉ ituvum ōr marapu vaḻuvamiti eṉak koḷka.
kuraṅku kiyiṟṟu vīḻntatu eṉa immuṭipu aḵṟiṇi viṉikkum koḷka.
īṇṭuc ciṉiviṉi 20mutal viṉiyoṭu muṭiyum eṉpatē colliyatu eṉi21ṉ, cāttaṉatu kiyiṟṟu vīḻntatu eṉa viṉimuṭipuḷḷa vaḻiyum cāttaṉatu kiyiṟṟu vīḻntāṉ eṉavum āmpiṟa, eṉiṉ, ākātu; am22mutaltāṉum eḻuvāy ākiya vaḻiyatu immuṭipu eṉak koḷka. 	(33)

230.	nilaṉum poruḷum kālamum karuviyum
	viṉimutaṟ kiḷaviyum viṉiyum uḷappaṭa
	avvaṟu poruṭkum ōraṉṉa urimiya
	ceyyum ceyta eṉṉum collē.
eṉpatu eṉnutaliṟṟō 1eṉiṉ, eñciya kiḷavi eṉṟu eṭuttavaṟṟuḷ viṉiyeccam 2uṇarttip peyareccamākiya ceyyum ceyta eṉpaṉavaṟṟiṟku muṭipuṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
itaṉ poruḷ:- nilapporuṭpeyarum, ceyappaṭu poruṭ peyarum, kālap poruṭpeyarum, karuvip poruṭ peyarum, viṉimutaṟ poruṭpeyarum, 3viṉip poruṭ peyarum ākac collappaṭṭa avaṟuvakip poruṭ 4peyarkkum oru taṉmiyāṉa urimiyiṉiyuṭiya, ceyyum ceyta eṉṉum iruvakippaṭṭa collum eṉṟavāṟu.
(u-m.) 
nilam:- avaṉ uṇṇum illam, avaḷ uṇṇum illam, avar uṇṇum illam, atu uṇṇum illam, avi uṇṇum illam eṉavarum.
poruḷ: avaṉ uṇṇum cōṟu, avaḷ uṇṇum cōṟu, avar uṇṇum cōṟu, atu uṇṇum cōṟu, avi uṇṇum cōṟu eṉa varum.
5kālam: avaṉ uṇṇum kāli, avaḷ uṇṇum kāli, avar uṇṇum kāli, atu uṇṇum kāli, avi uṇṇum kāli eṉavarum.
karuvi: avaṉ eṟiyum kal, avaḷ eṟiyum kal, avar eṟiyum kal, aḵtu eṟiyum kal, avi eṟiyum kal eṉavarum.
viṉimutal: uṇṇum avaṉ, uṇṇum avaḷ, uṇṇum avar, uṇṇum atu, uṇṇum avi eṉavarum.
viṉi: avaṉ uṇṇum ūṇ, avaḷ uṇṇum ūṇ, avar uṇṇum ūṇ, atu uṇṇum ūṇ, avi uṇ ṇum ūṇ eṉavarum.
iṉi, ceyta eṉpataṟkum ivvāṟē yāṉ uṇṭa illam, nī uṇṭa illam, avaṉ uṇṭa illam eṉṟāṟ pōla mūṉṟu iṭattiṟkum ivviṭattu vāypāṭu vikaṟpaṅkaḷum aṟintu oṭṭikkoḷka.
7īṇṭuc ceyyum eṉpatu muṟṟum 8eccamum eṉa iru 9īṟṟatākum ciṟappuṭimiyiṉ muṟkūṟappaṭṭatu.
maṟṟuc ceyyum eṉpatu pallōr paṭarkki eṉpuḻik kūṟiṟṟu ākaliṉ īṇṭuk kūṟavēṇṭā eṉiṉ, āṇṭu muṟṟāya nilimikkuk kūṟiyatu; īṇṭu aḵtu eccam ākiya nilimikkuk kūṟiyatu eṉak koḷka. 10maṟṟu itu iv iru nilimiyum peyaroṭu 11muṭiyumēl avvēṟupāṭu aṟiyumāṟu eṉṉi eṉiṉ, muṟṟāyppeyarkoṇṭavaḻi maṟṟōr 12col nōkkātu 13ceppu......... mu.......kkāṟ pōla 14amintu māṟum. eccamāyp peyark koṇṭakkāl amiyātu maṟṟum ōr col nōkkiṟṟuppōla niṟkum eṉpatu.
iṉi muṟṟāyavaḻi uṇṇum eṉa ūṉṟiṉāṟpōla nalintu collappaṭum eṉṟum, eccamāyavaḻi ūṉṟātu nekiḻa muṭipu collappaṭum eṉṟum koḷka.
aḵtēl, pallōr paṭarkki eṉpuḻik kūṟiyatu 15muṟṟiṟku eṉṟum, īṇṭuk kūṟiyatu eccattiṟku eṉṟum peṟumāṟu eṉṉi eṉiṉ, īṇṭuc ceyta eṉpataṉōṭu 16paṭuttu muṭipu kūṟiṉami yāṉum, āṇṭu muṟṟuāyavaḻik koḷḷātaṉapaṟṟi vilakkiṉamiyāṉum 17peṟutum eṉpatu.
maṟṟu, im muṟṟu nilimiyiyum eccanilimiyiyum 18iraṇṭākap pakuttu ōtātatu eṉṉi eṉiṉ, poruḷ vēṟṟumi allatu vāypāṭṭu vēṟṟumi iṉmiyiṟ kūṟā19ṉ āyiṉāṉ eṉpatu.
ivaṟuvakip peyaruḷḷum viṉimutaṟ peyar oḻittu, oḻintavaṟṟiṟku ellām viṉimutaṟpeyar muṉvantallatu poruṇ muṟṟātu eṉpatūum, viṉimutaṟpeyar varuvaḻip piṉ niṉṟa eccattōṭu eḻuvāyāy 20iyikiṉṟatō? piṟa vēṟṟumiyāy iyikiṉṟatō? eṉṉum vikaṟpamum, īṇṭu viṉimutaṟ peyarē aṉṟip piṟa peyarōṭu muṭipu ātaṟkuk kāraṇam viṉiyē ceyvatu eṉa viṉi ilakkaṇam kūṟiyavaḻi viṉiccoṟkuṟippāyp piṟa peyarum pukku amiyātu eṉpatūum, av eṭṭaṉuṭ kaṭikkaṇ iraṇṭum oḻiya maṟṟi āṟum īṇṭu ōtappaṭṭaṉa eṉpatūum aṟintu koḷka.
yāṉ āṭi olikkum illam ; āṭi olitta kūli eṉṟāṟ pōlvaṉa muṭiyumāṟu eṉṉi eṉiṉ, avviṉi ilakkaṇattuḷ iṉṉataṟku itu payaṉ eṉṉum av21iraṇ ṭaṉ peyarvaki ākaliṉ taṉṉiṉamuṭittal eṉpataṉāṉ koḷḷappaṭum eṉpatu.
maṟṟinnōy tīrum maruntaruḷāy oṇṭo22ṭī eṉavum, niṉmukaṅ kāṇum 23maruntiṉēṉ eṉavum 24varuvaṉavum āmāṟu eṉṉi eṉiṉ, tīrum maruntu eṉpatu tīrutaṟkuk kāraṇamākiya 25maruntu eṉṟavāṟu. 
26kāṇum maruntu 27 eṉṟatu kāṇṭal kāraṇamāka ataṉ kāriyamākap piṟanta maruntu 28 ātal taṉmiyatu eṉṟavāṟu.
ivvāṟu varum kāraṇappeyarum kāriyappeyarumāy varuvaṉa av ilakkaṇam eṭṭaṉuḷḷum aṭaṅkāmiyiṉ oṉṟeṉa muṭittal eṉpataṉāṟ koḷḷappaṭum.
yāṉ cellum ūr, yāṉ pōnta ūr eṉpaṉa nilap peyaruḷ 29aṭakkuka. 30ūr kaḷiṟu mititta nīr eṉavum, nūlākkaliṅkam eṉavum, eḷḷāṭṭiṉa eṇṇey eṉavum, uṇṭa eccil eṉavum varuvaṉa ceyappaṭuporuḷiṉ vikaṟpamākki ataṉuḷ aṭakkik 31koḷka. eṉa ivvāṟu 32varum piṟapeyar vikaṟpaṅkaḷum aṟintu aṭakkik koḷka.
aviyāvaṉa:- tēroṭum puṟam, kuṇṭucuṉip pūtta vaṇṭu, paṭukaṇṇi eṉṟāṟ pōlvaṉa.
iṉi, ceyyum eṉpatu ceyyāniṟkum eṉavum, ceyta eṉpatu ceyyāniṉṟa eṉavum varum vāypāṭu vēṟṟumiyum taṉṉiṉa muṭittal eṉpataṉāṟkoḷka.
iṉic ceyta eṉpataṉ kuṟippāy iṉṉa aṉṉa eṉṉa eṉavum kariya, ceyya eṉavum varum iviyum 23avaṟṟāṟ  koḷka. 	(36)

231.	avaṟṟoṭu varuvaḻic ceyyumeṉ kiḷavi

eṉpatu eṉnutaliṟṟō eṉiṉ, ceyyum eṉpataṟku 2iṉṉum muṭipu vēṟṟumi kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
itaṉ poruḷ:- mēṟkūṟiya aṟuvakip peyarōṭu muṭintu varum iṭattuc ceyyum eṉṉum colmuṉ pallōr paṭarkki eṉṟataṟkaṇ variyappaṭṭa mūṉṟu kūṟṟiṉ kaṇṇum urimi uṭittu eṉṟavāṟu.
mūṉṟu kūṟṟāvaṉa:- pallōr paṭarkkiyum muṉṉiliyum taṉmiyum.
aviyāvaṉa:- avar uṇṇum illam, avar uṇṇum cōṟu, avar ōtum kāli, avar eṟiyum kal, uṇṇum avar vantār, avar uṇṇum ūṇ - ivi pallōr paṭarkki.
nī uṇṇum illam, nīyir uṇṇum illam, nī uṇṇum cōṟu, nīyir uṇṇum cōṟu, nī ōtum kāli, nīyir ōtum kāli, nī eṟiyum kal, nīyir eṟiyum kal, uṇṇum nī vantāy, uṇṇu nīyir vantīr, 3nī uṇṇum ūṇ, nīyir uṇṇum ūṇ  ivi muṉṉili.
4nāṉ uṇṇum illam, yām uṇṇum illam, nām uṇṇum illam ; yāṉ uṇṇum 5cōṟu, nām uṇṇum cōṟu, yām uṇṇum cōṟu, 6yāṉ uṇṇum kāli, nām uṇṇum kāli, yām uṇṇum kāli; yāṉ eṟiyum 7kal, nām eṟiyum kal, yām eṟiyum kal ; uṇṇum yāṉ 8vantēṉ, uṇṇum nām vantōm, uṇṇum yām vantōm, yāṉ uṇṇum ūṇ, nām uṇṇum ūṇ, yām uṇṇum ūṇ,   ivi taṉmi.
yāṉum nīyum uṇṇum illam, yāṉum avaṉum uṇṇum illam, yāṉum nīyum avaṉum uṇṇum illam eṉṟāṟpōlavarum vāypāṭum oṭṭikkoḷka.
itaṉāṟ colliyatu, ceyyum eṉpataṟku muṟṟāyavaḻi vilakkiya iṭaṅkaḷ eccamāyavaḻi varum eṉa 10iṟantatu kāttatāyiṟṟu.	(37)

232.	peyareñcu kiḷaviyum viṉieñcu kiḷaviyum
	etirmaṟuttu moḻiyiṉum poruṇili tiriyā.
eṉpatu eṉnutaliṟṟō eṉiṉ, peyareccamum viṉieccamum taṉittaṉi muṭiyumāṟu kūṟiviṭṭu, iṉi av iraṇṭaṟkum uṭaṉeytuvatu ōr ilakkaṇam uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
itaṉ poruḷ:- peyar eccamākiya collum, viṉiyeccamākiya collum toḻiliṉi etirmaṟuttuc coṉṉa 1iṭattum avac collātaṟ poruṇmi 2niliyiṉi vēṟupaṭā eṉṟavāṟu.
(u-m.) 
uṇṇum cāttaṉ eṉpatu uṇṇāc cāttaṉ eṉa varum. ceyta eṉpataṟku ituvē maṟi. kariya cāttaṟkuc ceyya cāttaṉ eṉavum, nalla cāttaṟkup pollāc cāttaṉ eṉavum, 3tīya cāttaṉ eṉavum varum. 4kuṟippu maṟi vikaṟpamum aṟika. 
immaṟikkaṇ uṇṇāc cāttaṉ eṉa ākāra īṟāyē niṟkum. ivi peyareccam.
iṉi, viṉiyeccam; uṇṭuvantāṉ eṉpatu, uṇṇātu vantāṉ eṉavarum. 
ivveccam cōṟuṇṭāyiruntatu eṉavum, cōṟā vatāyiruntatu eṉavum oru col aṭuttapōtu cōṟiṉṟi eṉavum vēṟu kuṟippu vāypāṭṭatām eṉak koḷka. 
ceyyū, ceypu eṉpaṉavaṟṟiṟkum ituvē maṟi.
iṉic ceyteṉa eṉpatu mutalviṉiyoṭu muṭintavaḻi im maṟiyāṉē varum. maḻi peyteṉa maraṅ kuḻittatu eṉap piṟaviṉiyāya vaḻic ceytu eṉ eccattu etirmaṟiyē taṉakku maṟiyāy, maḻi peyyāmal maram kuḻiyātāyiṟṟu eṉa varum.
iṉic ceyyiyar ceyyiya eṉpaṉa iraṇṭaṟku maṟipaṭu vaḻit taṉviṉi piṟaviṉi eṉṉum iruvaḻiyum ceya eṉ eccattiṉ maṟiyāṉē muṭiyum. maḻi peyyāmal eḻuntatu; maḻipeyyāmaṉ maraṅ kuḻiyātāyiṟṟu. 
iṉic ceyyiya eṉpataṟkum ivvāṟē koḷka.
ceyiṉ eṉpataṟkuc coṟṟaṉṉāṉ maṟiyiṉṟi 5maḻi peyyāviṭiṉ aṟam peṟātu; maḻipeyyāviṭiṉ maraṅ kuḻiyātu eṉap piṟa collāṉē maṟiyāy varumpōlum.
uṇṇa 6eṉpataṟku uṇṇāmal eṉavum, uṇṇāmi eṉavum, allum aiyum eṉa iru īṟṟatām. uṇṇāmē eṉpatō eṉiṉ, atuvum marūu eṉka. iṉip periya ōtiṉum eṉpataṟkuc ciṟiya ōtiṉum eṉavarum. kuṟippu maṟi vikaṟpamum aṟika. ceyaṟku eṉpataṟku uṇaṟku vantāṉ, uṇṇāmal vantāṉ eṉa itaṉ maṟiyē maṟi eṉak koḷka. uṇṇātu  oḻivāṉ eṉap piṟa vāypāṭṭāyum varum.
iṉip piṉ eṉpatu uṇṇāta piṉ eṉa varum. muṉ eṉpatu uṇṇāta muṉ eṉavarum. 7kāl ; uṇṇākkāl  eṉavarum. 8kaṭi ; uṇṇākkaṭi eṉavum, 9vaḻi uṇṇāta vaḻi eṉa varum. 10iṭattu uṇṇāta 11iṭattu eṉa varum.
pāṉ, pākku eṉṟāṟ pōlvaṉa uṇṇātoḻivāṉ eṉṟāṟ 12pōla 13vēṟu pāṭṭavāy varum. maṟṟu maṟi vikaṟpam uḷḷaṉa aṟintukoḷka.
itaṉāl colliyatu; ivvāṟu peyareccamum viṉi yeccamum etirmaṟuttuvarum eṉpatu pōtarak kūṟiṉamiyiṉ ataṉuḷ aṭaṅkiṟṟu eṉpatu. ta............. vaḻiyum peyareccam eṉap14paṭutalum 15peyarōṭum viṉiyōṭum muṭitalumuṭiya eṉṟāyiṟṟu.
16aḵtēl muṟṟuccol maṟutta vaḻiyum 17muṟṟu eṉpatu eṟṟāṟ peṟutum eṉiṉ, vēṟṟumi ilakkaṇattuḷ etirmaṟuttu moḻiyiṉum eṉpataṉuḷ avvēṟṟumiyiṉi etirmaṟuttu varum eṉpatu pōtarak kūṟiṉamiyiṉ ataṉuḷ 18aṭaṅkiṟṟu eṉpatu. 
maṟṟu iveccamum ataṉpāl aṭaṅkātō eṉiṉ, eṭuttu 19ottu ilvaḻiyatu ilēcum uttiyum eṉka.
aḵṟiṇi 20viṉiyuḷ a ā eṉṟu ākāra īṟṟi ōtiṉamiyāṉum maṟiyum ām eṉpatu peṟutum. muṟṟuccol etirmaṟuttavaḻi uṇṭāṉ eṉpataṟku uṇṇāṉ eṉa īṟu vēṟupaṭātu varutalāṉum, uṇṇumuṉ eṉpataṟku uṇṇāta muṉ eṉṟum, uṇṇa eṉpataṟku uṇṇāmal eṉṟum eccaṅkaḷ īṟu vēṟupaṭṭu varutalāṉum, avi iyalpu eṉṟu, vikāram 21uṭimiyiṉ iveccaṅkaḷi eṭuttu ōtiṉāṉ eṉṟalum oṉṟu. muṟṟuc 22col maṟivikaṟpam ellām avīṟṟuḷḷē kāṭṭiṉavāṟu kaṇṭukoḷka.
iṉic ceyyum eṉpatu muṟṟāyavaḻi ataṉ etirmaṟi āṇṭuk kūṟum uyartiṇi 23aḵṟiṇip24pālmēl, viṉi yāy 25uṇṇum avaṉ eṉpataṟku uṇṇā avaṉ eṉavum, uṇṇum atu eṉpataṟku uṇṇātatu eṉavum varum eṉṟu aṟika.
maṟṟu iv viravuviṉi muṟṟāy 26avaṟṟil taṉakku ēṟṟa viṉiyillaṉa āṇṭu uṇarttum uyartiṇi aḵṟiṇi viṉiyāṉ maṟipaṭu27māṟu aṟintu koḷka.	(38)

233.	tattam eccamoṭu civaṇum kuṟippiṉ
	eccol 1āyiṉum iṭinili variyār.
eṉpatu eṉnutaliṟṟō 2eṉiṉ, ituvum aveccaṅ kaḷiṭi nikaḻum muṭipu vēṟṟumi uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
itaṉ poruḷ:- muṉkūṟiya peyareccamum viṉi yeccamum tattam eccamākiya peyaroṭum viṉiyoṭum poruḷ iyiyum karuttiṉiyuṭiya peyar mutaliya evvakic collāyiṉum am muṭitaṟku iṭiniṟṟali nīkkār āciriyar; eṉavē koḷvar eṉṟavāṟu.
variyār eṉṟamiyiṉ ituvum oru marapu vaḻuvamiti nīrmittu eṉpatu pōntatu. civaṇuṅ kuṟippu eṉṟamiyiṟ civaṇāk 3kuṟippiṉa variyappaṭum eṉpatu.
(u-m.) 
aṭum cenneṟ cōṟu;  aṭṭacenneṟ cōṟu eṉa varum. ivi peyareccam.
“uppiṉṟu puṟki yuṇkamā, koṟki 4yōṉē” eṉpatu viṉiyeccam.
civaṇāk kuṟippiṉatu ‘vallameṟinta nalliḷaṅkōcar’
‘tanti mallal yāṉip peruvaḻuti’ eṉpatu.

234.	avaṟṟuḷ,
	ceyyum eṉṉum peyareñcu kiḷavikku
	meyyoṭum keṭumē īṟṟumici ukaram
	avviṭaṉ aṟital 1eṉmaṉār pulavar.
eṉpatu eṉnutaliṟṟō eṉiṉ, 2iveccaṅkaḷuḷ ceyyum eṉṉum peyareccattiṟku 3īṟu vēṟupāṭu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
itaṉ poruḷ:- mēṟ4kūṟiya peyareccaṅkaḷuḷ5 ceyyum eṉṉum peya reccattiṟku īṟṟiṉ6mēl niṉṟa ukaram taṉṉāṟ paṟṟappaṭṭa meyyoṭum keṭṭu muṭiyum. avvāṟu keṭum iṭaṅkaḷi aṟika eṉṟu colluvar pulavar eṉṟavāṟu.
(u-m.) 
7vām 8puravi vaḻuti, yāṉ 9pōm 10puḻi eṉavarum.
iṉi uruvu tiri eṉṟāṟ 11pōla īṟu tāṉ keṭuvaṉavum, uḷavāl eṉiṉ atu viṉittoki eṉa maṟukka.
cāralnāṭa eṉ12ṟōḻiyuṅ kaluḻmē eṉa avukaram tāṉ ēṟiyameyoḻiyak keṭṭaviṭam uḷavāl eṉiṉ av iṭaṉaṟital eṉṟa mikivāypāṭṭāṉ orōvaḻi mey oḻiyak keṭutalum uṇṭu eṉpatu koḷḷappaṭum.
iṉic ceyyum eṉṉum peyar eñcukiḷavikku eṉṟāṉ aṉṟē; maṟṟatu, 13ampalūru mavaṉoṭu moḻimē eṉa muṟṟāya 14vaḻiyum aṟṟāy vantatāl eṉiṉ ataṉiyum ivilēciṉāṟ kōṭalum oṉṟu. itaṉiyum utāraṇamākak 15kūṟiya uriyiṟ kōṭalum oṉṟu.
iṉi vāvum puravi eṉa ukaram keṭātu varutaṟku viti yātu eṉiṉ meyyoṭum keṭum eṉṟa ummi etirmaṟi ākalāṉ meyyoṭum keṭātu niṟṟal perumpāṉmi 16eṉpa tūum peṟappaṭum. ēṟpuḻik kōṭal eṉpataṉāl āṭunākam eṉṟāṟpōlvuḻik keṭāmiyum koḷka.	(40)

235.	ceyte ṉeccat tiṟanta kālam
	eytiṭa ṉuṭittē vārāk kālam.
eṉpatu eṉnutaliṟṟō eṉiṉ, cey eṉṉum viṉi yeccattuk kālamayakkam uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
itaṉ poruḷ:- iṟanta kālattiuṭiya ceytu eṉṉum viṉiyeccam taṉ iṟanta kālattiṉi nōkka vārātavākiya iyalpiṉiyuṭiya etirkālattiṉiyum nikaḻkālattiṉiyum poruntum kūṟṟiṉiyuṭiyatu eṉṟavāṟu.
iṟantakālattuc ceytu eṉ eccam eṉa moḻi māṟṟik koḷka. 1kiṭantavāṟum vārākkālam eṉa nikaḻkālamum aṭaṅkiṟṟu.
(u-m.) 
uḻutu varuvāṉ cāttaṉ eṉpatu. itu, varuvāṉ eṉṉum etirkāla viṉi koṇṭamiyāṉ muṉ uḻutu eṉa niṉṟa iṟanta kālam uḻuvatu mēl eṉa etirkālattu āyiṟṟu.
koṭiāṭit tōṉṟum eṉpatu. attōṟṟamum 2āṭṭamum uṭaṉ nikaḻtalāṉ nikaḻkālam āyiṟṟu.
oṉṟeṉa muṭittal eṉpataṉāṟ ceyyū ceppu eṉpa ṉavaṟṟiṟkum ivvāṟē mayakkam 3koḷka. 
4maṟṟum viṉiyecca vāypāṭuḷḷum kālam mayaṅkuvaṉa uḷavēl aviyum ivvāṟṟāṉē koḷka.  iccūttiram kālavaḻuvamiti. itu oru coṉmayakkam.	(41)

236.	muṉṉilik kālamum tōṉṟum iyaṟki
	1emmuṟic collum nikaḻum kālattu
	meynnilip potuccoṟ kiḷattal vēṇṭum.
eṉpatu eṉnutaliṟṟō eṉiṉ, ellā viṉic coṟkaḷum poruḷatu uṇmi iyaṟki kūṟumvaḻic ceyyum eṉṉum viṉiccolloṭu kālam mayaṅkumāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
itaṉ poruḷ:- mūṉṟu vakippaṭṭa nilimiyiṉi uṭiya kālattum tōṉṟum iyalpiṉiyuṭiya evvakip paṭṭa poruḷiyum; nikaḻkālattiṉit taṉakkuk kālamāka uṭiya, 2piṟakālattiṉiyum 3potiyum poruḷ nilimi yiṉi uṭiya ceyyum eṉṉum colliṉāṟ collutali virumpum āciriyar eṉṟavāṟu.
(u-m.) 
mali niṟkum, yāṟoḻukum ; tīccuṭum eṉṉum ivi. tī ettaṉmiyatu eṉṟāl ataṉ taṉmi ekkālattum uḷatātali4ṉ paṇṭu cuṭṭatu iṉṟuñ cuṭukiṉṟatu mēlum cuṭuvatu eṉa mūṉṟu kālattāṉum kūṟavēṇṭuvataṉi nikaḻkālattāṟ colla avi ellām kūṟiyavāṟu āyiṟṟu eṉak koḷka.
“munnilikkālamum tōṉṟum iyaṟki 5emmuṟic col” eṉṟamiyāṉ oru 6poruḷ orukālat toḻil aṉṟi ekkālattum nikaḻum toḻil iyalpu kūṟum vaḻiyatu immayakkam eṉak koḷka.
col 7eṉṟataṟkup peyarp poruḷi eṉka. iṉic col eṉiṉum paṭum. 
8meynnili eṉṟataṉāṟ 9piṟa nikaḻkālac collōṭu ovvātu mūṉṟu kālattiyum potiyu nilimiyatu ic ceyyum eṉṉum col eṉpatu peṟappaṭṭatu. ituvum kālamayakka vamiti.
īṇṭu mayaṅkiyatu eccollō eṉiṉ, neruppuccuṭum eṉṟavaḻi cuṭṭatu cuṭāniṉṟatu cuṭuvatu eṉṟu mūṉṟukālac collum ceyyum eṉpataṉāṟ collappaṭutaliṉ, avi mayaṅkiṉa eṉappaṭum. ic cuṭum eṉṟa coṟṟāṉum taṉ nikaḻ 10kālattatāy 11niṟṟaliyiṭṭu oru collutaṟkaṇṇē mūṉṟu kālamum paṭa niṟṟaliṉ atuvum mayaṅkiṟṟu eṉappaṭum. iḵtu oru coṉmayakkam.	(42)

	ciṟappat tōṉṟum mayaṅkumoḻik kiḷavi.
eṉpatu eṉnutaliṟṟō eṉiṉ, mēṟkūṟiya kāla 1mayakkam pōla oru poruṇmiyik kuṟiyātu pala poruṭkaṇṇum mayaṅkuvaṉavaṟṟuḷ, iṟantakālam etirkālattōṭu mayaṅku māṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
itaṉ poruḷ:- iṟappum etirvum eṉṟu collappaṭṭa avirukālamum mayaṅkum collākiya coṟkaḷiṭattu mayakkamāyt tōṉṟum eṉṟavāṟu.
(u-m.) 
yām paṇṭuviḷiyāṭuvatu ikkā. yām paṇṭu cūtu poruvatu ikkaḻakam eṉavarum.	(49)
aṭikkuṟippukaḷ
243-1 	ii-ei-eii “mayakkam” ovvoru poruṇmiyi.
243-2 	e “tōṟṟum”
244.	ēṉik kālamum mayaṅkutal variyār.
eṉpatu eṉnutaliṟṟō eṉiṉ, 1ituvum poruṭkaṇ 2iṟantataṉōṭu nikaḻvatu mayaṅkumāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
itaṉ poruḷ:- oḻinta nikaḻkālam iṟantataṉōṭu mayaṅkutali nīkkār āciriyar eṉṟavāṟu.
(u-m.) 
yām paṇṭuviḷiyāṭum kā eṉavarum.
iṉi, oṉṟeṉa muṭittal eṉpataṉāl iṟantataṉōṭu etirvatum nikaḻvatum mayaṅkiyavāṟu pōla nikaḻvataṉōṭu iṟantatuvum etirvum, etirvataṉōṭu iṟantatum nikaḻvatum mayaṅkumāṟu koḷka.
yām iṉṟu viḷiyāṭāniṉṟatu ikkā eṉpuḻi 3viḷiyāṭṭiṟku ikkā eṉavum kūṟutal nikaḻkāla mayakkam.
yām nāḷi viḷiyāṭuvatu ikkā eṉpuḻi viḷiyāṭiṟṟu eṉavum, viḷiyāṭā niṉṟatu eṉavum kūṟutal etirkāla mayakkam.
ivi itupoḻuti 4vaḻakkiṉuḷ ēlā eṉiṉum ivvāṟu mayaṅkutaṟku ilakkaṇam uṇmiyāṉum ka - ca - ta - pa mutaliya eṉpuḻic collumuṟi eṉpataṉic colliya muṟimi eṉṟamiyāṉum amiyum eṉak koḷka.	(50)

244-1 	ei “ituvum apporuṭkaṇ”
2 	e “ciṟantataṉōṭu”
3 	i-eiii “viḷiyāṭṭiṟki . . . ṟikkā”
	e “viḷiyāṭṭi . . . ṟikkā”

	e “viṉiyiyal muṟṟum”
āṟāvatu -  viṉiyiyaṉ muṟṟum.


iṭiyiyal
iṭiccoṟkaḷiṉ ilakkaṇamuṇarttiṉamiyāl iṭiyiyaleṉṉum peyarttāyiṟṟu. peyariyum viṉiyiyum cārntu tōṟṟutaliṉ avaṟṟiṉpiṉ kūṟappaṭṭatu moḻikku muṉṉum piṉṉum varumāyiṉum perumpāṉmiyum iṭivarutaliṉ iṭiccollāyiṟṟu eṉpar cēṉāvariyar. iṭiccollāvatu peyarum viṉiyumpōlat taṉittaṉiyē poruḷuṇara uccarikkappaṭātu peyar viṉikaḷic cārntu pulappaṭumeṉṟum peyarum viṉiyum iṭamākaniṉṟu poruḷuṇarttaliṉ iṭiccollāyiṟṟeṉṟum kūṟuvar teyvacciliyār. poruḷiyum poruḷatu puṭi peyarcciyiyun tammālaṉṟit tattaṅkuṟippāluṇarttuñ coṟkaḷ peyarccol viṉiccoṟkaḷumākātu avaṟṟiṉ vēṟumākātu iṭinikaraṉavāy niṟṟaliṉ iṭiccolleṉap paṭṭaṉa eṉpar civañāṉa muṉivar. ivviyaliluḷḷa cūttiraṅkaḷ 48. ivaṟṟi 47-ākak koḷvar teyvacciliyār.
iṭiccolleṉṟu collappaṭuvaṉa peyarum viṉiyum uṇarttum poruḷic cārntuniṉṟu avaṟṟiyē veḷippaṭuttu niṟṟalallatu tamakkeṉa vēṟu poruḷillātaṉa eṉpar āciriyar. eṉavē avi poruḷuṇarttumvaḻip peyarp poruṇmi yuṇarttiyum viṉipporuṇmi yuṇarttiyum varuvaṉa eṉpatu peṟappaṭum.
oru collōṭu oru col puṇarntiyalum vaḻi apporuḷ nilikku utaviyāki varuvaṉavum, viṉic coṟkaḷi muṭikkumiṭattu accollakattuk kālaṅkāṭṭum uṟuppu mutaliyaṉavāy niṟpaṉavum, vēṟṟumiyurupukaḷāy varuvaṉavum, tamakkeṉap poruḷiṉṟic cārttic collappaṭum acinilikaḷāy niṟpaṉavum, ici niṟikka varuvaṉavum, tattaṅkuṟippāṟ poruḷuṇarttuvaṉavum. oppumiyuṇarttum uvamavurupukaḷāy varuvaṉavum eṉa iṭiccoṟkaḷ eḻuvakippaṭumeṉpar tolkāppiyar. avaṟṟuḷ ‘puṇariyal niliyiṭip poruḷ nilikkutavuna’ eṉṟatu alvaḻip poruḷukku uriyaṉa ivi vēṟṟumip poruḷukku uriyaṉa iviyeṉa eḷitil uṇarntu koḷḷutaṟku aṟikuṟiyākiya iṉ, vaṟṟu, mutaliya cāriyikaḷi ivi eḻuttatikārattiṟ collappaṭṭaṉa.
‘viṉi ceyal maruṅkiṟ kālamoṭu varuna’ eṉṟatu viṉiccol muṭivupeṟumiṭattuk kālaṅkāṭṭiyum pālkāṭṭiyum viṉiccollakattu uṟuppāy niṟpaṉavaṟṟi. ivi viṉiyiyaluṭ kūṟappaṭṭaṉa vēṟṟumip poruḷiṭattu urupāy varum ai, oṭu, ku, iṉ, atu, kaṇ eṉavarum vēṟṟumiyurupukaḷ vēṟṟumiyiyaliṟ kūṟappaṭṭaṉa. aciniliyum iciniṟiyum tattaṅ kuṟippiṟ poruḷ ceyvaṉavum ākiya mūvakiyiṭic coṟkaḷum ivviyaliṉ kaṇṇē uṇarttappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. oppumiyuṇarttum iṭic coṟkaḷākiya aṉṉa, āṅku mutaliya uvama urupukaḷ poruḷatikārattu  uvama iyalil viritturikkappaṭum. ivviṭiccoṟkaḷ tammāṟ cārappaṭum coṟku muṉṉum piṉṉum varutalum tammīṟutiritalum piṟitōriṭic col tammuṉvantu cārappeṟutalum ākiya iyalpiṉavām.
ivviyaliṉkaṇ uṇarttappaṭum acinili, iciniṟi tattaṅkuṟippiṟ poruḷceyvaṉa eṉṉum mūvakiyiṭic coṟkaḷuḷ poruḷpuṇar iṭiccollākiya tattaṅkuṟippiṟ poruḷ ceyvaṉavaṟṟi mutaṟkaṇṇum, poruḷpuṇarā iṭiccoṟkaḷākiya acinili iciniṟikaḷi ataṉ piṉṉarum uṇarttukiṉṟār. tattaṅkuṟippiṟ poruḷceyyum iṭic coṟkaḷuḷ palaporuḷ kuṟitta iṭiccoṟkaḷi 4-mutal 12-variyuḷḷa cūttiraṅkaḷilum, oruporuḷ kuṟitta iṭiccoṟkaḷi 13-mutal 21-variyuḷḷa cūttiraṅkaḷilum āciriyar eṭuttōtukiṉṟār. avar eṭuttōtiya iṭiccoṟkaḷuḷ el eṉṉum col ilaṅkutal eṉṉum oru poruḷ kuṟitta iṭiccollākum. “elleṉpatu uriccol nīrmittāyiṉum āciriyar iṭiccollāka otiṉamiyāṉ iṭiccol leṉṟu kōṭum” eṉṟār cēṉāvariyar. ‘uriccol kuṟic collāki niṟkum, itu kuṟiyiṉṟi niṟṟaliṉ iṭic collāyiṟṟu’ eṉṟār teyvacciliyār. aciniliyākavum iciniṟiyākavum varum iṭiccoṟkaḷi 22-mutal 32-variyuḷḷa cūttiraṅkaḷāl eṭutturittār. uyireḻuttukkaḷuḷ oḷakāramallāta neṭilkaḷ āṟum iraṭṭittum aḷapeṭuttum taṉittum iṭiccoṟkaḷāy niṉṟu ōciyālum kuṟippālum poruḷuṇarttum muṟiyiṉi 38-ām cūttirattilum, naṉṟē, aṉṟē, antō, aṉṉō eṉpavaṟṟiṉ iṟuti niṉṟa ēkāramum ōkāramum kuṟippāṟ poruḷuṇarttu muṟiyiṉi 34-ām cūttirattilum, mēṟkūṟiya iṭic colliṉkaṇ ilakkaṇa vēṟupāṭukaḷiyellām 35-mutal 46-variyuḷḷa cūttiraṅ kaḷilum āciriyar virittuk kūṟiyuḷḷār.
mēṟkūṟappaṭṭa iṭiccoṟkaḷil iccollukku itu poruḷ eṉa nilipeṟac collappaṭṭaṉavāyiṉum accoṟkaḷiṉ muṉṉum piṉṉum niṉṟa viṉiyōṭum peyarōṭum iyittu nōkka accoṟkaḷ muṟkūṟiyavāṟaṉṟi vēṟu poruḷavāyum aciniliyāyum tirintu vēṟupaṭiṉum avaṟṟiṉ poruḷ niliyi ārāyntuṇartal vēṇṭu meṉavum, iṅkeṭutturitta iṭiccoṟkaḷēyaṉṟi ivipōlvaṉa piṟa variṉum avaṟṟiyum iṅkuc colliya vaṟṟiṉ ilakkaṇattāl uṇarntu vakippaṭuttik koḷḷutal vēṇṭumeṉavum ivviyaliṟutiyiluḷḷa puṟaṉaṭic cūttiraṅkaḷāl āciriyar aṟivuṟuttukiṉṟār. itaṉāl āciriyar kālattu vaḻaṅkiya tamiḻccoṟkaḷiṉ parappum iruvaki vaḻakkiṉum coṟkaḷ poruḷuṇarttum neṟiyiṉ virivum iṉitu pulaṉātal kāṇalām.
- ka. veḷḷivāraṇaṉār 
nūlvarici 10, pak. 214-216


1ēḻāvatu
iṭiccelliyal
245.	iṭi eṉappaṭupa peyaroṭum viṉiyoṭum
	naṭipeṟ ṟiyalun tamakkiyal pilavē.
eṉpatu eṉ nutaliṟṟō eṉiṉ, 2ivvōttu eṉṉa peyarttō eṉiṉ:- iṭiccolliṉ ilakkaṇam uṇarttutalāṉ iṭiccollōttu eṉṉum peyarttu. iṭiccol eṉṉum 3poruṇmi eṉṉi eṉiṉ, peyar viṉikaḷ uṇarttum poruṭkut 4tāṉ iṭi niṟṟalāṉ iṭic col āyiṟṟu. iccūttiram eṉnutaliṟṟō eṉiṉ, iṭiccoṟku ellām potuvāyatōr ilakkaṇam uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
itaṉ poruḷ:- iṭiccol eṉṟu collappaṭuvaṉa peyarccollōṭum viṉic collōṭum vaḻakkup peṟṟu naṭakkum. avvāṟu avaṟṟōṭu naṭattal allatu tāmāka naṭakkum 5iyalpila eṉṟavāṟu.
(u-m.) 
atu maṉ, varukatillamma eṉavarum.
tamakkiyalpila eṉṟataṉāṉ peyaroṭum viṉi yoṭum aviṭiccoṟkaḷ varuvaḻic 6coṟpuṟattuvaḻi varutalum, accolluḷ vaḻivarutalum eṉa iruvakittu eṉpatu peṟappaṭṭatu.
(u-m.) 
varukatil, uṇṭāṉ eṉavarum: ivi viṉi. 
atu maṉ 7maṟṟiyatu ivi peyar.
maṟṟu, ic cūttirattiṉ poruṇmiyum ilēciṉ poruṇmiyum iṭiccol kiḷaviyum uriccol kiḷaviyum eṉṟa cūttirattuḷḷum ataṉ ilēciṉuḷḷum aṭaṅkumāl eṉiṉ, atu niraṉiṟi vāypāṭṭatākalāṉ 8peyarōṭu iṭivarum eṉṟum, viṉiyōṭu urivarum eṉṟum koḷḷak kiṭantamiyiṉ iṭiyum iraṇṭōṭu varum 9eṉṟaṟkuk kūṟiṉāṉ eṉpatu. iṉi avilēcu niraṉiṟic cūttirattatākalāṟ puṟattu vaḻi varutal iṭiccoṟkākavum, uḷvaḻi varutal uriccoṟkākavum koḷḷak kiṭakkum eṉpatu karuti 10īṇṭum iruvakiyāṉum iṭivarum eṉpataṟku 11ilēcu kūṟiṉāṉ pōlum.
