itaṟku eḻuttatikārattil
illeṉ kiḷavi iṉmi ceppiṉ
valleḻuttu mikutalum ai iṭi varutalum
iyaṟki ātalum ākāram varutalum
koḷattaku marapiṉ ākiṭaṉ uṭittē	(372)
eṉṟu cūttiramum ceytuḷḷār. inta aṭippaṭiyil tolkāppiya vaḻakkukaḷiyum cūttirattiyum iṭiccerukal eṉṟu colla muṭiyumā?
atē camayattil tamiḻ marapukku māṟupaṭṭatu eṉṟu karuti taṟcārpu nōkkil, cilavaṟṟi iṭiccerukal eṉṟu muṉṉavarkaḷ kūṟiyataṟku maṟuppākavum, iṭiccerukalukkuk koḷki aḷavil vaḻikāṭṭiyākavum, muraṇ, piṟkāla vaḻakku eṉpavikaḷik koḷḷalām. iruntālum ovvoru vaḻakkiyum taṉittaṉiyē paricīlittē muṭivu ceyya vēṇṭum.
iṅkut tolkāppiya mūlapāṭam paṟṟiya ceytiyik kavaṉattil koḷḷavēṇṭum. atāvatu: iḷampūraṇar 11-ām nūṟṟāṇṭil tolkāppiyam muḻumikkum uri eḻutiyatāl avarukkup piṟaku iṭiccerukal ēṟpaṭa vāyppukaḷ illi. pāṭapētam, oṉṟākkal, palavākkal, pāṭappaṭippu, pāṭaviḷakkam pōṉṟavikaḷil maṭṭumē mūlappāṭa piracciṉi eḻuntaṉa; iruntum āṅkāṅkē, orucilar iṭiccerukuvataṟku muyaṉṟu vantālum, atu veṟṟi peṟavilli; atu tolkāppiyamāka eṭuttukkoḷḷappaṭavilli. eṉavē iḷampūraṇar kālattiṟku muṉṉarē iṭiccerukal naṭipeṟṟirukka vēṇṭum. anta niliyil te.po.mī. iṭiccerukali yār ceytatu eṉṟu iṉam kāṇa muyaṟci ceytuḷḷār.
iṭikkālattil uriyāciriyarum piṟarum pativu ceytuḷḷa katikaḷ aṭippaṭiyil iṭiccerukalic ceytatu akattiyar māṇavarākak karutappaṭum tolkāppiyar eṉṟum kūṟiyuḷḷār. akattiyar iṭippaṭṭa pallavar kālattavarāka iruntirukkalām eṉṟum (pa.1) kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār, One has to assume therefore that there was an earlier Tolkappiyar and a later Tolkappiyar alone was the disciple of Agattiyar. writing the first chapter of Pannirupatalam. Evidently, Tolkappiyam of the older ageï not only with reference to the theory of puram poetry but also with reference to the other aspects of grammar and poetics. An author coming laterï naturally takes note of the developments in literary and colloqquial usages. But when both the authors are identified ooneï the readers are naturally perple4xed to find inconsistencies in hte work which is really a combination of a rather confusion of the works of the earlier and later authors. (1974. P. 3).
(muṟkālat tolkāppiyar, piṟkālattolkāppiyar eṉṟu iruvar iruntatāka ūkikkavēṇṭiyuḷḷatu. piṉṉarē akattiyariṉ māṇavar; paṉṉirupaṭalattiṉ mutal atikāratti eḻutiyavar. muṟkāla tolkāppiyam piṟkāla tolkāppiyat tiliruntu puṟattiṇi ilakkaṇattil maṭṭum allāmal, ilakkaṇattiṉ piṟappakutiyilum ceyyuḷiyalilum māṟupaṭukiṟatu. piṟkālattil vanta oru āciriyarukku ilakkiyattilum pēccumoḻiyilum naṭanta māṟṟaṅkaḷ teriyum. iraṇṭu āciriyarkaḷiyum oruvarākak koḷḷumpōtutāṉ nūliṉ muraṇ pulappaṭṭu, vācakarukkuk kuḻappam ēṟpaṭukiṟatu.)
ataṟku ātāramākat tamiḻil pala āciriyarkaḷ (utāraṇam nakkīrar, kallāṭar) oru ciṟappup peyarip peṟṟiruppatiyum cuṭṭikkāṭṭiyuḷḷār. itaṉ uḷkiṭi eṉṉa? tolkāppiyattil iṭiccerukali ottukkoṇṭu atu piṟkāla tolkāppiyarāl eḻutappaṭṭirukka vēṇṭum eṉṟu colvatu iṭikkālattil tamiḻ ulakil vaḻaṅkiya katikaḷukku oru varalāṟṟu aṅkīkāram koṭuppatākavum, iṭiccerukalukkum oru varalāṟu kaṟpippatākavum amintuḷḷatu. itu ōr ūkamē. ēṉeṉṟāl, iṉṟu akattiyar katiyum, tolkāppiyar paṟṟiya puṉivum, iṭikkāla aṟiñar camūkattiṉ moḻi uṇarviṉ veḷippāṭu, avi moḻit toṉmaṅkaḷ eṉṟu oru purital iṉṟu ēṟkappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
eppaṭiyāṉālum paḻiya tolkāppiyattiyum, putiya tolkāppiyattiyum pirittaṟiyum muyaṟciyil tolkāppiya āyvu naṭattappaṭa vēṇṭum eṉṟum; atu oru putiya nōkku eṉṟum; atu etirkālattil toṭarntu mēṟkoḷḷappaṭa vēṇṭum eṉṟum; kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷatu iṭaccerukal paṟṟiya āyvu toṭara vēṇṭum eṉṟum; mūlappāṭa āyvu mukkiyam eṉṟum avar uṇarttavatākak koḷḷalām. ēṉeṉṟāl iṭiccerukal, muṟkālattil tolkāppiyam toṭarntu paṭikkappaṭṭu vantatiyum, iṭikkāla aṟiñarkaḷiṉ tolkāppiyam paṟṟiya matippīṭu, ilakkaṇam paṟṟiya uṇarvu ākiyavaṟṟip pulappaṭuttuva  tākak koṇṭē avar iti putiya aṇukumuṟi eṉṟu karutiyirukka vēṇṭum. iṭik kālattil tolkāppiyattip paṭittavarkaḷ tolkāppiyattil cila ceytikaḷ viṭupaṭ ṭuḷḷaṉa eṉṟum; tolkāppiyam ellāk kālattiṟkum uriya ilakkaṇam eṉṟāl taṅkaḷ kālaṅkaḷilum nikaḻnta māṟṟaṅkaḷiyum varuṇikkum cūttiram iṭampeṟa vēṇṭum eṉṟu karuti, iṭiccerukali ceytirukka vēṇṭum eṉṟum iṭiccerukalukku oru viḷakkam peṟappaṭukiṟatu.
tolkāppiyam mūlappāṭam paṟṟi piṉṉāl vanta āyvāḷarkaḷ (kantacāmi, pālacuppiramaṇiyaṉ) tolkāppiya mūṉṟu atikāraṅkaḷum oru āciriyarāl eḻutappaṭṭirukka vēṇṭum eṉṟu niṟuvuvatiltāṉ kavaṉam celuttiyuḷḷārkaḷ (caṇmukam, 1989: 145-57). atāvatu iraṇṭu tolkāppiyar eṉṟa karuttu piṟkāla tolkāppiya iṭiccerukal piracciṉi ituvari tīravum illi; orunāḷum tīrappōvatum illi. iruntālum, iṭiccerukal paṟṟiya kōṭpāṭṭu nili viḷakkamum; muraṇākap piṟkālattaviyākavum karutupavikaḷi iṉamkaṇṭu avaṟṟi tīra āyvukku uṭpaṭuttuvatum mikamika avaciyamē. aviyē tolkāppiya vācippukkum, puritalukkum utaviceyyum. avarē kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷapaṭi iṭiccerukalō muraṇō tamiḻk kalviyil tolkāppiyattiṉ mukkiyattuvattik kuṟikkātu. `it may be there are interpolatcon of a later age here and there, in this work but in the min represents the ancient Tamilians cultureï,. capable of being deciphered bny the modern mind’). (nūliṉ iṭiccerukalkaḷ āṅkāṅkē iruntālum mukkiya pakuti paṇṭiya tamiḻarkaḷiṉ paṇpāṭṭup piratinitiyāyt taṟkālattil purintukoḷḷak kūṭiyatāy amintuḷḷatu) eṉpatē uṇmi. tolkāppiyam paḻantamiḻar aṟivuc cātaṉiyiṉ koṭumuṭi; tamiḻiṉ mutal aṟiviyal nūl.
3.1.2. kālam
kaḷaviyal uri, tolkāppiyam iṭiccaṅka, kaṭiccaṅka ilakkaṇam eṉṟum iṉṟu namakkuk kiṭittirukkum tokiyum pāṭṭum mūṉṟām caṅkattiṟku uriyatu eṉṟum kūṟi, ōr uḷvaralāṟu kaṟpittatu. uriyāciriyarkaḷum āṅkāṅkē cila māṟupāṭukaḷi eṭuttukkāṭṭic ceṉṟuḷḷārkaḷ. inta nūṟṟāṇṭil, ilakkiya varalāṟṟu āciriyarkaḷ tolkāppiya vitikkum caṅka ilakkiya moḻikaḷukkum uḷḷa māṟupāṭukaḷi - atāvatu avaruṭiya ilakkaṇa vitikaḷ puṟakkaṇikkap paṭṭatāl ēṟpaṭṭa māṟupāṭukaḷi varalāṟṟu māṟṟamākak koṇṭu tolkāppiyam caṅka ilakkiyattiṟku muntiyatu eṉpatu uṟutippaṭuttuvatākak koṇṭatōṭu tolkāppiyattiṉ kālamēl elliyi avaravar viruppattiṟku ēṟpa amittuk koṇṭuviṭṭārkaḷ.
te.po.mī. oru moḻi varalāṟṟu aṟiñar eṉṟa muṟiyil, tolkāppiyattil uḷḷa akac cāṉṟukaḷip puṟac cāṉṟukaḷōṭu iṇittu uḷnili varalāṟṟu muṟiyil oru muṭivukku vantuḷḷār. akac cāṉṟukaḷāka, tolkāppiyattil tamiḻiṉ varivaṭiva amippu paṟṟi nēraṭiyākac cila cūttiraṅkaḷum (15-7, 104-5), neṭuṅkaṇakku vippu muṟi paṟṟik kuṟippākac cila cūttiraṅkaḷum (1, 18-9 piṟappiyal) uḷḷaṉa. puṟaccāṉṟukaḷāka, iṉṟu vari naṭanta āyvāl tamiḻ pirāmi kalveṭṭukaḷē mikap paḻiya cāṉṟu ātāraṅkaḷ eṉpatu aṟiyamuṭikiṟatu. avaṟṟi virivāka ārāyntu (1974 a: 282-4)  tolkāppiyam tamiḻ pirāmi kalveṭṭukaḷukkup piṉvarum - (atāvatu ki.mu. mūṉṟu allatu iraṇṭām nūṟṟāṇṭukkup pintiyatu eṉṟu mēlelli variyaṟuttuḷḷār) caṅka ilakkiyattiṟku muṉṉarum eḻutappaṭṭirukka vēṇṭum eṉṟu tamiḻ moḻi varalāṟṟil (1965) variyaṟuttuḷḷār.
te.po.mī. itaṟku ātaravākak kaḷaviyal uri marapiyum cuṭṭik kāṭṭiṉālum atil muḻu nampikki illi eṉpatu atē pattiyil uḷḷa kīḻkkaṇṭa pakuti uṇarttakiṟatu. atāvatu tolkāppiyattil ilakkiyat toki nūlkaḷ paṟṟiya kuṟippu kāṇappaṭuvatu viyappāka uḷḷatu. avaruṭiya ilakkiyak kōṭpāṭu caṅka ilakkiyat tokiyi oṭṭiyatu eṉpatu cūcakamāka uṇarttukiṟatu eṉkiṟār. itaṟku mukkiya kāraṇam avaruṭiya tirāviṭa moḻi oppilakkaṇa aṟivu.
iṉṟiya ilakkaṇa āyvil oppilakkaṇa nōkku, ilakkaṇa uruvākka nōkku, ilakkiya moḻi nōkku mutaliya aṇukumuṟikaḷāl tolkāppiya moḻiyiliruntu caṅkat tamiḻ māṟupaṭum moḻiyamippu taravukaḷ maṟuparicīlaṉikku uḷpaṭuttappaṭṭup putiya eṇṇaṅkaḷ muṉvikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa (caṇmukam 1989: 166 to). utāraṇamākat tolkāppiyar collukku mutalil varātu eṉṟa cakara eḻuttu caṅka ilakkiyattil 61 coṟkaḷil vantuḷḷatu. avaṟṟuḷ carakku, cakki, cammaṭṭi, caṅku, cantu ākiyavi piṟa tirāviṭa moḻikaḷilum kāṇappaṭuvatāl, tirāviṭa moḻikaḷukkum - eṉavē paḻantamiḻukkum uriya paḻiyacoṟkaḷ. anta niliyil ivi varalāṟṟu māṟṟattāl tōṉṟiya coṟkaḷākak karutamuṭiyātavi. appaṭiyāṉāl ivaṟṟit tolkāppiyar ēṉ puṟakkaṇittār eṉṟa kēḷvi eḻukiṟatu. atu oru ilakkaṇa uruvākkak kōṭpāṭṭu nōkkiyum, ilakkiya moḻik kōṭpāṭṭu nōkkiyum pulappaṭuttukiṟatu. atāvatu antac coṟkaḷit takuvaḻakkuc coṟkaḷāka - iyaṟcoṟkaḷākak (standard dialect) karutāmal, ticiccoṟkaḷākat tolkāppiyar karuti otukkiyirukka vēṇṭum. atē camayattil tolkāppiyar ticiccoṟkaḷik koḷki aḷavil - ticic coṟkaḷ eṉṟu aṅkīkarittu, vēṟu vakiyil (oli uṟaḻcci ākiyavaṟṟi) cila ticiccoṟkaḷukku ilakkaṇam eḻutiyiruntālum akarātiyiṉ uṟaḻcciyi - inta ticic coṟkaḷip puṟakkaṇittu iyaṟcoṟkaḷākak karutappaṭṭavi kaḷukkē ilakkaṇam eḻutiyuḷḷār. inta vēṟupāṭu tolkāppiyariṉ ilakkaṇa uruvākkak kōṭpāṭṭil taravut tērvu eṉṟa oru parimāṇattip pulappaṭuttukiṟatu. atē camayattil tolkāppiyar collukku mutalil varātu eṉṟu kūṟiya yakaratti mutalāka uṭiya coṟkaḷ caṅka ilakkiyattil vantuḷḷaṉa. ivi piṟamoḻiccoṟkaḷāka iruppatāl iṅku tolkāppiya moḻi paḻamiyāṉatu. eṉavē inta iraṇṭu niliyum paricīlikka vēṟu cila mukkiyamāṉa kēḷvikaḷ ilakkaṇa uruvākka nōkkil eḻutappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa: 1. tolkāppiya ilakkaṇattukku aṭippaṭiyāṉa ilakkiyam evi? 2. muṉṉāl eḻunta tolkāppiyam piṉṉāl vanta caṅka ilakkiyattiṉ ilakkiya kōṭpāṭukaḷip perumpāṉmiyum eppaṭi piratipalikka muṭiyum? 3. tolkāppiyar caṅka ilakkiyattiṟkup piṉ tōṉṟiya kuṟaḷ, patiṉeṇ kīḻkkaṇakku nūlkaḷ ākiyavaṟṟiṉ yāppukku(veṇpā) eppaṭi ilakkaṇam eḻutiṉār? eṉpavi avi.
moḻiyamippu nōkkil tolkāppiyattil kuṟippiṭṭa pala meymayakkaṅkaḷ caṅka ilakkiyattil kāṇappaṭavilli. ataṟku uriyāciriyarkaḷ iraṇṭu vitamāka viṭi aḷittuḷḷārkaḷ: 1. eḻuttatikārak kaḷaṉ paṟṟiya kōṭpāṭṭup piracciṉiyāka - taṉi moḻiyil varumā? toṭar moḻiyil varumā? eṉpatu. 2. piṟkālattil maṟintu viṭṭatu eṉpatu. iṉṟu uriyāciriyarkaḷiṉ māṟupaṭṭa ilakkaṇakkōṭpāṭukaḷāka karuti, mey mayakkam paṟṟiya karuttukkaḷ puṟakkaṇikkappaṭṭu varukiṟatu. tolkāppiyar kūṟiya ellā mey mayakkaṅkaḷum utāraṇaṅkaḷ kāṭṭavēṇṭi, avi toṭar moḻiyil varum eṉṟu koṇṭālum ñt (uṟiñ tītu), lt (vēl tītu) lm (vēl māṇṭatu), ḷt (vēl tītu) pōṉṟa mey mayakkaṅkaḷit tolkāppiyar kuṟippiṭa villi eṉṟa kuṟṟam varum. ilakkaṇa kōṭpāṭṭu nōkkil taṉi moḻiyil varum mey mayakkaṅkaḷē ciṟappākak koḷḷappaṭum . nacciṉārkkiṉiyariṉ karuttum atuvē. pala mey mayakkaṅkaḷukku utāraṇam kāṇappaṭātataṟku ilakkaṇa uruvākkak kōṭpāṭē kāraṇam. atutāṉ ilakkaṇa āciriyar kāṭci niṟiviviṭa (ṭiṭiḷenaceyaviṭiyēṭa yaṉanaḷūrayula) - moḻiyil vantatip paṭṭiyal iṭuvativiṭa ilakkaṇap potumikkum eḷimikkum iṭam koṭuppār. ati viḷakka niṟivu (ṉanaḷuciyīviena yaṉanaḷūrayula) eṉpārkaḷ moḻiyiyalār. ataṉ mūlam mikippaṭak kūṟal, mīppotumippaṭuttutal (ṭienaca pananēcayaṭaṇiyaviṭin) eṉṟa kuṟṟam ēṟpaṭum eṉṟālum ilakkaṇattiṉ potumiyum, eḷimiyum ēṟpaṭuvatum oru naṉmiyākak karutappaṭum. mēlum appaṭic ceyyappaṭṭa cila vaḻakkukaḷ piṟkālattil moḻiyil kāṇappaṭum. utāraṇamākat tolkāppiyar - ṅṅ - eṉṟa uṭaṉili mey mayakkam kuṟaḷ, cilampu, mēkali ākiya nūlkaḷilēyē vantuḷḷatu; vēṟu cila meymmayakkaṅkaḷāl ilakkaṇa varuṇaṉi eḷimippaṭuttappaṭṭuḷḷatu (caṇmukam, 1981: 99, 151).
iṅku iṉṉoru uṇmiyum cuṭṭikkāṭṭa takuntatu. caṅkap pulavarkaḷum, piṟkālap pulavarkaḷum, ilakkaṇa āciriyarkaḷum colliṟku mutalil varātu eṉṟa eḻuttukkaḷil tolkāppiya vitiyip puṟakkaṇittavarkaḷ mey mayakkattil avar coṉṉa mey mayakkaṅkaḷ palavaṟṟi ēṟṟuk koḷḷavilli. (vy, ry pōṉṟaviyē viya, riya eṉṟu māṟṟik koṇṭārkaḷ). atāvatu varakkūṭātu eṉṟa vitiyi varalām (collukku mutalil varum eḻuttukkaḷ paṟṟiyatu) eṉṟum varalām eṉṟa vitiyi varakkūṭātu eṉṟum iraṇṭu niliyil tolkāppiya vitikaḷ piṟkālattil puṟakkaṇikkappaṭṭaṉa. tolkāppiyak kalvi nōkkil ārāyappaṭa vēṇṭiya oṉṟu. tolkāppiyattil viḷakka niṟivu kōṭpāṭṭic collatikārattil viravup peyarkaḷ varuṇaṉiyilum (caṇmukam 1984: 160 to) poruḷatikārattil puṟattiṇikaḷiṉ iṇiyāṉa akattiṇi varuṇaṉiyilum (caṇmukam 1989: 174) kāṇalām.
3.2. ilakkaṇa moḻiyiyal kōṭpāṭu
tolkāppiya ilakkaṇa moḻiyiyal kōṭpāṭu āyvil avaruṭiya uriccol (1974 ā) eṉṟa kaṭṭuri mukkiya iṭam peṟum. ataṉpaṭi uriccol eṉpatu aṭiccol eṉpatu poruttamāṉatē. āṉāl, ataṉ col vakippāṭu (yīyacavaḷa ṭika ḷanayīnuh) vivātattiṟku uriyatu. itu muṉṉarē virivāka vivātikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu (caṇmukam 1984: 163-72). pirayōka vivēkam mutaliya vaṭamoḻi marapi oṭṭiya ilakkaṅkaḷil kāṇappaṭum moḻiyiyal kōṭpāṭṭi āṅkāṅkē (1974 a) viḷakkiyuḷḷār.
3.3. ilakkaṇa uruvākkam
tolkāppiyariṉ kālac cūḻal, ilakkaṇa vitikaḷiṉ tōṟṟam ākiyavi paṟṟit te.po.mī.  kūṟiyavi iṉṟu pēcappaṭukiṉṟa ilakkaṇa camūkaviyalukku (ḷuṭiuiṭiṭaṭipala ṭika ḻucayaaayaca) aṭinātamāka amintuḷḷaṉa. tolkāppiyariṉ kālac cūḻalip palarum kaṇakkil eṭuttukkoḷvatilli. varalāṟṟu uṇarvu aṭippaṭiyil kālaccūḻaliyum ilakkaṇa uruvākkattiṟku oru kāraṇiyākat te.po.mī. kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār. atāvatu tolkāppiyam tamiḻil mikap paḻamiyāṉa nūlāka iruntālum atu vaṭamoḻic coṟkaḷum vaṭakkattiya celvākkum tamiḻ moḻiyilum paṇpāṭṭilum pukunta piṟakē, tolkāppiyam iyaṟṟappaṭṭirukka vēṇṭum. tamiḻarkaḷ aṉittulaka kīḻi nāṭṭup potumoḻiyāṉa vaṭamoḻi yōṭum paṇpāṭṭōṭum toṭarpu ēṟpaṭṭu vaḷarnta piṟakē, atu naṭantirukka vēṇṭum eṉṟu avar karutukiṟār.
tolkāppiyar aṉittintiya ilakkaṇa marapi aṟintu piṉpaṟṟiyavar, appaṭi piṉpaṟṟiṉālum, tamiḻ moḻimarapiṉ uyirppukkuk kuntakam viḷikkātavar eṉṟa karutti teḷivākak kūṟiyatu ilakkaṇa uruvākkam paṟṟiya cintaṉiyiṉ aṭinātam. atāvatu oru moḻiyil ilakkaṇam paṟṟiya cintaṉi mutaṉ mutalil piṟa moḻiyōṭum tamiḻmoḻiyiṉ paḻiya ilakkiya moḻiyōṭum uḷḷa māṟupāṭṭi uṇarnta piṟakē tōṉṟum eṉṟa ulakaḷāviya cintaṉi (ḻalaaḷa 1974) piratipalikkiṟatu. mēlum cila cintaṉikaḷi atu tōṟṟu vittuḷḷatu (caṇmukam 1989: 176-81). te.po.mī.yiṉ karuttukkaḷic curukkamākak kūṟavēṇṭumāṉāl avar tamiḻ moḻimutalvātam eṉṟa karuttiyum ottukkoḷvār alla; atē camayattil vaṭa moḻi mutal vātak karuttiyum ottukkoḷpavar alla eṉṟu collalām.
moḻiyiyal aṟiñar eṉṟa muṟiyil ilakkaṇattiṉ paṇpit te.po.mī. naṉku vaṟpuṟuttiyuḷḷār. tolkāppiyam oru viḷakkamuṟi ilakkaṇam, varalāṟṟu ilakkaṇam alla. atil ulakap potumiyum kāṇappaṭukiṟatu (1961: 115). itaṟku mēlāka ilakkaṇa vitikaḷ anta moḻic camūkattiliruntu peṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉa eṉṟa karutti nuenacala caraṭana ṭika ḻucayaaayaca ḷi ḷaṭiuyiṭa in ṭicapiin,, ṉanaayaṉēnaṉa le vāna nopinanuniḷa ṭika viana yaṉē raḷayapana (1961: 117). eṉṟa avaruṭiya vācakam ilakkaṇavitikaḷil allatu ilakkaṇaṅkaḷiṉ camūka iyakkap paṇpit tolinōkkōṭu teḷivupaṭuttukiṟatu. atu ilakkaṇak kalvikkup payaṉ paṭuvatiyum kūnayuhipē ṭika kūyaaṭi ḻucayaaayaca eṉṟa kaṭṭuriyil aḻakākavum teḷivākavum viḷakkiyuḷḷār. ataṉ tākkattāl iṉṟu tamiḻilum ilakkaṇac camūkaviyal paṟṟip pēcappaṭuvatik kāṇamuṭikiṟatu (caṇmukam 1992, 1994).
4. muṭivuri
aṟiñarkaḷum kālattip piratipalittum (oṭṭiyum), kālattāl uruvākkappaṭṭum (veṭṭiyum) uruppeṟu kiṟārkaḷ. avaruṭiya āyvip poṟutta variyil, mūṉṟu caṅkam paṟṟiya ōraḷavu nampikki, akattiyar caṅka kālattil iruntirukkāviṭṭālum, piṟkālattil vāḻntirukka vēṇṭum eṉṟa eṇṇam avaruṭiya kālatti oṭṭiyavi. pala tamiḻ nūlkaḷukku vaṭamoḻi mūlam kaṟpikkappaṭṭati maṟuttut taṉṉuṭiya āyvāl tamiḻē mūlamāka uṭiyatu eṉṟu nirūpittatu avaruṭiya kālatti oṭṭiyavi. avarukkup pukaḻit tēṭittantavi. pala paṭṭaṅkaḷiyum peṟṟut tantavi. āṉāl ilakkaṇap paṇpu, ilakkaṇa uruvākkam paṟṟiyavi putiyavi; iṉṟum muḻumiyāka ēṟṟukkoḷḷappaṭātavi. taṉippaṭṭa maṉitarkaḷ iḻivup paṭṭam kūṭac cūṭṭip parappiṉārkaḷ. āṉāl avar avaṟṟi yellām paṟṟik kavalippaṭavilli. taṉ maṉatukkum - aṟivukkum cari eṉṟu paṭṭatit tiriyamākavē colli vantār. tamiḻ ulakil moḻiyuṇarvu atikamāka āṭci ceyta kālattil aṟivu vaḻippaṭṭa viññāṉa rītiyāṉa karuttukaḷ paravuvataṟkup pāṭupaṭṭār.
(intak kaṭṭuri maturi kāmarācar palkalikkaḻakat tamiḻiyaṟ pulam 22-3-1999 naṭattiya `te.po.mī. nūṟṟāṇṭu viḻāvi nōkki’ eṉṟa karuttaraṅkil paṭikkappaṭṭatu.)
-ce.vi. caṇmukam
nāṉ aṟinta tamiḻ aṟiñarkaḷ, pak. 85-100,


3. urineṟiyum viḷakkamum
uri tōṉṟātu iruppiṉ, tolkāppiyam kiṭittirukkumā eṉpatu aiyappāṭē. uriyāciriyar purintiruppatu mikap periya toṇṭu. ivartam koṇṭāl nūl pātukākkappaṭṭatu; cemmiyākkappaṭṭatu. nuṇṇaṟivu uṭiyōrkkum pulaṉākāta ilakkaṇam ciṟṟaṟiviṉōrkkum pulaṉākiyatu. tolkāppiyam uṇarttum ilakkaṇattōṭu, 1500 āṇṭu kālamāka vaḷarntu uriyāciriyar kālattu nilaviya ilakkaṇamum uriyiṉ ūṭē āṅkāṅkē uṇarttappaṭṭatu. uriyāciriyarkaḷ uritta ilakkaṇaṅkaḷi ellām orumukap paṭuttuvōm eṉil, aḵtōr ilakkaṇa nūlāka amiyum. urivaḻi avar kālattuc camutāya nili aṟiyappaṭṭatu. uriyiṉṟēl "tolkāppiyam' eṉum arumperum celvattiṉ uṇmit taṉmiyi aṟintu koḷḷaviyalātu. nāmē aṟiyā niliyil ulakaṟiyac ceyvatu eṅṅaṉam? iruṭṭaṟiyil kiṭanta celvam, iṉṟu veḷiccattiṟku vantirukkiṟatu eṉil, ataṟkuk kāraṇam uriyeṉum tiruviḷakkē.
iḷampūraṇam' eṉum tiruviḷakkē tolkāppiyattiṟku mutal viḷakkāy niṟkiṟatu. ivviḷakkiṟku muṉpu pala  viḷakkukaḷ iruntaṉa eṉpataṟku iḷampūraṇam tarum kuṟippukaḷē cāṉṟāka viḷaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. avviḷakkukaḷi ellām nām iṉṟuvari peṟāmiyāl, iḷampūraṇamē mutal viḷakkākat tikaḻkiṟatu. ivviḷakkut tolkāppiyam kaṟpōrkku maṭṭumaṉṟi, piṉṉar vanta uriyāciriyarkkum oḷiviḷakkāy viḷaṅkiṟṟu. iḷampūraṇattip perumaḷavil maṟutturikkum vaṭa nūṟkaṭali nilikaṇṭu aṟinta cēṉāvariyarukkum oḷiviḷakkāy viḷaṅkiṟṟu eṉpatu kuṟippiṭattakkatu. uri eṉpatu nūliṉ nuṭpatti viḷakkamuṟac ceyvatu. uri, nūlukku iṇiyāṉatu. ātaliṉ itaṉi urinūl eṉkiṟōm. ovvoru nūṟpāviṟkum collum uri āyvuri eṉpatāl, itaṉi āyvuri nūl eṉṟu vaḻaṅkutal vēṇṭum. itukāṟum kiṭittavaṟṟāl iḷampūraṇam, pērāciriyam, teyvacciliyam, cēṉāvariyam, nacciṉārkkiṉiyam ākiyaṉa kallāṭaṉārukku muṉ viḷaṅkiṉa.
urineṟi
iḷampūraṇamē, cēṉāvariyam mutalāṉa aṉittu urikaḷukkum aṭittaḷamāka amintatu. iḷampūraṇattiṟku iṟiyaṉār kaḷaviyal uriyum yāpparuṅkala viruttiyum aṭittaḷamāka amintaṉa. nūṟcemmikkum kaṟpōrkku aiyamaṟat teḷivippataṟkum uriyāciriyar kālattu ilakkaṇatti uṇarttuvataṟkum urineṟi payaṉpaṭṭatu. īṇṭuk kallāṭaṉār mēṟkoṇṭa urineṟikaḷip paṟṟik kāṇpōm. avi varumāṟu:
1.	atikāra muṉṉuri eḻututal
2.	atikāram, iyalkaḷukkup peyarkkāraṇam kūṟal
3.	iyipu kūṟal (atikāra iyipu, iyal iyipu, nūṟpā iyipu) 
4. 	vippuk kūṟal (atikāra vippu, iyal vippu, nūṟpā vippu, vakimuṟi vippu)
5. 	nūṟpā amippiṉi ārāytal 
6.	nūṟpā uriyamippu (karutturi, poruḷuri, eṭuttukkāṭṭu, viḷakkavuri, itaṉāṟ colliyatu)
7.	eṭuttukkāṭṭiṉip poruttik kāṭṭutal
8.	coṟporuḷ taral 
9.	viṉā viṭiyāṉ viḷakkutal
10.	mūlapāṭatti ārāytal (nūṟpā, coṟṟoṭar,  vakippāṭu)
11.	pāṭa vēṟupāṭu kāṇutal
12.	piṟar uriyi ēṟṟum, maṟuttum urittal 
13.	iru karuttuk kūṟal (aiyappāṭṭōṭu uri eḻututal)
14.	moḻi māṟṟip poruḷ kāṇutal
15.	urittavāṟē urikka, okkum eṉal
16.	uṇarttiyatum uṇarttuvatum; kāṭṭiṉām, kāṭṭutum eṉal 
17.	nūṟpā variyaṟi kūṟutal
18.	vaḻikāṭṭutal
19.	uvami, utti, urikkuṟiyīṭu koṇṭu viḷakkutal 
20.	eṉṟataṉāṉ eṉṟurittu viḷakkam tarutal
21.	ilēcu, māṭṭēṟu koṇṭu viḷakkutal
22.	ilakkaṇak kuṟippu, ilakkaṇa muṭipu kūṟic cellutal 
23.	ilakkaṇattik kaṇakkiṭutal
24.	itaṉāṟ colliyatu eṉṟurittut tokuttuk kūṟal
25.	vantavaḻik kāṇka eṉṟal
26.	camutāya niliyiṉik kūṟic cellutal
27.	nūlāciriyar kūṟātu ceṉṟa ilakkaṇattiyum uriyāciriyar kālattu uruvāṉa ilakkaṇattiyum kūṟic cellutal
iḷampūraṇar muppattintum teyvacciliyār iruppattu oṉpatum cēṉāvariyar muppatum pērāciriyar muppattoṉṟum nacciṉārkkiṉiyar muppattoṉṟum eṉa urineṟikaḷik koṇṭu ilakkaṇatti viḷakki yirukkiṉṟaṉar. kallāṭaṉār iruppattēḻu urineṟikaḷik koṇṭu viḷakkukiṟār.
uriyamippu
collatikāra uri atikāra uri, iyal uri, nūṟpā uri eṉa mūvakippaṭum. iḷampūraṇar, nacciṉārkkiṉiyar ākiyōr eḻuttatikārattiṟkum uri eḻutiyiruttalāl, pāyira uriyōṭu cērttu nāṉku vakippaṭum. niṟutta muṟiyāṉē atikāra uriyiṉi mutaṟkaṇ kāṇpōm.
atikāra uri
iḷampūraṇattilum (eḻuttu, poruḷil akat. puṟat.) paḻiyavuriyattilum ivvuri taṉiyākak kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. ēṉiya urikaḷil avvav atikārattut tali nūṟpāviṉkīḻ collappaṭukiṟatu. itil peyarkkāraṇam, atikāra iyipu, atikāra vippu. atikārattuḷ otappaṭum ilakkaṇam ākiyaṉa viḷakkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. kallāṭaṉār,
	ivvatikāram eṉ nutali eṭuttuk koḷḷap paṭṭatō eṉiṉ, atikāra nutaliyatūum atikārat tiṉatu peyar urippavē viḷaṅkum. ivvatikāram eṉṉa peyarttō eṉiṉ collatikāram eṉṉum peyarttu. aḵtu iṭukuṟiyō kāraṇakkuṟiyō eṉiṉ, kāraṇakkuṟi. eṉṉi kāraṇam eṉiṉ, col uṇarttiṉami kāraṇattiṉ eṉpatu. eṉṉi? kiḷaviyākkam eḻuvāyāka, eccaviyal iṟutiyākak kiṭanta oṉpatu ōttukkaḷuḷḷum colliṉkaṇ kiṭanta vikaṟpam ellām ārāyntār eṉak koḷka
eṉap peyarkkāraṇatti ēṉiyōriṉum viḷakkik kūṟi yiruppatu ciṟappiṟkuriyatu. aṭuttu atikārap poruṇmi, coṟporuṇmiyi viḷakkukiṟār  atikāram eṉṟataṉ poruṇmi eṉṉi eṉiṉ muṟimi eṉpar. ‘atu colli uṇarttiya muṟimi eṉa viriyum' eṉpar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar. ‘atikāram eṉṉum coṟkup pala poruḷ uḷavēṉum, īṇṭu atikāram eṉṟatu oru poruḷ nutali varum pala ōttiṉatu tokutiyi eṉka' eṉpar cēṉāvariyar. ‘atikāram’ eṉpataṉi iyalukkum oru colliṟkum uriyāciriyarkaḷ payaṉpaṭuttiyuḷḷaṉar. itu paṟṟi ‘iḷampūraṇar urineṟi’ nūlil virivāka ārāyap paṭṭuḷḷatu. cēṉāvariyar viḷakkam ēṉiyōriṉum ciṟappāka amintirukkiṟatu. aṭuttu,
col eṉṟataṟkup poruṇmi eṉṉi eṉiṉ ōci eṉṟavāṟu eṉavum,
eḻuttoṭu puṇarātu poruḷ aṟivuṟuttum ociyum uḷavō eṉiṉ, uḷa eṉavum,
eḻuttoṭu colliṭi vēṟṟumi eṉṉi eṉiṉ, taṉṉi uṇartti niṉṟavaḻi eḻuttu eṉappaṭum; tāṉ iṭi niṉṟu poruḷ uṇarttiya vaḻic col eṉappaṭum
eṉavum kallāṭaṉār viṉavi virivāṉatoru viḷakkattiṉit tarukiṟār. ivi yāvum nacciṉārkkiṉiyar uriyit taḻuvi eḻutappaṭṭavi. ivar, ivviṭattu,
eḻuttal ōciyum eḻuttoṭu puṇarātu poruḷ aṟivikkum ociyum eḻuttoṭu puṇarntu poruḷi aṟivikkum ōciyum eḻuttoṭu puṇarntē poruḷi aṟivuṟuttātu iṟiñi, miṟiñi eṉṟāṟ pōl varum ociyum eṉa oci nāṉku vakippaṭum. annāṉkaṉuḷ piṉṉiṉṟa iraṇṭum ivvatikārattu ārāyappaṭukiṉṟaṉa
eṉṟu kūṟiyatu piṟar evarum kūṟātatu. aṭuttu atikārat tiṉuḷ ōtappaṭum ilakkaṇatti ,
avvōttukkaḷuḷḷum eṉittu vakiyāṉ uṇarttiṉāṉō eṉiṉ, eṭṭu vakippaṭṭa ilakkaṇattāṉ uṇarttiṉāṉ eṉpatu. ava uṇarac col uṇarntāṉām.
avi yāviyeṉiṉ iraṇṭu tiṇi vakuttu, attiṇikkaṇ aintu pāl vakuttu, ēḻu vaḻu vakuttu, eṭṭu vēṟṟumi vakuttu, āṟu toki vakuttu, mūṉṟu iṭam vakuttu, mūṉṟu kālam vakuttu iraṇṭu iṭattāṉ ārāyntāṉām eṉpatu
colliṟku ilakkaṇam āmāṟu eṉṉiyō eṉiṉ, iṉmi mukattāṉum uṇmi mukattāṉum aminta ilakkaṇamām eṉṟu uṇarka
eṉṟurittu ovvoṉṟiyum virittuk kūṟukiṟār. iṉmi mukattāṉum uṇmi mukattāṉum aminta ilakkaṇamām eṉpatu paṭittu makiḻtaṟkuriya urittoṭar. oṉṟaṟku viti kūṭṭam peṟāmal irukka vēṇṭum eṉavum, peṟṟirukka vēṇṭum eṉavum āciriyar otic celluvar.
viṉiyeṉap paṭuvatu vēṟṟumi koḷḷātu niṉiyuṅ kālik kālamoṭu tōṉṟum
eṉpatu viṉikku ōtappaṭum potuvilakkaṇam. itil, `vēṟṟumi koḷḷātu' eṉpatu iṉmi mukattāṉ kūṟiyatu: ‘kālamoṭu tōṉṟum' eṉpatu uṇmi mukattāṉ kūṟiyatu. ‘nampi makaṉ, nampikku makaṉ eṉpatu ilakkaṇamilvaḻi mayaṅkal. nampiyatu makaṉ eṉpatu iṉmiyiṉ eṉka' (vē.ma:11) eṉa iṉmi mukattāṉ eṭuttukkāṭṭiṉi viḷakkukiṟār kallāṭaṉār. uyartiṇik kuṟippu viṉiyāvatu, aṉmi, iṉmi, uṇmi, vaṉmi eṉṉum poruḷilum varum eṉpar (viṉi:16) āciriyar. ivvuriyāciriyar, ivaṟṟil uḷḷa iṉmi, uṇmi ākiyavaṟṟik koṇṭu ciṟappiṟkuriya ittoṭari amittār eṉalām.
col ilakkaṇaṅkaḷ eṭṭiṉiyum kūṟiya kallāṭaṉār, avi, ‘uriyiṟkōṭal' eṉum utti vakiyāṉ kūṟappaṭṭaṉa eṉa uṇarka eṉpar. atāvatu, āciriyar avaṟṟiṟku viti ōtātati uṇarttiyatōṭu, ‘uriyiṟkōṭal' eṉum urineṟiyāl ivar kūṟic  ceṉṟiruppatu ciṟappiṟkuriyatu. collatikārattil ōtappaṭum eṭṭu vakiyāṉa ilakkaṇaṅkaḷiyum oru nūṟpā mūlam āciriyar otiyiruttal vēṇṭum eṉpati, ivvuri valiyuṟuttukiṟatu. iv eṭṭu ilakkaṇatti iḷampūraṇar, nacciṉārkkiṉiyar, paḻiyavurikārar ākiyōr kūṟiyiruppiṉum, āciriyar viti ōtātati uṇarttavilli. ivviṭattu nūlāciriyar paṇiyi uriyāciriyarkaḷ ceytiruppatu pōṟṟutaṟkuriyatu. teyvacciliyārum cēṉāvariyarum toṭarmoḻi, taṉimoḻi eṉa vēṟu muṟiyil kūṟuvar.
‘piṟa ilakkaṇam uṇṭeṉiṉum, ivi perumpāṉmiya eṉṟātal, aviyum ivaṟṟuḷ aṭaṅkum eṉṟātal koḷḷappaṭum eṉka' eṉṟu kallāṭaṉār teḷivupaṭutti yiruppatu kuṟippiṭattakkatu. itaṉi, ‘eṭṭiṟanta palavaki yāṉum uṇarttupa' eṉpar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar.
iyal uri
itil, iyal peyarkkāraṇam, iyal iyipu, iyal vippu iyalil ōtappaṭum ilakkaṇam ākiyaṉa tarappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
1. collatikāra mutal iyal uri atikāra uriyi aṭuttum, ēṉiya urikaḷ avvav iyaliṉ mutal nūṟpāvi aṭuttum iṭam peṟukiṉṟaṉa. ‘immutalōttu eṉṉutali yeṭuttuk koḷḷappaṭṭatō veṉiṉ' eṉṟum, ‘ivvōttu eṉṉa peyarttōveṉiṉ' eṉṟum viṉāvi, kiḷaviyākkattiṟku viḷakkam tarappaṭukiṟatu. itē pōṉṟu ‘ivvōttu eṉṉa peyarttō eṉiṉ, ‘yātō vēṟṟumi ceytavāṟeṉiṉ' eṉa vēṟṟumiyiyalilum, ‘ivvōttu eṉṉa peyarttō eṉiṉ' eṉṟu vēṟṟumi mayaṅkiyal, peyariyal, viṉiyiyal, iṭiyiyalilum viṉā eḻuppi viḷakkam koṭukkappaṭukiṟatu. iṭiyiyalil, `iṭiccol eṉṉum poruṇmi eṉṉi yeṉiṉ' eṉṟu kiḷaviyākkam, vēṟṟumiyiyalil toṭuttatip pōṉṟu mēlum oru viṉāt toṭuttu viḷakkappaṭukiṟatu. mutal iyal uriyil peyarkkāraṇam, ōttiṉkaṇ collappaṭum ilakkaṇam, ōttu nāṉku eṉātu oṉpatu eṉa variyaṟuttatu ēṉ? ākiyavaṟṟiṟkāṉa viḷakkam tarappaṭukiṟatu.
	ōttu nutaliyatūum ōttiṉatu peyar urippavē viḷaṅkum. kiḷaviyākkam eṉṉum peyarttu. kiḷavi eṉpatu col. ākkam eṉpatu coṟkaḷ poruḷkaṇ mēlāmāṟu. coṟkaḷ poruḷkaṇ mēlāmāṟu uṇarttiṉamiyiṉ kiḷaviyākkam eṉṉum peyarttu. oruvaṉ mēlāmāṟu itu, orutti mēlāmāṟu itu, palar mēlāmāṟu itu, oṉṟaṉ mēlāmāṟu itu, palavaṟṟiṉ mēlāmāṟu itu, vaḻuvāmāṟu itu, vaḻuvamiyumāṟu itu eṉap poruṭkaṇ mēlāmāṟu uṇarttiṉamiyiṉ ciḷaviyākkam eṉṉum peyarttāyiṟṟu.
eṉṟu kallāṭaṉār. iyal peyarkkuk kāraṇamum viḷakkamum aḷikkiṟār. ivviḷakkam nacciṉārkkiṉiyar kūṟiyatu. aṭuttu, ivvōttiṟkum ēṉi ōttiṟkum uḷḷa vēṟupāṭṭiṉi,
	ēṉi ōttukkaḷuḷḷum poruṭkaṇ mēlāmāṟē yaṉṟō uṇarttiṉatu; poruḷ allavaṟṟiṉ mēlāmāṟu uṇarttiyatilli eṉiṉ, ēṉi ōttukkaḷuḷ poruṭkaṇ mēlāy niṉṟu avaṟṟilakkaṇam uṇarttiṉār; īṇṭu avi tammi āmāṟu uṇarttiṉār eṉpatu
eṉṟurittut teḷivupaṭuttiyiruppatu kuṟippiṭattakkatu.
2. vēṟṟumiyiyalil, ivvōttu eṉṉa peyarttō eṉiṉ, vēṟṟumi eṉṉum poruṇmi eṉṉi eṉiṉ eṉa viṉavi viḷakkam aḷitta kallāṭaṉār, yātō vēṟṟumi ceytavāṟu eṉiṉ eṉa viṉavi,
	oru poruḷ oruvaḻi oṉṟaṉic ceyyum viṉi mutalākiyum, oruvaḻi oṉṟu nikaḻtaṟku ētuvākiyum, oruvaḻi oṉṟu ceytaṟkuk karuviyākiyum, oruvaḻi oṉṟu ceytaṟkuc ceyappaṭu poruḷākiyum, oruvaḻi oṉṟu koṭuppataṉi ēṟpatākiyum, oruvaḻi oṉṟaṟku uvamam ākiyum, oruvaḻi oṉṟu nīṅkutaṟku iṭamākiyum, oruvaḻi oṉṟaṟku elliyākiyum, oruvaḻi oṉṟaṟku uṭimi yākiyum, oruvaḻi oṉṟu ceytaṟku iṭamākiyum, oruvaḻi muṉṉiliyātaṟ poruṭṭu viḷikkappaṭuvatu ākiyum iṉṉōraṉṉa piṟavumākiya potuppaṭa niṟṟaluṭittu
eṉat tantirukkum viḷakkam paṭittu iṉpuṟattakkatu. itu, teyvacciliyār uriyik kaṇṭu eḻutiyatu. eḻuvāy, vēṟṟumiyāyatu evvāṟu? itaṟkāṉa viḷakkam cēṉāvariyar uriyit taḻuviyatu.
vēṟṟumiyiyal, vēṟṟumi mayaṅkiyal, viḷimarapu ākiyavaṟṟuḷ kūṟappaṭum ilakkaṇam peyaratu ilakkaṇamē eṉak kallāṭaṉār niṟuviyiruppatu ciṟappiṟkuriya oṉṟu.
	yāṅṅaṉam uṇarttiṉārō eṉiṉ, ellāp peyarkaḷum eḻuvāyākip payaṉili kōṭalum, oruvaḻi eḻuvāyākātu vēṟōr nilimiyavāy niṟṟalum, urupēṟṟalum, oruvaḻic cila peyar urupēlātu niṟṟalum, kālam tōṉṟāmi niṟṟalum, oruvaḻit toḻiṟ poruḷoṭu kūṟiyakkāl kālam tōṉṟi niṟṟalum, viḷiyēṟṟu niṟṟalum, cila peyar viḷiyātu niṟṟalum iṉṉōraṉṉa piṟavum peyaratu ilakkaṇam eṉa uṇarttiṉār eṉpatu.
	potuvilakkaṇamēyaṉṟi urupu ilakkaṇam uṇart tiṉārāl eṉiṉ, avvārāycci peyaratu ilakkaṇa māy viṭutal uṭimiyiṉ amiyum eṉpatu
eṉpatu avaratu uri. itaṉāṉ ivvuriyāciriyariṉ nuṇmi naṉku pulappaṭum. ivviḷakkam piṟar evarum tarātatu. aṭuttut tarum viḷakkamum piṟar evarum tarātatu. avviḷakkam varumāṟu:
	ivvēṟṟumi ceyyumvaḻip palacol oru poruḷākiyum pala poruḷ oru collākiyum varum. iṉip palacol oru poruḷāki varumvaḻi, pala vēṟṟumi pala collākiyum, oru vēṟṟumi pala collākiyum varum. aviyāmāṟu aṟintu koḷka
3. vēṟṟumi mayakkam iruvakippaṭum. avi poruḷ mayakkam, urupu mayakkam eṉappaṭum eṉṟurittu avviraṇ ṭaṟkum viḷakkam taruvar cēṉāvariyar. nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum immuṟiyilē kūṟuvar. kallāṭaṉār ivvōttu eṉṉa peyarttō eṉiṉ eṉa viṉavi,
	urupum poruḷum uṭaṉ mayaṅkutalum, oruvaḻi urupē mayaṅkutalum, oṉṟaṟkuriyataṉōṭu oṉṟu mayaṅkutalum, iraṇṭum ottu mayaṅkutalum, oru poruṇmi oṉṟaṟkē uriyatākātu palavaṟṟōṭu mayaṅkutalum, oṉṟaṉatu oru poruḷōṭu oṉṟu mayaṅkutalum, oṉṟaṉatu pala poruḷōṭu oṉṟu mayaṅkutalum, oṉṟaṟku urimi pūṇṭu eṭuttōtiṉa poruḷvaḻi mayaṅkutalum, ōtāta poruḷvaḻi mayaṅkutalum, oṉṟu taṉ marapāy mayaṅkutalum, ilakkaṇa vaḻakkuḷvaḻi mayaṅkutalum, ilakkaṇa milvaḻi mayaṅkutalum, mayakka vakiyāṉ mayaṅkutalum, oṉṟaṉōṭu poruṇ muṭintu toṭarntaṭukki mayaṅkutalum, oṉṟaṉōṭu poruṇ muṭiyātu toṭarntaṭukki mayaṅkutalum, tokiyuḷ mayaṅku talum, tokiyil mayaṅkutalum, urupu vēṟṟumi yāy mayaṅkutalum, urupum urupum mayaṅkutalum, eṉṟiṉṉōraṉṉa vēṟṟumi mayakkam pala kūṟaliṉ vēṟṟumi mayaṅkiyal eṉṉum peyarāyiṟṟu
eṉat tantirukkum viḷakkam pārāṭṭutaṟkuriya oṉṟu. ittaku varivāṉatoru viḷakkam piṟar evarum tarāta oṉṟu.
nūṟpā uri
ovvōr atikārattiṉ mutal iyaliṉ nūṟpā uri, atikāra, iyal uri aṭuttum; ēṉiya iyalkaḷiṉ mutal nūṟpā uri iyal uri aṭuttum; ēṉiya nūṟpākkaḷiṉ uri ellām nūṟpā aṭuttum iṭam peṟukiṉṟaṉa. itu, karutturi, poruḷuri, eṭuttukkāṭṭu, viḷakkavuri eṉum nāṉku uṭpakutikaḷi uṭiyatu. ivvuri amippu aṉittu nūṟpākkaḷiṭattum kāṇutaṟku illi. cila iṭaṅkaḷil oṉṟu allatu iraṇṭu pakutikaḷ  kuṟintum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. karutturi, ‘itaṉ poruḷ' eṉṉum poruḷurimaṭṭumē uḷḷa uri amippu viḷimarapilum,  peyariyalilum (viḷi6,24,25,31; peya:8,9,13,14,22-25; viṉi :2) uṇṭu. karutturi, poruḷuri, eṭuttukkāṭṭu eṉṟu mūṉṟaṉ aḷavil aminta uri uṇṭu (kiḷavi:28, 29; 
viḷi: 4,5,13- 17, 21,27-30; pe:12). poruḷuri, viḷakkavuri iṉṟik karutturi, eṭuttukkāṭṭu maṭṭum uḷḷa iṭaṅkaḷum uṇṭu (vē.ma: 25). iṅṅaṉam uriyāciriyarkaḷ aṉivarumē mūṉṟu allatu nāṉku nilikaḷil uri amippiṉi amittuk koṇṭu uri eḻutuvataṉ kāraṇam, nūṟpā aḷavum, nūṟpā nuval poruḷum ākum. ivviraṇṭip poṟuttē nūṟpā uriyamippiṉ nili māṟukiṟatu. kallāṭaṉār uriyil ‘itaṉāl colliyatu’ eṉum pakutiyum uṇṭu. avvakiyil nūṟpā uri, aintu  uṭpakutikaḷi uṭiyatu. ippakuti iḷampūraṇam teyvacciliyam ākiyavaṟṟilum uṇṭu.
karutturi
itu, nūṟpā nuvalum karuttiṉic curukkamākak kūṟum pakuti. ivvuriyāciriyar, `kuṭimi āṇmi ' eṉavarum pattaṭi nūṟpāviṟku (kiḷavi:57), `eṉ - eṉiṉ, tiṇivaḻuk kāttal nutaliṟṟu' eṉavum, viṉiyiyalil varum cila nūṟpākkaḷukku (viṉi:4-6), `eṉ - eṉiṉ, viṉiccoṟkaḷatu pākupāṭu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu', `eṉ - eṉiṉ, mēl niṟuttamuṟiyāṉē uyartiṇi uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu', `eṉ eṉiṉ, taṉittaṉmi āmāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu' eṉavum curukkamākak karutturi eḻutuvar. `eṉ -eṉiṉ, itaṟkum viti okkum eṉṟavāṟu', `eṉ- eṉiṉ, itaṟkum akkaruttu okkum (kiḷavi:30; vē.ma:5; peya3; viṉi:29) eṉa muṉ oṉṟaṟkuk kūṟiyataṉik kūṟic celluvatum uṇṭu. iḵtu, `ituvumatu' eṉṟatu pōṉṟatu. cilaviṭaṅkaḷil virivāka eḻutuvatum uṇṭu (kiḷavi `1,19,23,31,33; viṉi :8,44), `aṉāṉ' eṉa uyartiṇip paṭarkki orumi viṉimuṟṟu īṟu otum īraṭi nūṟpāviṭattu,
	eṉ - eṉiṉ, uyartiṇit taṉmi viṉi uṇartti, attiṇip paṭarkki viṉi uṇarttuvāṉ eṭuttuk koṇṭār eṉpatu. appaṭarkki viṉitāṉ orumi viṉiyum paṉmi viṉiyum eṉa iru vakittu. avaṟṟuḷ orumi viṉitāṉ āṇpāl orumiyum peṇpāl orumiyum eṉa iru vakittu. avviru vaki orumiyum itaṉāṟ kūṟukiṉṟatu eṉa uṇarka (viṉi :8)
eṉa eḻutuvar. `eṭuttuk koṇṭār eṉpatu' eṉṟatōṭu niṟuttiyirukkalām. maṟṟati viḷakkavuriyil kūṟiyirukkalām. itu, paṭarkki orumi muṟṟu `uṇarttukiṉṟatu' eṉa nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum, `uyartiṇip paṭarkki viṉiyuḷ orumi viṉi uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu' eṉat teyvaccili yārum curukkamākak kūṟiyiruttali īṇṭu oppunōkkik kāṇutal nalam. iḷampūraṇar, `uyartiṇit taṉmic col uṇartti, attiṇip paṭarkki viṉic col uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu' eṉpar. `mikkataṉ maruṅkiṉ' (viṉi:44) eṉavarum nūṟpāviṭattuk karutturi viḷakkavuri pōla amin tirukkiṟatu. ivviṭattu iḷam. nacci. teyvac. ākiyōr oru variyil karutturi eḻutukiṉṟaṉar. māṇākkarkku naṉku viḷakkum mukattāṉ kallāṭaṉār iṅṅaṉam virittuk kūṟukiṟār.
atikārattiṉ mutal iyalit toṭaṅkumpōtu ‘mutalōttu’ eṉṟu kuṟippiṭuvatip pōṉṟu, iyaliṉ mutal nūṟpāvi, ‘itaṉ mutaṟ cūttiram eṉṉutaliṟṟō veṉiṉ’, ‘ittalic cuttiram eṉṉutaliṟṟō veṉiṉ’ eṉṟu kallāṭaṉār viṉāvi nūṟpā uriyi eḻutat toṭaṅkukiṟār. iṅṅaṉam ellā iyalkaḷilum, viṉāvukiṟārā eṉil, illi. mutaṟcūttiram eṉa kiḷaviyākkam, viḷimarapu, peyariyal ākiya mūṉṟu iyalkaḷilum, taliccūttiram eṉa vēṟṟumiyiyal, viṉiyiyal ākiya iraṇṭu iyalkaḷilum kuṟippiṭuvar.  vēṟṟumi mayaṅkiyal, iṭiyiyal ākiya iraṇṭu iyal kaḷilum mutal cūttiram eṉṟō, talic cūttiram eṉṟō eṉṟu etuvum kuṟippiṭātu, ‘ic cūttiram eṉṉutaliṟṟō veṉiṉ’ eṉṟu  uri eḻutukiṉṟār. iyaliṉ mutal nūṟpāvit ‘taliccūttiram' eṉṟu iḷampūraṇar, pērāciriyar, nacciṉārkkiṉiyar, teyvacciliyār ākiyōrum kuṟippiṭṭu uri eḻutuvar.
kallāṭaṉār, iyaliṉ mutal nūṟpāvi aṭuttu varum nūṟpākkaḷil ellām `eṉ - eṉiṉ' eṉṟu toṭaṅki uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu, kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu, kāttal nutaliṟṟu, vakuttal nutaliṟṟu, virittali nutaliṟṟu, vilakkutal nutaliṟṟu. ārāytal nutaliṟṟu, kūṟukiṉṟatu eṉak kūṟik karutturiyiṉi muṭikkiṉṟār. `eṉ - eṉiṉ' eṉpatu eṉṉutaliṟṟō veṉiṉ eṉṟu iḷampūraṇar toṭaṅki vitta coṟṟoṭariṉ curukkam. `ic cūttiram eṉṉutaliṟṟō veṉiṉ' eṉṟu toṭaṅki vitta iḷampūraṇar cila iṭaṅkaḷil iṅṅaṉam kūṟāmal celluvatu uṇṭu. ivarip piṉpaṟṟi eḻutum ivvuriyāciriyar ellā iṭaṅkaḷilum iṅṅaṉam toṭaṅki eḻutic ceṉṟiruppatu kuṟippiṭattakkatu.
cēṉāvariyarum paḻiyavurikārarum karutturi eḻutuvatu illi (cila iṭaṅkaḷ nīṅkalāka). iḷampūraṇar iṭiyiyalilum uriyiyalilum perumpāṉmiyāṉa iṭaṅkaḷil karutturi eḻutuvatu illi. teyvacciliyār, ‘ikkaruttu varukiṉṟa cūttiraṅkaṭkum okkum' eṉṟu uriyiyalil coṟporuḷ uṇarttum toṭakka nūṟpāviṟkuk (3) kūṟiyavar, ēṉiya 92āvatu nūṟpā vari karutturi vaḻaṅkuvatu illi. kallāṭaṉār, ellā nūṟpākkaḷukkum karutturi eḻutiyirukkiṟār. ēṉiyōr urikaḷil amintiruppatip pōṉṟu ivar uriyilum urikkuṟiyīṭu karutturiyāka amivatu uṇṭu. e.ṭu: eṉ - eṉiṉ, eytiyatu vilakkip piṟituviti vakuttal nutaliṟṟu (viḷi:24) eṉavarum. ‘eṉ - eṉiṉ iḵtu aruttāpatti mēṟṟu’ (kiḷavi:61) eṉa uttiyum karutturiyāka amivatu uṇṭu. cila iṭaṅkaḷil piṟarip pōṉṟu ivarum kūṟiyatiyum kūṟap pōvatiyum tokutturikkiṉṟār. kaṟpōrkku muṟpaṭak kiḷantati niṉivupaṭuttuvataṟkākavum, piṟpaṭak kiḷakkap pōvati muṉ kūṭṭiyē terivippataṟkākavum iṅṅaṉam uriyāciriyarkaḷ eḻutukiṉṟaṉar.


poruḷuri
kallāṭaṉār, (i - ḷ) eṉṟu kuṟiyiṭṭup poruḷuri eḻutukiṉṟār. paḻiyavurikārar tavira, iḷampūraṇar mutaṟkoṇṭu aṉivarumē ‘itaṉ poruḷ' eṉṟu poruḷuriyiṉi eḻutukiṉṟaṉar. eḻuttu, poruḷ atikāraṅkaḷil ‘itaṉ poruḷ’ eṉṟu poruḷuri vaḻaṅkiya iḷampūraṇar, collatikārattil maṭṭum ‘uri' eṉṟu kuṟippiṭṭup poruḷuri tarukiṉṟār. ēṉiya uriyāciriyarip pōṉṟu kallāṭaṉārum ‘itaṉ poruḷ' eṉṟu toṭaṅki ‘eṉṟavāṟu' (e - ṟu) eṉṟu poruḷ uriyiṉi muṭikkiṉṟār. nūṟpāvil kāṇappaṭum vakippāṭu ovvoṉṟiṟkum `eṉpatu', `āvatu' eṉṉum tuṇiccoṟkaḷik koṇṭu poruḷuri vaḻaṅkuvatu uṇṭu. teyvacciliyār, iraṇṭu coṟkaḷiyum tuṇic collākak koḷvar. iḷampūraṇarum, pērāciriyarum ‘eṉpatu' eṉṉum colliyum, nacciṉārkkiṉiyar ‘āvatu' eṉṉum colliyum tuṇic collākak koṇṭu poruḷ vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉar. ivviruvarum iṅṅaṉam mēṟkoḷvatu poruḷatikārattil eṉpati aṟital vēṇṭum.
kallāṭaṉār, cila iṭaṅkaḷil `eṉpatu', ‘eṉṉum', ‘eṉṟatu' (viṉi:16,30) ākiya tuṇiccoṟkaḷik koṇṭu uri eḻutuvatu uṇṭu. e.ṭu: kuṭimi eṉpatu, āṇmi eṉpatu (kiḷavi : 7); kālam eṉṉum collum ulakam eṉṉum collum; kālam eṉṟatu, ulakam eṉṟatu (kiḷavi:58). iḷampūraṇaratu eḻuttatikāra uri patavuriyākavum collatikāra uri poḻippuri yākavum poruḷatikāra uri patavuri, poḻippuriyākavum amintuḷḷatu. pērāciriyar poruḷuri patavuri, poḻippuriyākavum; cēṉāvariyar poruḷuri poḻippuriyākavum; nacciṉārkkiṉiyar eḻuttu, col, poruḷ ākiya muṉṟu urikaḷumē patavuriyākavum; teyvacciliyār, kallāṭaṉār, paḻiyavurikārar uri poḻippuri yākavum uḷḷaṉa. collatikāra uriyāciriyar aṟuvaruḷ nacciṉārkkiṉiyar tavira ēṉiyōr aivar urikaḷumē poḻippuriyāka amintavi eṉpatu kuṟippiṭattakkatu. kallāṭaṉār, cila iṭaṅkaḷil poruḷuriyiṉṟik (vē.ma: 14) karutturi, eṭuttukkāṭṭu viḷakkavuri eṉum niliyil uri eḻutic celvatum uṇṭu. iḷampūraṇar, iṭiyiyalilum  uriyiyalilum perumpāṉmiyāṉa iṭaṅkaḷil poruḷuri eḻutuvatu illi. atāvatu eṭuttukkāṭṭiṉit toṭakka mākak kūṟum iṭaṅkaḷil ellām poruḷuri illi.

poruḷuri viḷakkavuriyāka amital
‘mikkataṉ maruṅkiṉ viṉiccoṟ cuṭṭi, appaṇpu kuṟitta viṉimutaṟ kiḷavi, ceyva tilvaḻi nikaḻum kālattu meypeṟat tōṉṟum poruṭṭā kummē' (viṉi:44) eṉpatu nūṟpā.
	ulakattu oruvaḻi naṉmiyāṉum oruvaḻit tīmiyāṉum tavañceytal, tāykkoli eṉpaṉa mikka toḻiliṭattu varum. tavañceyvāṉ, tāyik kolvāṉ eṉṉum viṉippeyarc collāṉ oruvaṉ taṉṉi vēṟu kūṟutalik kuṟitta viṉip peyarkku muṭipāka ammikka viṉippayaṉākiya cuvarkkam pukutalum, nirayam pukutalum eṉṉum paṇpiṉi mēlvarum cuvarkkam pukuvaṉ, nirayam pukuvaṉ eṉṉum coṟkaḷāṉ taṉṉic collu taliyum kuṟitta avviṉi mutalākiya poruḷtāṉ mika attoḻiliṉic ceyyātu irunta nilimik kaṇṇē attoḻilic ceytataṉ payaṉi uṟukiṉṟāṉik kaṇṭāṉ pōla oruvaṉ tavañceytāṉ cuvarkkam pukum, ta hyik koṉṟāṉ nirayam pukum eṉac colla nikaḻkālattiṉ kaṇṇē uṇmi peṟat tōṉṟum poruṇmiyiṉi uṭittām eṉṟavāṟu
eṉpatu apporuḷuri. itil, tavañceyvāṉ cuvarkkam pukuvaṉ, tāyik kolvāṉ nirayam pukuvaṉ; tavañceytāṉ cuvarkkam pukum, tāyik koṉṟāṉ nirayam pukum eṉa eṭuttukkāṭṭuṭaṉ kūṭiya viḷakkam uḷatu. poruḷuri eṉpatu patavuriyāyiṉum poḻippuriyāyiṉum nūṟpāt toṭarukku uriyatāka maṭṭumē amiyum. iḵtu, eṭuttuk kāṭṭu, viḷakkam ākiyavaṟṟōṭu cērnta poruḷuriyāka amintuḷatu.
eṭuttukkāṭṭu
itu, karutturi poruḷuri aṭuttu iṭampeṟukiṟatu. perumpāṉmiyāṉa iṭaṅkaḷil immuṟiyum, cila iṭaṅkaḷil immuṟi māṟiyum amivatu uṇṭu. atāvatu viḷakkavuri aṭuttum eṭuttukkāṭṭu amikiṟatu. iḷampūraṇar, iṭiyiyalilum, uriyiyalilum perumpāṉmi yāṉa iṭaṅkaḷil eṭuttukkāṭṭiṉi mutalil tantu celluvar; eṭuttukkāṭṭu maṭṭumē tantu celvatum uṇṭu. nacciṉārkkiṉiyar ivviṭaṅkaḷil ellām karutturi, poruḷuri, eṭuttukkāṭṭu eṉum uriyamippil kūṟic celluvar. nūṟpā nuvalum ilakkaṇattik kaṟpōr eḷimiyāka aṟintu koḷvataṟku eṭuttukkāṭṭu iṉṟiyamiyātatākiṟatu. ēṉiya uriyāciriyarip pōṉṟu ivarum ‘eṉa varum' eṉa eṭuttukkāṭṭiṉi muṭikkiṉṟār. cila iṭaṅkaḷil ‘eṉavum varum', ‘eṉak koḷka', ‘aṟintu koḷka' eṉṟum kūṟuvatu uṇṭu. karutturiyi ‘nutaliṟṟu' eṉṟum, poruḷuriyi `eṉṟavāṟu' eṉṟum, kūṟi muṭittatip pōṉṟu, eṭuttuk kāṭṭiṉiyum iṅṅaṉam muṭittu uriyeḻutukiṉṟaṉar uriyāciriyarkaḷ.
nūṟpākkaḷil kāṇappaṭum vakippāṭu ovvoṉṟiyum eṭutturittut taṉittaṉiyāka eṭuttukkāṭṭut taruvatu uṇṭu (vē:18,19). kiḷaviyākkattu 11āvatu nūṟpā uriyil, nūlāciriyar tokuttuk kāṭṭāta eḻuvaki vaḻukkaḷiyum tokutturittu, ovvoṉṟiṟkum taṉittaṉiyāka eṭuttuk kāṭṭuṭaṉ kūṭiya viḷakkavuriyum tantirukkiṟār kallāṭaṉār eṉpatu kuṟippiṭattakkatu. ivviṭattu ivar eṭṭu muṟi eṭuttukkāṭṭiṉit tantirukkiṟār. iḷampūraṇarum, nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum ivaṟṟi `eḻuvaki mayakkam’ eṉṟurittu eṭuttukkāṭṭuṭaṉ viḷakkukiṉṟaṉar. eṭuttukkāṭṭiṉi utāraṇam allatu, eṭuttukkāṭṭu eṉṟu kallāṭaṉārum piṟarum kuṟippiṭa, iḷampūraṇar `varalāṟu' eṉṟu kuṟippiṭuvar. cilaviṭaṅkaḷil varumāṟu, atu varumāṟu, avi varumāṟu eṉavum kuṟippiṭuvar.
poruḷuriyil eṭuttukkāṭṭut taral
eṭuttukkāṭṭu eṉpatu taṉittē kūṟappaṭuvatu. vakippāṭṭu nūṟpāvāyiṉ ovvoru vakippāṭṭiyum eṭutturittu eṭuttukkāṭṭut taruvatu uṇṭu. kallāṭaṉār, īṇṭup poruḷuriyilē eṭuttukkāṭṭut tantiruppatu kuṟippiṭattakkatu. peyariyalil uyartiṇip peyarkaḷeṉa nilappeyar mutalāka iṉṟivar eṉṉum eṇṇiyaṟpeyar īṟākap patiṉoṉṟaṉit tokuttu ōtuvar āciriyar (11). itaṟku,
	aruvāḷaṉ cōḻiyaṉ eṉṟāṟ pōla oruvaṉ tāṉ piṟanta nilattiṉāṟ peṟṟa peyarkaḷum, maliyamāṉ cēramāṉ, pārppāṉ aracaṉ eṉṟāṟ pōla avaṉ tāṉ piṟanta kuṭiyāṟ peṟṟa peyarkaḷum, -------- oruvar iruvar mūvar nālvar eṉṟāṟ pōla ittuṇiyar eṉat tamatu variyaṟi uṇara niṟkum eṇṇiyalpiṉāṟ peṟṟa peyarkaḷum mēṟkūṟiya ṭayarkaḷ pōlap pālaṟiya vanta uyartiṇip peyarām eṉṟavāṟu
eṉap poruḷuri eḻutukiṟār. peya:9,14āvatu nūṟpāk kaḷilum immuṟiyiṉik kāṇalām. ēṉiyōr ivviṭaṅ kaḷil eṭuttukkāṭṭukaḷit taṉittē kūṟiyuḷḷaṉar. teyvacciliyārum cila iṭaṅkaḷil poruḷuriyilē eṭuttukkāṭṭiṉit tantu celvatu uṇṭu (vē:4,12,18). cēṉāvariyar, itaṉ poruḷ eṉak  kuṟiyīṭu tantuviṭṭu, atil eṭuttukkāṭṭiṉit tantu celluvar. ivar, `urukeḻu kaṭavuḷ' eṉavum, `puriya maṉṟa puriyōr kēṇmi’ (naṟ:1) eṉavum urupum puriyum uṭkum uyarpum uṇarttum eṉṟavāṟu' (uri:4) eṉa, uruvuṭ kākum puriyuyar pākum' eṉṉum nūṟpāviṟkup poruḷuri eḻutuvar. itupōṉṟa muṟiyiṉi uriyiyalil 87 iṭaṅkaḷil kāṇalām. ivaṟṟāṉ, eṭuttukkāṭṭuṭaṉ kūṭiya immuṟiyilum poruḷuri eḻutum nili iruntuḷatu eṉpatu aṟiyappaṭukiṟatu. āyiṉ, uri muṉṉavar iḷampūraṇar ivviṭaṅkaḷil ellām `varalāṟu' eṉṟu kuṟippiṭṭu eṭuttukkāṭṭiṉit tantu cellum muṟiyiṉi mēṟkoṇṭiruppatu aṟiyattakkatu.
poruttik kāṭṭutal
1. aintām vēṟṟumi, itaṉiṉ iṟṟitu eṉpatu. atāvatu, `poruḷiṉ ittaṉmittu ipporuḷ eṉap porūup poruḷil varum eṉavum, atu vaṇṇa m, vaṭivu, aḷavu, cuvi eṉa irupatteṭṭup poruṇmikaḷil varum eṉavum kūṟuvar āciriyar. ivaṟṟuḷ mutaṟkaṇ niṉṟa vaṇṇattiṟku,
	e.ṭu: kākkiyiṟ karitu kaḷampaḻam eṉṟāṟ pōlvaṉa. itaṉiṉ eṉpatu kākkiyiṉ eṉpatu. iṟṟu eṉpatu karitu eṉpatu. itu eṉpatu kaḷam paḻam eṉpatu. eṉṟatu kākkiyiṉuṅ kariyatu kaḷam paḻam eṉṟu mikuttuk kūṟiyavāṟāyiṟṟu (vē:16)
eṉṟu poruttik kāṭṭukiṟār kallāṭaṉār.
2. vēṟṟumi mayaṅkiyalil, urupu pala toṭarntu aṭukki varum ilakkaṇatti āciriyar kūṟuvar. itaṟku,
	e.ṭu: yāṉiyatu kōṭṭi nuṉikkaṇ kuṟittāṉ. yāṉiyatu eṉpatu kōṭu eṉpataṉōṭu taṟkiḻamip paṭṭu āṇṭē muṭintatu. nuṉikkaṇ eṉṉum ēḻāvatu kuṟittāṉ eṉpataṉōṭu muṭintatu. iṭikkaṇ niṉṟa kōṭu eṉṉum iraṇṭāvatu muṭipu iṉṟi niṉṟatu. avvāṟu taṉakkuriyatōr muṭipiṉṟē eṉiṉum ēḻāvataṉ muṭipōṭu muṭintatākak koḷḷappaṭum (nū:19)
eṉṟu poruttik kāṭṭukiṟār.
3. ku, ai, āṉ eṉum mūṉṟu urupukaḷum ceyyuḷuḷ akaramākat tirintu varum. aḵṟiṇikkaṇ āṉ eṉṉum ōrupu maṭṭumē tiriyum; ēṉiya kuvvum, aiyum tiriyā eṉpar āciriyar (vē.m:25, 26). ivaṟṟiṟku,
	e.ṭu: `kaṭinili yiṉṟē yāciriyarkka' (pu.ma:94) āciriyarkku eṉaṟpālatu āciriyarkka eṉṟāyiṟṟu. '`hvalōṉak kaḷiṟañcummē' kāvalōṉi eṉaṟ pālatu kāvalōṉa eṉṟāyiṟṟu. pulavarāṉ eṉpatu pulavarāṉa eṉṟāyiṟṟu. ivi uyartiṇik kaṇ tirintavāṟu. puḷḷiṉāṉ eṉpatu puḷḷiṉāṉa eṉṟāyiṟṟu. iḵtu aḵṟiṇikkaṇ tirintavāṟu
eṉṟu eṭuttukkāṭṭukaḷip poruttik kāṭṭukiṟār. `varinili yiṉṟē yāciriyarkka ' (pu.ma:20) eṉpatum kukara urupiṟku eṭuttukkāṭṭāka amikiṟatu.
4. iraṇṭām vēṟṟumi urupum mūṉṟām vēṟṟumi urupum mayaṅkum taṭumāṟu toḻiṟpeyar paṟṟi āciriyar ilakkaṇam ōtuvar. itaṟku,
	e.ṭu: puli kol yāṉi eṉpatu, orukāl puliyik koṉṟa yāṉi eṉavum, orukāl puliyāṟ kollappaṭṭa yāṉi eṉavum kollutal toḻil iraṇṭiṟkum ceṉṟu varutalil taṭumāṟu toḻiṟ peyar eṉṉappaṭṭatu (vē.ma:12)
eṉa eṭuttukkāṭṭiṉit tantu viḷakkukiṟār.
5. vēṟṟumi mayaṅkiyalil, `viṉiyē ceyvatu ceyappaṭuporuḷē' eṉa viṉiccoṟku ilakkaṇam uṇarttuvar āciriyar. itaṟkut tarum e.ṭu: vaṉintāṉ eṉpatu. itaṉuḷ,
	vaṉintāṉ eṉa vaṉital toḻiṉmi viḷaṅkiṟṟu. vaṉintāṉ eṉa oruvaṉum vaṉiyappaṭṭa kuṭam mutalāya ceyappaṭu poruḷum viḷaṅkiṉa. vaṉintōriṭam akamāṉum puṟamāṉum viḷaṅkiṟṟu. kōlum tikiriyum mutalākiya karuviyum viḷaṅkiṟṟu. vaṉivittuk koṇṭāṉum viḷaṅkiṟṟu. vaṉintāṉ peṟṟatoru payaṉum marapāṉum poruḷāṉum viḷaṅkiṟṟu vē.ma:29)
eṉa āciriyar otiya eṭṭaṉiyum poruttik kāṭṭiyiruppatu ciṟappiṟkuriyatu. iṉṉum viṉi:23 mutalāṉa nūṟpākkaḷil kāṇalām.

viḷakkavuri
ēṉiya uriyāciriyarip pōṉṟu ivarum nūṟpāviṉ poruḷi eṭuttukkāṭṭu, mēṟkōḷ, uvami, utti, ilakkaṇam, ilēcu, māṭṭēṟu mutalāṉavaṟṟik koṇṭu viḷakkukiṉṟār. viḷakkappaṭum nūṟpāviṟku muṉpiṉ amintuḷḷa nūṟpā, muṉpiṉ iyalil amintuḷḷa nūṟpā, muṉpiṉ atikāraṅkaḷil amintuḷḷa nūṟpā eṉat toṭarpuṭiya nūṟpākkaḷik koṇṭu uriyāciriyarkaḷ viḷakkic celluvar. atu, kaṟpōr nūl muḻumiyum aṟintu koḷḷa mikavum payaṉuṭiyatāka amikiṟatu. viḷakkavuriyil, nūṟpā vippu, nūṟpā vakimuṟi vippu, nūṟpā iṉṟiyami yāmi  ākiyavaṟṟiṟ kāṉa viḷakkamum, nūṟpāvil kūṟappaṭum vakippāṭu oṉṟiṉuḷ oṉṟu `aṭaṅkātō eṉiṉ, aṭaṅkātu’ eṉpataṟkāṉa viḷakkamum kūṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. nūṟpāvil kūṟappaṭāta ilakkaṇaṅkaḷum collappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. curuṅkac coṉṉāl uriyāciriyartam pulamiyiṉi veḷippaṭuttum vakiyil viḷakkavuri amintirukkiṟatu. atē nērattil, nūṟpā nuvalum poruḷiṉi muḻumiyāka aṟintu koḷḷavum tuṇi purikiṟatu. ivvuriyāl, nūliṉ ciṟappum, nūliṉ ciṟappāl tamiḻiṉ ciṟappum veḷippaṭukiṟatu. vaḻakku, ceyyuḷ mēṟkōḷkaḷi maṭṭumallātu tolkāppiya nūṟpākkaḷiyum mēṟkōḷākak kāṭṭi viḷakkic ceṉṟiruppatu ciṟappiṟkuriya oṉṟu.
tolkāppiya mēṟkōḷ
1. takutiyum vaḻakkum taḻīiyiṉa oḻukum, pakutik kiḷavi varinili yilavē' (kiḷavi:17) eṉṉum nūṟpā uriyil,
	takuti eṉṟatu poruḷ paṟṟi amintatu. vaḻakkāṟu vaḻaṅkutal paṟṟi amintatu. yāṉi, yāṟu, yāṭu eṉṉum iṉṉōraṉṉavaṟṟi āṉi, āṟu, āṭu eṉṉum iṉṉōraṉṉavākak kūṟutalum vaḻakkāṟu eṉa itaṉuṭ koḷḷappaṭum. piṟavumaṉṉa. avi cāṉṟōr coṉṉa ceyyuḷuṭ perumpāṉmi kāṇappaṭāmiyiṉ, itaṉuṭ koḷḷātu, `kaṭicol lilli kālattup paṭiṉē' (ec:56) eṉpuḻik koḷḷappaṭum
eṉṟu eccaviyal nūṟpāvik koṇṭu viḷakkukiṟār. `irīr miṉṉeṉa' (viṉi:26) eṉavarum nūṟpāvilum, `uṇkiṭu uṇkiṭā eṉpaṉavō eṉiṉ’ avi cāṉṟōr ceyyuṭkaṇ iṉmiyiṟ, `kaṭi col illi kālattup paṭiṉē' eṉpataṉāṟ koḷḷappaṭum' eṉṟu innūṟpāvik koḷvar.
2. kūṟiya muṟiyiṉ urupunili tiriyātu, īṟupeyark kākum iyaṟkiya eṉpa (vē:8) eṉpatu nūṟpā. ai mutalākiya vēṟṟumi urupukaḷ āṟum, tattam nilimiyil tiriyātu peyarkku īṟāy varum iyalpiṉi uṭiya eṉṟu colluvar eṉpatu poruḷ. itaṉ viḷakkavuriyil nili tiriyātu' eṉpataṟku,
	mēl, kuai āṉeṉa varūum iṟuti' (vē.ma:25) eṉṟu ceyyuṭkaṇ urupu īṟu tiripu kūṟukiṉṟār ātaliṉ, aḵtoḻiya vaḻakkiṉuḷ tiripilli eṉṟaṟku nili tiriyātu eṉappaṭṭatu eṉka
eṉṟu viḷakkam tarukiṟār. vēṟṟumi urupukaḷukkut tiripu vēṟṟumi mayaṅkiyalil ōtukiṟār āciriyar. ku, ai, āṉ ākiya mūṉṟum ceyyuṭkaṇ akaramākat tirintu varutalum uṇṭu; ammūṉṟaṉuḷ āṉ urupu maṭṭumē aḵṟiṇikkaṇ akaramākat tirintu varum; ēṉiya tiriyā ākiyaṉa avviyaṟkaṇ ōtappaṭum ilakkaṇam. ati īṇṭu eṭutturittu, aḵtoḻiya vaḻakkiṉuḷ tiripilli eṉṟaṟku nili tiriyātu eṉṟār eṉṟu teḷivupaṭuttiyiruppatu ciṟappiṟkuriya oṉṟu.
3. taṭumāṟu toḻiṟpeyarkku, puli kol yāṉi eṉpatu, orukāl puliyik koṉṟa yāṉi eṉavum orukāl puliyāṟ kollappaṭṭa yāṉi eṉavum kollutal toḻil iraṇṭiṟkum ceṉṟu varutaliṉ taṭumāṟu toḻiṟ peyar eṉṉappaṭṭatu (vē.ma:12) eṉṟu eṭuttukkāṭṭiṉip poruttik kāṭṭiyavar,
	puliyāl eṉpuḻi viṉi yātō eṉiṉ, āṇṭum viṉi koṉṟa eṉpatu. aḵtēl puli karuviyāka yāṉitāṉ piṟitoṉṟaṉik koṉṟatu eṉpatu poruḷāmāṟu eṉ eṉiṉ, `ceyappaṭu poruḷic ceytatu pōla' (viṉi:48) eṉpataṉāl kollap paṭṭa eṉpatu poruḷāka nōkka avvāṟākum eṉpatu
eṉṟu viṉiyiyal nūṟpāvik koṇṭu viḷakkukiṟār.
4. nīyir nīyeṉa varum kiḷavi, pālteri pilavē uṭaṉmoḻip poruḷē (peya:34) eṉpatu nūṟpā. nīyir, nī eṉpaṉa viravup peyarkaḷ. ivi uyartiṇi, aḵṟiṇi iraṇṭaṉ poruṇmikkum iṭamāka viḷaṅkum. ātaliṉ ettiṇi eṉpatu teriyātu. īṇṭup `pāl' eṉpatu tiṇiyik kuṟikkum. itaṉi,
	tiṇi teriyāmiyiṉ aṉṟē viravup peyarāyatu. itu colla vēṇṭumō eṉiṉ, mēl viravup peyarkaḷit `tattam viṉiyō ṭallatu pāl teripila' (peya:18) eṉṟamiyiṉ, iviyum tattam marapiṉ tiṇiyāṉ uṇaraṟpāṭu ceṉṟami kaṇṭu ivi muṉṉilip peyarākaliṉ ivaṟṟukku varum muṉṉili viṉiyum viravākaliṉ eytiyatu vilakkutaṟkuk kūṟiṉār. e.ṭu: nīyir vantīr, nī vantāy eṉavarum
eṉa peyariyal nūṟpāvi eṭutturittu viḷakkukiṟār. muṉṉili viṉi viravuviṉi eṭṭaṉuḷ oṉṟu. īr eṉpatu muṉṉilip paṉmi īṟu. āy eṉpatu muṉṉili orumi īṟu. iṉṉum viṉi:14,28,50; iṭi:2 ākiya iṭaṅkaḷilum tolkāppiya nūṟpākkaḷi mēṟkoṇṭu viḷakkuvar.
itaṉāṟ colliyatu
nūṟpā uriyamippiṉ vakippāṭu nāṉkē. iruppiṉum, itaṉōṭu aintākiṟatu. itu perumpālum uriyiṉ muṭivil iṭampeṟum. orōviṭattup poruḷuri aṭuttum (kal. vē.ma:18), viḷakkavuri iṭiyilum (kal. kiḷavi:4,51), eṭuttukkāṭṭu aṭuttum (kiḷavi:46,56; viṉi:44) amivatu uṇṭu. ivvurineṟiyiṉi iḷampūraṇar, teyvacciliyār, kallāṭaṉār ākiyōr mēṟkoṇ ṭirukkiṉṟaṉar. ivarkaḷil kallāṭaṉārē mikutiyāṉa iṭaṅkaḷil payaṉpaṭuttukiṟār eṉpatu aṟiyattakkatu. kiḷaviyākkattil muppattu nāṉku iṭaṅkaḷilum (1-4, 16-18, 22, 25-27, 30, 32,35,37-39, 41-49, 51,52, 55, 56, 58-60,62), vēṟṟumi mayaṅkiyalil aintu iṭaṅkaḷilum (11,18,21,32,33), viṉiyiyalil āṟu iṭaṅkaḷilumāka (16,37,38,44,45,48) nāṟpattu aintu iṭaṅkaḷil kūṟukiṟār eṉpatu kuṟippiṭattakkatu. āyiṉ, vēṟṟumiyiyal, viḷimarapu, peyariyal, iṭiyiyalkaḷil iṭi. nū:10 vari) oru nūṟpāvil kūṭa iṅṅaṉam kuṟippiṭṭu uri eḻutavilli eṉpatum aṟiyattakkatu.
`ic cūttiraṅkaḷāṟ kūṟiyatu' (kiḷavi:55) eṉṟu oṉṟaṟku mēṟpaṭṭa nūṟpākkaḷukkuc cērttuk kūṟuvatum uṇṭu. kiḷaviyākkattu 32āvatu nūṟpāvil, `ic cūttirattāṟ kūṟiyatu' eṉṟum, `ivaṟṟāṟ colliyatu' eṉṟum irumuṟi kūṟi muṭikkiṟār. muṉṉatil kūṟiyataṟkut teḷivu kāṭṭavēṇṭip piṉṉatik kūṟiyirukkiṟār. iḷampūraṇar, inneṟiyiṉic collatikārattil mēṟkoḷḷavilli. poruḷatikārattil mēṟkoḷkiṟār.
itaṉi, ic cūttirattāṟ colliya poruḷ, ic cūttirattāṟ colliyatu, ic cūttirattāṟ kūṟiyatu, itaṉāṟ kūṟiyatu, itaṉāṟ colliyatu, ivaṟṟāṟ colliyatu, ivaṟṟāṉ, iṉṉum itaṉāṉē eṉak kuṟippiṭṭum, itaṉāṟ colliyatu eṉṉi eṉiṉ' (kiḷavi:25, 26) eṉa viṉaviyum, ic cūttiraṅkaḷāṟ kūṟiyatu' (kiḷavi:55) eṉa mūṉṟu nūṟpākkaḷukkuc cērttum kallāṭaṉār kūṟic cellukiṟār. ippakutiyil,
1.	toki variyaṟi kūṟutal
2.	viri variyaṟi kūṟutal
3.	nūṟpā uṇarttāta poruḷik kūṟutal
4.	aiyattit teḷivupaṭuttutal
5.	ciṟupāṉmi, perumpāṉmi paṟṟik kūṟutal
6.	nūṟpā iṉṟiyamiyāmi paṟṟik kūṟutal
7.	toṭarpuṭiya poruḷ kūṟutal
8.	āciriyar ōtiya ilakkaṇatti  eḷimippaṭuttic curuṅkak kūṟutal
9.	nūlāciriyar ōtiya ilakkaṇattit tokuttuk kūṟutal
eṉa oṉpatu vakiyāṉa poruḷkaḷ kūṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
toki variyaṟi
	ic cūttirattāṟ colliya poruḷ iv vatikārattuc collukiṟa collatu toki vari yaṟiyum ataṉi variyaṟukkuṅkāl poruḷāṉallatu variyaṟi yiṉmiyiṉ apporuḷatu toki variyaṟiyum poruṭku nūlakattu āṭci peṟṟa kuṟiyīṭum uṇarttiṉa vāṟāyiṟṟu (kiḷavi:1)
eṉṟu mutal nūṟpā uriyiṉ muṭivil kallāṭaṉār kūṟukiṟār. itil, uyartiṇi, aḵṟiṇi eṉum col variyaṟi; makkaḷ, avarala piṟa eṉṉum poruḷ variyaṟi ākiyaṉa collappaṭuvatāl, toki variyaṟi eṉpatāyiṟṟu. pala cūttiraṅkaḷāṟ kūṟiya poruḷi ittuṇi eṉa variyaṟi urittalum toki variyaṟiyākum. `irutiṇi maruṅkiṉ aimpā laṟiya', `paṉmiyum orumiyum pālaṟi vanta' (kiḷavi:10, viṉi:11), `vaṇṇan tāmē yaviyeṉa moḻipa (cey:224) eṉṉum nūṟpākkaḷ avvakiyic cārntavi. itu tokuttuk kūṟal eṉṉum uttiyiṉ pāṟpaṭum. toki variyaṟi maṭṭumaṉṟi, uyartiṇi, aḵṟiṇi eṉum kuṟiyīṭukaḷum īṇṭu uṇarttappaṭukiṉṟaṉa eṉkiṟār.
viri variyaṟi
	itaṉāṟ colliyatu, mēṟkūṟiya uyartiṇi eṉpatu, virivakiyāṉ āṇpāl, peṇpāl, paṉmippāl eṉa mūṉṟu kūṟupaṭum eṉpatūum, apporuḷ mukkūṟu paṭumeṉavē, tantiravutti vakiyāṉ mēṟ kūṟiya uyartiṇic col eṉpatu viri variyaṟi yāṉ āṇpāṟcol, peṇpāṟ col, paṉmip pāṟ colleṉa mūṉṟu kūṟupaṭum eṉpatūum kūṟiya vāṟāyiṟṟu (kiḷavi:2)
eṉṟu kūṟukiṟār. uyartiṇi eṉpatu mukkūṟukaḷi uṭiyatu. avi iviyeṉa virittu variyaṟi kūṟutalāṉ, itil viri variyaṟi kūṟiyatāyiṟṟu. aṭuttu varum nūṟpāvum itaṟkuriyatu. itu, vakuttu niṟuttal eṉṉum uttiyiṉ pāṟpaṭum.
aiyattit teḷivupaṭuttutal
`aṉṉa piṟavum toṉṉeṟi piḻiyātu, ----- tiripiṭa ṉilavē teriyu mōrkkē' (vē.ma:18) eṉpatu vēṟṟumi mayakkattiṟku otappaṭum puṟaṉaṭi nūṟpā. itaṟkuk karutturi, poruḷuri kūṟiya kallāṭaṉār,
	itaṉāṟ colliyatu, vēṟṟumi ōttiṉkaṇ iṉṉa poruṭku iṉṉatu uritteṉa eṭuttōtappaṭṭa vēṟṟumikaḷ avvap poruṭku uriyavāṟāy nillātu, piṟa poruṭkaṇṇum ceṉṟu mayaṅkutal uṇmi kaṇṭu ivi vaḻuvaṉṟō eṉṟu viṉāya māṇākkaṟku, avi mēṟṟoṭṭup piṟaporuṇ mēlum vaḻaṅkum muṭiviṉi ārāynta mutaṉūlāciriyarkaḷum vaḻaṅki varutalāṉ vēṟṟumi vaḻu eṉṟu puṟattiṭṭārallar. ataṉāṉē, yāṉum am muṭipē nērntēṉ eṉpatu kūṟiyavāṟu. ituvum marapu vaḻuvamiti
eṉṟu māṇākkaṟku eḻum aiyattit tāṉē viṉavi viḷakkiyiruppatu ciṟappiṟkuriyatu. `vaḻuvaṉṟu eṉṉum viṭikku innūlāciriyaric cāṉṟāka niṟuttātu, mutaṉūlāciriyaric cāṉṟāka niṟutti, avar vaḻiyē ivarum ceṉṟār eṉkiṟār. piṟa poruṭkaṇ ceṉṟu mayaṅkutal eṉpatu vēṟṟumi vaḻuvē. mutaṉūlāciriyar puṟattiṭṭā rallar eṉa urittu ivvuriyāciriyar amiti kāṇukiṟār.
coṟporuḷ viḷakkam
ivar, poḻippuri eḻutiyatōṭu, cilaviṭaṅkaḷil nūṟpāvil uḷḷa coṟkaḷukkup poruḷ, viḷakkam ākiyaṉa kūṟic celluvatu uṇṭu .
ciṉi (kiḷavi:16)	-	uṟuppu
mutal	-	avvuṟuppiṉi uṭiyatu
viṉinili (vē.ma:2)	-	iraṇṭāvataṟku ōtiya viṉi, 			viṉikkuṟippu eṉa uṇarka
iyaṟki (viṉi:47)	-	vaḻaṅkukāl tāṉ oṉṟaṉi iḵtu 			ippeṟṟiyatākum eṉṟu 				aṟintirunta iyalpu
teḷivu	-	oru nūl neṟiyāṉ itu nikaḻum 			eṉak kaṇṭu vittut tuṇital
toki, vakippāṭu kūṟal
vaḻukkāttal eṉpatu vaḻuvaṟka, vaḻuvamika eṉa iru vakippaṭum (kiḷavi:11). vaḻuvamiti, ilakkaṇam uḷvaḻik kūṟum vaḻuvamiti, ilakkaṇam ilvaḻik kūṟum vaḻuvamiti eṉa iruvakippaṭum (kiḷavi: 12). ceppu iraṇṭu vakippaṭum. avi maṟuttal, uṭaṉpaṭutal eṉpaṉa. viṉā aintu vakippaṭum. avi aṟiyāṉ viṉātal, aṟivu oppuk kāṇṭal, aiyamaṟuttal, avaṉ aṟivu tāṉ kōṭal, mey avaṟkuk kāṭṭal eṉpaṉa (kiḷavi:13). peyar eṉpatu peyarppeyar, toḻiṟpeyar eṉa iruvakippaṭum. toḻiṟpeyar eṉpatu toḻiliṉ mēl niṉṟa toḻiṟpeyar, poruḷ mēl niṉṟa toḻiṟpeyar eṉa iruvakippaṭum (vē:9). vaṇṇam eṉpatu aintu vakippaṭum. vaṭivu eṉpatu muppattiraṇṭu vakippaṭum. cuvi eṉpatu aṟuvakippaṭum (vē:17). taṟkiḻami aintu vakippaṭum. avi, oṉṟu pala kuḻīiyatu, vēṟu pala kuḻīiyatu, oṉṟiyaṟkiḻami, uṟuppiṉ kiḻami, mey tirintākiya taṟkiḻami eṉappaṭum (vē.m:18), eccam iṟantatu taḻīiyatum, etiratu taḻīiyatum eṉa iruvakittu iṭi:7), uyarvuc ciṟappum iḻivuc ciṟappum eṉac ciṟappu iruvakittu iṭi:7) eṉak kallāṭaṉār vakippāṭukaḷik kūṟik tokiyiṉik kūṟic celluvar. iviyēyaṉṟi iṉṉum pala uḷa.
piṟitiṉ kiḻami itu pōlap pakutippaṭātu eṉpar kallāṭaṉār. iḷampūraṇarum iṅṅaṉamē kūṟuvar. āyiṉ, cēṉāvariyar, nacciṉārkkiṉiyar, paḻiyavurikārar ākiyōr mūṉṟu vakippaṭum eṉpar. avi, poruḷiṉ kiḻami, nilattiṉ kiḻami, kālattiṉ kiḻami eṉappaṭum. ivvāṟu uriyāciriyarkaḷ tokiyiṉiyum ataṉ vakippāṭṭiṉiyum kūṟic colluvar. itukāṟum kūṟiyavi yāvum nūlāciriyar kūṟātu, uriyāciriyarkaḷ kūṟiyavi eṉṟu aṟital vēṇṭum.
vēṟupāṭu kūṟal
a. eḻuttu, col: taṉṉi uṇartti niṉṟavaḻi eḻuttu eṉappaṭum; tāṉ iṭi niṉṟu poruḷ uṇarttiya vaḻic col eṉappaṭum (kiḷavi:1).
ā. poruvu, uvamam: poruvu eṉpatu oṉṟi oṉṟaṉōṭu okkum eṉpataṉṟi ataṉiṉ itu naṉṟu eṉa mikuttuk kūṟuvatu. uvamam eṉpatu oppu uṇarttal eṉa uṇarka (kiḷavi:16).
i. eḻuvāy vēṟṟumi, viḷi vēṟṟumi: eḻuvāy vēṟṟumi , īṟu tiriyātu urupēṟkum. viḷi vēṟṟumi, īṟu tirintum, īṟu tiriyātum urupēṟkum. īṟu tiriyātavaḻi oruvaḻippaṭarkkiyākiyum, muṉṉiliyākiyum varum (vē:4). iv vēṟupāṭṭiṉi iḷampūraṇarum kūṟiyuḷḷār.

ī. viyaṅkoḷa varutal, viṉinili uṇarttal: ivi eḻuvāy vēṟṟumi ēṟkum payaṉilikaḷuḷ iṭampeṟukiṉṟaṉa. muṉṉatu piṉṉataṉuḷ aṭaṅkum. eṉiṉum vēṟupāṭu uṇṭu. viyaṅkoḷa varutal eṉpatu, taṉkaṇ toḻil nikaḻvatāka oruvaṉāl ēvappaṭṭu niṟkum nili. viṉinili uṇarttal eṉpatu, taṉkaṇ nikaḻntatōr viṉi (vē:5). e.ṭu: ā celka; ā kiṭantatu eṉavarum.
u. eccam, etirmaṟi: ivviraṇṭum `um' eṉum iṭiccol vakippāṭukaḷuḷ iṭampeṟuvaṉa. eccam eṉpatu piṟitōr poruḷiṉit taḻuvi varuvatu. aḵtu, iṟantatu taḻīiyatu etiratu taḻīiyatu eṉa iruvakippaṭum. e.ṭu: yāṉ karuvūrkkuc celvēṉ eṉṟārkku yāṉum avvūrkkup pōtuval eṉpatum, avvāṟu kūṟiṉārkku yāṉum uriyūrkkup pōtuvaṉ eṉpatum ākum. etirmaṟi eṉpatu, apporuṭṭāṉum oru kūṟṟit taḻuvi varuvatu (iṭi:7). e.ṭu: koṟṟaṉ varutaṟkum uriyaṉ, `aḵṟiṇi viravuppeya riyalpumā ruḷavē' (toki:13) eṉavarum.
- ca. kurucāmi
kallāṭaṉār urineṟi, pak. 85-108
